| `GET /bdm/manager/activities?date=&bdm_user_id=&channel=` | `bdm_manager`, `super_admin` | team / all | `bdm_user_id` is ANDed with scope: outside the team → empty page and zero counts |

**Status table (A4):** `401` no session; `403` wrong role, not the assigned BDM, not the logger (manager / `super_admin` writes included);
`404` activity or organization outside the caller's scope (incl. out-of-type organization); `409` not today's activity (day gate) and the
200-per-day cap; `422` schema, time rules, contact, archived organization on create, future `?date=`. `detail` is a string except schema
`422`s (FastAPI's list). **Retry semantics (A3):** `POST` is not retry-safe (no idempotency key; a duplicate is fixed by a same-day delete);
`PATCH` is idempotent (same body → same row, no second audit); `DELETE` answers `204`, a second `DELETE` answers `404`. **V9:**
`occurred_at` up to 5 minutes after the server clock is saved as the server's now; more than 5 minutes ahead → `422`; older than 7 IST
days → `422`. **V10:** the 201st activity of a BDM's IST day → `409`. Refusal logging: `bdm_activity_write_refused` is logged for "not the organization's assigned BDM" (create) and "not the BDM who logged it" (patch / delete, which also covers a manager or `super_admin` touching a readable team activity); 403s from the role gate (`bdm_context` on create, `caller_scope` for other roles) are not logged.

**`bdm-004` / `DEC-SCOPE-071` (built 2026-10-05; migration `0073_bdm_pipeline`, after bdm-007's `0072_bdm_meeting_reports`) — organization pipelines per BDM type + stage history.**
Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-05-bdm-004-organization-pipelines-design.md` §6. All routes are new; the only change to an
existing response is **additive**: every organization detail (`GET/POST/PATCH /bdm/organizations…`, archive / restore / assign / contact
writes) gains `pipeline {stage, stage_label, lost: {at, reason} | null, agent_status, steps[{key, label, kind, state}]}`. List rows and
the four permission keys are unchanged. Writes reuse `can_edit` (S1: the assigned BDM or `super_admin`; a manager reads only).

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /bdm/organizations/{id}/stage` `{from_stage, to_stage, note?}` | `bdm`, `super_admin` | `can_edit` | `200 {organization}`; a manual stage of the organization's own pipeline; backward needs `note` |
| `POST /bdm/organizations/{id}/lost` `{reason}` | `bdm`, `super_admin` | `can_edit` | `200 {organization}`; the stage is kept (S5) |
| `POST /bdm/organizations/{id}/revive` `{reason}` | `bdm`, `super_admin` | `can_edit` | `200 {organization}`; back at the stage it was lost at |
| `GET /bdm/organizations/{id}/stage-history?limit=&offset=` | any reader of the organization | `caller_scope` | `{items, total, limit, offset}` newest first; items `{id, kind, from_stage, from_label, to_stage, to_label, note, actor {id, full_name}, created_at}` |
| `GET /bdm/pipeline?bdm_type=&assigned=&stage=&limit=&offset=` | `bdm`, `bdm_manager`, `super_admin` | `caller_scope` | `{bdm_type, stages[{key, label, kind, count}], lost_count, items, total, limit, offset}`; `count` null for live / volume steps; archived excluded; `stage` = a step key or `lost` |

**Status table (spec §6.4):** `401` no session; `403` wrong role or not `can_edit` (logged); `404` organization outside the caller's
scope; `409` archived ("Restore this organization first"), `{code: "organization_lost"}`, `{code: "organization_not_lost"}`,
`{code: "stage_changed", current_stage}` (stale `from_stage`); `422` field errors with `loc` `["body", <field>]` (unknown / other-type
stage, live stage "set by the onboarding handover", volume step, same stage, backward without a note, blank reason). `GET /bdm/pipeline`:
`422` "Choose a BDM type" (manager / super_admin without `bdm_type`), "You can only view your own module's pipeline" (a BDM naming another
type), unknown `stage`, `assigned=me` from a non-BDM. **Retry semantics:** a repeated move answers `409 stage_changed` with
`current_stage` equal to the requested stage (the UI treats it as done); a repeated lost / revive answers `409`. No idempotency key.

**`bdm-005` / `DEC-SCOPE-080` (built 2026-10-06; migration `0079_bdm_mous`, after `0078_enquiry_lead_record`) — MoU tracking.**
Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-bdm-005-mou-tracking-design.md` §6. All routes are new; no existing request or response
changes (the organization detail does not embed the MoU). Writes reuse `can_edit` (M3: the assigned BDM or `super_admin`); reads and
downloads follow `caller_scope`. `status` everywhere is the **effective** status: a Signed / Active MoU with `valid_until` before today
(Asia/Kolkata) reads `expired`, which is never stored or accepted as input (M2, M7).

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /bdm/organizations/{id}/mou` | any reader of the organization | `caller_scope` | `200 {current: MouOut \| null, can_start}` |
| `POST /bdm/organizations/{id}/mou` `{status?, proposal_sent_on?, signed_on?, valid_from?, valid_until?, reference?, notes?}` | `bdm`, `super_admin` | `can_edit` | `201 {mou}`; a first MoU, or a renewal when the current one reads Expired / Rejected (the old row becomes `is_current=false`) |
| `PATCH /bdm/organizations/{id}/mou` `{status?, from_status?, expected_updated_at?, …fields}` | `bdm`, `super_admin` | `can_edit` | `200 {mou}`; omitted = unchanged, `null` = clear; `status` needs `from_status`; `expected_updated_at` (optional, QA5-01) = the MoU `updated_at` the form showed; Signed advances the pipeline when behind (D28) |
| `PUT /bdm/organizations/{id}/mou/document` multipart `file` | `bdm`, `super_admin` | `can_edit` | `200 {mou}`; PDF / JPEG / PNG by bytes, `max_upload_bytes`; 20 per user per hour |
| `GET /bdm/mous?status=&bdm_type=&organization=&current=&limit=&offset=` | `bdm`, `bdm_manager`, `super_admin` | `caller_scope` | `{items, total, limit, offset}`; rows `{id, organization {id, code, name, bdm_type}, assigned_bdm, status, status_label, status_changed_at, signed_on, valid_until, reference, has_document, is_current}`; `current` defaults to `true` |
| `GET /bdm/mous/{id}/history?limit=&offset=` | any reader of the organization | `caller_scope` | newest first; items `{id, kind, from_status, from_label, to_status, to_label, changed[], actor, created_at}` |
| `GET /bdm/mous/{id}/document` | any reader of the organization | `caller_scope` | the bytes, `attachment; filename="mou-<org code>.<ext>"`, `nosniff`, `no-store`, CSP sandbox; audited before any byte |

`MouOut` = the row + `proposal_sent_on`, `valid_from`, `notes`, `document {name, content_type, uploaded_at} | null` (never a key or a
URL), `expired_on`, `created_by`, `permissions {can_edit, can_upload, can_renew}`, `pipeline_on_sign {key, label} | null`,
`created_at`, `updated_at`.

**Status table (spec §6.4):** `401` no session; `403` wrong role or not `can_edit`; `404` organization / MoU outside the caller's scope
(same body as an unknown id), "No MoU yet", "No document on file"; `409` archived ("Restore this organization first"),
`{code: "organization_lost"}`, `{code: "mou_exists"}`, `{code: "mou_status_changed", current_status}` (stale `from_status`),
`{code: "mou_expired"}` (a status change on an Expired MoU), `{code: "mou_changed"}` (`expected_updated_at` older than the stored MoU; checked after the status conflict); `413` too large; `415` not a PDF / JPEG / PNG; `422` field errors with `loc`
`["body", <field>]` (Signed / Active without `signed_on`, Active without the window, `valid_until` before `valid_from`, same status,
`expired` sent as a status, `status` without `from_status`, malformed dates) or an unknown `status` filter; `429` + `Retry-After` upload
limit. **Retry semantics:** a retried create answers `409 mou_exists`; a retried status change answers `409 mou_status_changed`. No
idempotency key.

**`bdm-018` / `DEC-SCOPE-085` (built 2026-10-06; migration `0084_bdm_onboarding`, after `0083_tel_content`) — school onboarding handover.**
Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-bdm-018-school-onboarding-handover-design.md` §5. Three things change additively:
`POST /overseas-admin/schools` accepts an optional `bdm_onboarding_request_id` (absent = unchanged), `SchoolOut` gains
`linked_bdm {full_name, active, organization_code} | null` (derived from the linked organization's assignee, H1; returned only on the
overseas-admin School routes), and the BDM organization detail gains `onboarding` (null unless `bdm_type = 'school'`). The School
pipeline's live steps (9–14) now take `done` / `current` / `upcoming` from the linked School's evidence (spec §4); `stage` stays the stored
manual stage. Unchanged: `PATCH /overseas-admin/schools/{id}` (no `bdm_user_id`), the stage-move route, the pipeline view, `/school/*`,
and ENH-029's bulk template.

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /bdm/organizations/{id}/onboarding-request` `{note?}` | `bdm`, `super_admin` | `can_edit` | `201 {organization}`; a School organization whose current MoU reads Signed or Active, not Lost, not linked, none pending; in-app notice to every active `overseas_admin` |
| `GET /overseas-admin/bdm-onboarding-requests?status=pending\|completed\|rejected&limit=&offset=` | `overseas_admin`, `super_admin` | all | `{items, total, limit, offset}`; pending oldest first, else newest resolved first; items carry the organization prefill, primary contact, MoU reference / signed date, requester, assigned BDM, School |
| `POST /overseas-admin/bdm-onboarding-requests/{id}/reject` `{reason}` | `overseas_admin`, `super_admin` | all | `200` item; the BDM is told the reason (in-app); the BDM may request again |
| `POST /overseas-admin/bdm-onboarding-requests/{id}/link` `{school_code}` | `overseas_admin`, `super_admin` | all | `200` item; links an existing School (H3); the BDM is told (in-app) |
| `POST /overseas-admin/schools` `{…, bdm_onboarding_request_id?}` | `overseas_admin`, `super_admin` | all | `201` as before; with the id, School + Coordinator + link + completed request commit together |

**Status table:** `401` no session; `403` wrong role ("Overseas Admin role required") or not `can_edit`; `404` organization outside scope,
"Onboarding request not found"; `409` archived ("Restore this organization first"), `{code: "organization_lost"}`,
`{code: "request_pending"}`, `{code: "already_linked"}`, `{code: "request_resolved"}`, `{code: "school_linked"}`; `422` "Onboarding
requests are for School organizations", "The MoU must be Signed or Active to request onboarding", "No School has that School ID", field
errors (empty reason, note over 1000). **Retry semantics:** a retried request answers `409 request_pending`; a retried reject / link /
create answers `409 request_resolved`. No idempotency key.

**`bdm-020` / `DEC-SCOPE-089` (built 2026-10-06; no migration) — School activity per linked School.** Design spec
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-bdm-020-school-activity-design.md` §2. Additive; nothing existing changes.

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /bdm/organizations/{id}/school-activity` | `bdm`, `bdm_manager`, `super_admin` | `load_scoped` (module / team / all) | `200 {linked, school: {name, school_code} \| null, total_students \| null, metrics: [{key, label, tracked, completed \| null, pending \| null}]}`; unlinked: `linked: false`, `metrics: []`; linked: the five School-module Student development rows (Career Guidance, Psychometric Test, Foreign Language, English Testing, University Guidance; pending = total − completed) then Student Profile Completion `tracked: false`. Aggregates only — never a student id, name or row |

**Status table:** `401` no session; `403` any other role ("BDM role required"); `404` "Organization not found" (unknown / out of scope),
"School activity is only for School organizations"; `422` malformed id. Read-only (no idempotency concern).

**`bdm-017` / `DEC-SCOPE-072` (built 2026-10-05; migration `0074_enquiry_bdm_attribution`) — student lead attribution.**
Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-05-bdm-017-lead-attribution-design.md` §4–§5. Two new BDM routes and two new admin routes;
`GET /admin/leads` is extended **additively** (every existing key, the 500-row cap and the ordering unchanged); `PATCH /admin/leads/{id}`,
`POST /public/enquiries` and the CRM webhook payload are **unchanged**. `BdmLeadOut`: `id, name, email, phone, interest, status,
bdm {id, full_name}, converted (bool), created_at` — the linked account is never shown to BDMs.

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /bdm/organizations/{org_id}/leads?limit&offset` | any reader of the organization | `caller_scope` | `{items,total,limit,offset}`, `created_at desc, id desc`; `total` is the exact count (AC4) |
| `POST /bdm/organizations/{org_id}/leads` | `bdm` | assigned BDM of the organization | `201`; body `name, email, phone?, interest, note?, acknowledge_duplicate?` (`extra="forbid"`); server sets `source='bdm'`, division (D3), attribution; CRM task queued after commit |
| `GET /admin/leads?division&status&bdm_organization_id` | three admin roles | division (super_admin: all) | rows add `organization {id,code,name}`, `bdm {id,full_name}`, `converted_user {id,full_name,email}` — each `null` when absent; the organization filter is ANDed with the division scope |
| `POST /admin/leads/{lead_id}/conversion` `{student_email}` | three admin roles | division (super_admin: all) | `200` with the row; sets the link and `status='converted'` |
| `DELETE /admin/leads/{lead_id}/conversion` | three admin roles | division (super_admin: all) | `200` with the row; clears the link, status unchanged |

**Status table:** `401` no session; `403` wrong role, not the organization's assigned BDM ("Only the organization's assigned BDM can add
leads", logged `bdm_lead_write_refused` with ids only), another division's lead ("Wrong division"); `404` organization outside the caller's
scope or unknown, unknown lead; `409` daily cap ("You've added 200 leads today"), same email already a lead of this organization
(`{message, code: "possible_duplicate", matches[{id,name,created_at}], total}`; resend with `acknowledge_duplicate: true`), lead already
linked ("Unlink the current student first"), student already linked to another lead ("This student is already linked to another lead" —
also for two admins at once, via `uq_enquiries_converted_user`), unlinking an unlinked lead; `422` schema (FastAPI's list), archived
organization ("This organization is archived"), invalid conversion target (one message for missing / inactive / wrong role / wrong
division). **Retry semantics:** lead `POST` is not retry-safe (no idempotency key) — a repeat is caught by the duplicate 409; a CRM broker
failure after commit still answers `201` and leaves the lead `pending` (logged `bdm_lead_crm_enqueue_failed`).

**`AGN-011` / `DEC-SCOPE-058` (built 2026-10-02/03) — agent deposit through Razorpay.** Design spec
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-agn-011-deposit-collection-design.md` §4. Errors are FastAPI `{"detail": ...}`. INR only (D1). Every
agent write: gate `403` → org lock → application `FOR UPDATE` (scope `404 "Application not found"`) → archived/withdrawn `409` → deposit
`FOR UPDATE`. Lock order everywhere: organisation, application, deposit, payment (verify/webhook: deposit, payment).

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PUT /workflows/overseas/agent/crm/applications/{id}/deposit` (**new**) | Authenticated | Agent (Master or staff), AGN-008 scope | Body `AgentDepositSave` (`extra="forbid"`; no `currency`/`status`): `required` bool; `amount` Decimal 0.01–99,999,999.99, 2 dp (required when `required`); `due_date` nullable 2000–2100. Not required with amount/date → `422 "A deposit that is not required has no amount or due date"`; required without amount → `422 "Enter the deposit amount"`. Paid/remitted/refunded → `409 "This deposit is already paid and can no longer be changed"`. Identical body → `200`, nothing written. Amount change or `required=false` cancels the open checkout's payment (`status="cancelled"`). Audit `overseas.application.deposit` (fields, required). |
| `POST …/crm/applications/{id}/deposit/checkout` (**new**) | Authenticated | Same | `Idempotency-Key` header 1–200 chars (else `422`); no body read — the amount is the stored deposit's (AC1). No/not-required deposit `409 "No deposit is required for this application"`; paid `409 "This deposit is already paid"` (AC3); Razorpay keys missing → `200 {"status":"configuration_required"}`, nothing written (AC6); same key + payer with an order → `200` replay incl. `key_id`, `replayed: true`; same key still opening → `409 "A checkout is already being opened for this deposit"`; another member's open checkout < 15 min → `409 "Another team member is paying this deposit -- try again in a few minutes"`; ≥ 10 attempts/deposit/hour → `429` + `Retry-After`; provider error → `502 "The payment provider is unavailable -- nothing was charged. Try again shortly"` (attempt cancelled). Success `200 {status:"ready", payment_id, key_id, provider_order_id, amount, currency}`; creates a payer-owned `Payment` (`reference_type="agent_deposit"`, `reference_id`=deposit id) and its invoice; audits `payment.checkout`, `overseas.application.deposit_checkout`. No row lock held during the provider call (three steps). |
| `GET …/crm/applications/{id}/deposit/receipt` (**new**) | Authenticated | Same (any in-scope member, not only the payer) | `{url, expires_in}` like `GET /payments/{id}/receipt`; `404 "Receipt not available"` until paid. The receipt PDF adds `Student: <name>` (AC5). |
| `GET …/crm/applications/{id}` and every write response — **changed** | Authenticated | Same | Additive: `deposit`: `null` \| `{id, required, amount, currency, due_date, status, paid_at, paid_by, receipt_available, remitted_at, remittance_reference, refunded_at, refund_amount, refund_reason, checkout_in_progress}` (no payment/order ids); `payment_available` bool. |
| `GET /overseas-admin/deposits` (**new**) | Authenticated | `overseas_admin`, `super_admin` | `status` `all`(default, excludes not_required)\|`pending`\|`paid`\|`remitted`\|`refunded`, `limit` 1–100 (25), `offset` → `{items,total,limit,offset}`; item `{id, application_id, agency, student, university, amount, currency, status, due_date, paid_at, paid_by, remitted_at, remittance_reference, refunded_at, refund_amount, refund_reason, unlinked_paid_payments}`. |
| `POST /overseas-admin/deposits/{id}/remit` \| `/refund` (**new**) | Authenticated | `overseas_admin` only (D5; others `403 "Only an Overseas Admin can record remittances and refunds"`) | Remit `{remitted_on, reference 1–100}` from `paid`; refund `{refunded_on, amount, reason 1–500}` from `paid`/`remitted`, once (D3); amount above the paid amount → `422 "A refund cannot exceed the paid amount"` (AC4); date in the future / before payment → `422`; wrong state `409`; unknown `404`. Audit `overseas.deposit.remit\|refund`. Returns the item. |
| `POST /payments/{id}/verify`, `POST /payments/webhooks/razorpay` — **changed (D2, all payments)** | Unchanged | Unchanged | Decide on a row-locked payment; a `paid`/`succeeded` payment never changes status again (a later `payment.failed` or a second paid event with its own event id is recorded and ignored); paid side effects (receipt, deposit) run once. Response shapes unchanged. Agent deposit: paid only by its open checkout at the stored amount (webhook paise cross-checked); any other captured payment is audited `overseas.deposit.unlinked_payment`. |
| `GET /payments/mine` — **changed (D4)** | Unchanged | Unchanged | Excludes `reference_type="agent_deposit"`. |
| `POST /payments/{id}/checkout`, `POST /admin/payments`, `POST /admin/payments/{id}/discount` — **changed** | Unchanged | Unchanged | Refuse agent deposits: `409 "Pay this deposit from its application"`, `422 "Agent deposits are created from the application"`, `409 "An agent deposit's amount is set on its application"`. |

**`AGN-003` / `DEC-SCOPE-044` (built 2026-10-01).** Design spec §6–§8. Two optional §6 rows (Verify Documents, Reports) are switched on per
staff member by a Master; the flags are read from the database on every request, so a change applies on the next request.

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PUT /workflows/overseas/agent/team/staff/{member_id}/permissions` `{can_verify_documents, can_view_reports}` | Authenticated | Active Master of the staff member's active organisation | Body: both keys required strict booleans (`"true"` is refused), `extra=forbid`; bad body → `422` (standard `detail` list). `404 "Staff member not found"` for another agency's member, a Master's id or an unknown id; `403 "Only an agency Master can manage the team"` for staff; `403` for a pending/suspended organisation. Allowed on deactivated staff (P8). **Idempotent** (full replacement): a no-op writes nothing; a real change writes one `agent_org.staff_permissions` audit row (`entity_type='agent_org'`, outcome `updated`, `{member_id, code, before, after}`) in the same transaction and logs `agent_org_staff_permissions_updated` (ids/codes only). `200 {member}` with `permissions`. |
| `PATCH /workflows/overseas/documents/{id}/verify` — **agent addendum** | Authenticated | Agent: Master, or staff with Verify; the document must be in the agency's scope | Counselor/admin behaviour is unchanged (the route signature is still `payload: dict`). Agent check order: `403 "Your agency Master hasn't given you permission to verify documents"` (staff without Verify) → `422` (body `{verification_status: verified\|rejected\|changes_required, notes? ≤ 10000}`, `extra=forbid`) → `403 "Only an agency Master can reject documents or request changes"` (staff, any decision but `verified`) → `404 "Document not found"` (row locked `FOR UPDATE`) → `403 "Document is outside your assigned scope"` → `409 "This document has already been reviewed"`. **Pending only** (P5): a repeat after success gets `409` and changes nothing, so retry is safe. Scope: the document's application via `_assigned_application`, else an `AgentStudent` link by an organisation member. Notifies the student ("Document reviewed"); audit `document.verify` with metadata = the validated `{verification_status, notes}` plus `member_role` (never the raw payload). Logs (`app.workflows`): `agent_document_reviewed`, `document_review_refused_no_permission`, `document_review_refused_staff_outcome` (ids only). Response `{id, verification_status}`. |
| `GET /auth/me` and the staff member shape | Authenticated | Self / active Master | Additive. `/auth/me` gains `agent_permissions`: **effective** permissions `{can_verify_documents, can_view_reports}` — a Master gets both `true`, staff get their toggles, a non-agent gets `null`. Every staff member object (list, create, edit, deactivate, reactivate, reset, PUT) gains `permissions: {can_verify_documents, can_view_reports}`. |
| `GET /portal/overseas/agent/reports` | Authenticated | Master; staff with Reports | Staff without `can_view_reports` → `403 "Your agency Master hasn't given you access to reports"`. With it on, staff get today's report without the commission row. |

**`AGN-021` / `DEC-SCOPE-046` (built 2026-10-01).** Design spec §4–§5. A Master reads one staff member's student-journey activity from the
existing audit log; read-only (no lock, no commit, no cache, no migration).

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /workflows/overseas/agent/team/staff/{member_id}/activity?limit=25&offset=0` | Authenticated | Active Master of the staff member's active organisation | `member_id` UUID. `limit` 1–100 (default 25), `offset` 0–10000 (default 0), else `422`. Response `{items, total, limit, offset}`; item `{id, at, action, subject, fields}`, ordered `created_at DESC, id DESC`. `action` is one of seven, in this order: `agent_student.create`, `agent_student.update`, `agent_student.duplicate_override`, `agent.student_link`, `overseas.application.create`, `document.upload`, `document.verify`. `subject`: record → student name; application → `"<student> — <university>"`; document → `"<document_type> — <student>"`; missing or unresolvable → `"No longer available"`. `fields`: field names for `agent_student.update`, else `null`. Errors: Staff `403` "Only an agency Master can manage the team"; inactive agency `403`; another agency's member, a Master's member id or an unknown id `404` "Staff member not found" (identical); `422` for bad `limit`/`offset`/`member_id`. **Never returned:** reviewer notes, field values, emails, phone numbers, other metadata. One info log `agent_org_staff_activity_viewed` (ids only). |

**`AGN-007` / `DEC-SCOPE-049` (built 2026-10-01; migration `0056_agent_shortlist`).** Design spec §5. Agency-private universities and a
per-student university shortlist. Errors are FastAPI `{"detail": ...}`, snake_case fields, `422` for validation, `extra="forbid"` on every body
(server-owned `org_id`, `agent_student_id`, `created_by_*` are never accepted). Every write runs `_gate` → agency lock → scoped `FOR UPDATE` loads →
validate → write → audit → one commit. No `Idempotency-Key` and no ETag (D10). `POST` is **not idempotent** and is unsafe to retry; concurrent
`PATCH`es are last-write-wins under the agency lock; a repeated `DELETE` is `404`.

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /workflows/overseas/agent/crm/universities?q=&limit=20&offset=0` | Authenticated | Agent (Master or staff) of an active organisation; **not** `super_admin` (`403`) | `q` (max length 100) searches name, country and city (`LIKE` metacharacters literal). `limit` 1–100, `offset` ≥ 0, else `422`. `{items, total, limit, offset}` ordered `lower(name), id`; item `{id, name, country, city, entry_requirements, created_at, updated_at}`. |
| `POST /workflows/overseas/agent/crm/universities` | Authenticated | Master (staff `403 "Only an agency Master can add universities"`) | Body `{name (≤200, required), country (≤120, required), city (≤120), entry_requirements (≤2000)}`; text through `clean_free_text`. `201 {"university": …}`. `409 "This university is already in your agency's list"` (same name + country, case-insensitive; the same pair in another agency is allowed); `422 "Your agency has reached the limit of 500 universities"`. |
| `PATCH …/crm/universities/{id}` | Authenticated | Master (staff `403 "Only an agency Master can edit universities"`) | Same fields, all optional; omitted = unchanged; `null` clears `city` / `entry_requirements`; `name` / `country` cannot be cleared (`422`). Loaded by `id AND org_id`; another agency's id → `404` (the role check runs **after** the scoped load). Same `409` duplicate. `200 {"university": …}`; a no-op PATCH writes no audit row. |
| `DELETE …/crm/universities/{id}` | Authenticated | Master (staff `403 "Only an agency Master can delete universities"`) | `204`. In use → `409 "This university is on N shortlist entries; remove it from them first"` (singular "entry" when N = 1: "This university is on 1 shortlist entry; remove it from them first"). |
| `GET …/crm/students/{student_id}/shortlist?limit=20&offset=0` | Authenticated | Master: any agency student; staff: assigned only | Student via `load_scoped`; out of scope → `404 "Student not found"`. `limit` 1–100, `offset` ≥ 0, else `422`; offset past the end → `items: []` with the true `total`. `{items, total, limit, offset}` ordered `created_at, id`. Readable for an archived student. |
| `POST …/crm/students/{student_id}/shortlist` | Authenticated | Master or staff (scope `404` first) | Body `{university_id, agent_university_id, course_id, course_title (≤200), intake (≤120), tuition_fee (≤120), entry_requirements (≤2000)}`, all optional in the schema; exactly one university is required by validation. `201 {"entry": …}`. Archived student → `409 "Unarchive this student first"`. |
| `PATCH …/crm/students/{student_id}/shortlist/{entry_id}` | Authenticated | Same | Merge-then-validate: the merged row is validated as a whole, so a university change that leaves a stale `course_id` is `422` unless the course is sent too. Omitted = unchanged; `null` clears. Entry loaded with `id AND agent_student_id`, else `404 "Shortlist entry not found"`. A PATCH that changes nothing → `200`, no audit row. `200 {"entry": …}`. |
| `DELETE …/crm/students/{student_id}/shortlist/{entry_id}` | Authenticated | Same | `204`. Archived student → `409`. |

Entry shape: `{id, university: {source: "catalogue"|"agency", id, name, slug (catalogue only, else null), country}, course: {id (catalogue only, else null), title} | null, intake, tuition_fee, entry_requirements, created_by (full name), created_at, updated_at}`. Catalogue country is `University.country.name`; agency country is `agent_universities.country`; country is never stored on the entry.

Entry validation order (`422`, nothing written): (1) exactly one of `university_id` / `agent_university_id` ("Choose a catalogue university or one of your agency's universities"); (2) `university_id` must exist ("University not found"); (3) `agent_university_id` must exist **and** belong to the caller's agency ("University not found" — the same answer for another agency's id and a nonexistent id); (4) `course_id` and `course_title` together ("Choose a catalogue course or type a course, not both"); (5) `course_id` with an agency university ("Catalogue courses can only be chosen with a catalogue university"); (6) the course must belong to the university ("Course does not belong to selected university", the existing message); (7) create only: 50 entries already → "This student's shortlist is full (50 entries)". An `IntegrityError` maps to the matching `409`, never a `500`.

Audit (same transaction, fail closed, ids / field names / counts only, never free-text values): `agent_student.shortlist_add` `{entry_id, university_source, fields}`, `agent_student.shortlist_update` `{entry_id, fields}`, `agent_student.shortlist_remove` `{entry_id}` (entity `agent_student` / student id; all three are in the `AGN-021` staff-activity whitelist) and Master-only `agent_university.create` / `.update` / `.delete` (entity `agent_university`; not whitelisted).

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /portal/overseas/agent/universities` | Authenticated | Agent | **New section**, header-only payload ("Universities"); exists because `PortalPage` fetches `/portal/overseas/agent/{section}` for every nav section. No other portal branch changes. |

## 10. Notifications, Payments (system-facing, `NOT-*`, `PAY-001`)

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| *(internal)* notification dispatch | System (Celery task, no external route) | — | Triggered by domain events (status changes, approvals). Not a client-callable endpoint — listed for traceability only (`NOT-001`–`003`). **Updated 2026-09-30 (`ENH-014` slice 1, spec §6, `DEC-NOT-001` extension):** triggers now queue one `notification_deliveries` row per channel (email always; WhatsApp/SMS only for recipients who opted in) and the row is published to Celery only when the root transaction commits; `deliver_notification_task` sends it (retries 60 s / 300 s / 1500 s, at most 4 attempts); `sweep_stale_deliveries_task` runs every 5 minutes. Statuses: `queued`, `sending`, `retrying`, `sent`, `failed`, `not_configured`, `skipped`. |
| `GET /account/notification-preferences` — **Addendum, 2026-09-30 (`ENH-014`, `DEC-NOT-001` extension, spec §5.1)** | Authenticated | Self (user from the session; no id in the path) | `200` `{"whatsapp": bool, "sms": bool, "phone_valid": bool}`; defaults all false when no row exists. `phone_valid` = whether `User.phone` normalises to E.164 (default country India); the phone is not echoed. `401` when signed out. Email and in-app are always on and are not fields. |
| `PUT /account/notification-preferences` — **Addendum, 2026-09-30 (`ENH-014`, spec §5.2, `ENH-014-AC01`–`AC03`, `AC15`)** | Authenticated | Self only; no admin override | Body `{"whatsapp": StrictBool, "sms": StrictBool}`, both required, unknown fields rejected (`422`). Returns the `GET` shape. `422` `"Add a valid mobile number to your profile first"` when turning a channel on while `phone_valid` is false (a user with no valid phone can still turn channels off); nothing is written. Upsert on `user_id`; opt-in timestamp set on false→true, cleared on →false; one `notification_preference.update` audit row per change (booleans and consent-copy version `enh014-v1` only, never the phone). |
| `POST /communications/notify` — **Status note, 2026-09-30 (`ENH-014`, spec §5.3)** | Authenticated | `super_admin`, `it_admin`, `overseas_admin`, `placement_team`, `counselor` (existing role gate, unchanged; `202`) | Request and response shape unchanged. In the recipient branch the per-channel statuses become `"queued"`; WhatsApp/SMS are dropped for recipients who have not opted in; the delivery rows attach to the notification created by the request. For a named recipient the request's `metadata` is no longer forwarded in the delivery payload: queued deliveries carry the title, body and link only. The no-recipient branch is unchanged (it still forwards `metadata`). |
| `POST /payments/webhooks/razorpay` | Provider signature (Razorpay), no session/cookie — a system endpoint | System | Actual path corrected `prompts/13`, 2026-09-03, to match the inherited `/payments` router (this document's original draft omitted the router prefix). Idempotent on the `x-razorpay-event-id` header (Razorpay's own dedup key — confirmed against Razorpay's docs, it is not a JSON body field). Never marks a `Payment` `succeeded`/generates a `Receipt` without signature verification (`PAY-001-AC03`); an invalid-signature or duplicate delivery is logged to `PaymentWebhookEvent` and discarded, never reprocessed. See `INTEGRATION_CONTRACTS.md` §2. |
| `POST /admin/payments` | Authenticated | Admin (own division; Super Admin any) | Manual/offline payment record (`STU-010-AC02`) — an Admin can record a `Payment` directly as `paid`, generating both `Invoice` and `Receipt` in the same step. |
| `POST /admin/payments/emi-schedule` | Authenticated | Admin (own division; Super Admin any) | Admin-discretionary EMI plan (`STU-010`) — each installment's amount/due date is supplied explicitly (no fixed amortization policy is confirmed anywhere), creating one linked `Payment` row per installment. |
| `POST /webhooks/twilio/status` | Provider signature (Twilio) | System | Delivery-status callback for WhatsApp/SMS — updates `NotificationLog`, does not affect the underlying business event. **Not implemented in slice 1 (`ENH-014`, `DEC-NOT-001` 2026-09-30, spec §2 non-goals; `INTEGRATION_CONTRACTS.md` §4) — recorded follow-up.** No such route exists; `sent` means Twilio accepted the message. When built it must verify the Twilio signature. |
| `POST /webhooks/crm/zoho` | Shared-secret / provider signature (config-dependent, see `INTEGRATION_CONTRACTS.md` §1) | System | Inbound sync-status acknowledgement from Zoho, if the configured integration supports it — outbound direction is primary (§ Integration Contracts). |

## 12A. School (`SCH-001`–`006`) — addendum, 2026-09-14

Per `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §2.12 and `DATA_MODEL.md` §6.11–6.18. School role division assignment
(`overseas`, proposed) is **not confirmed** (`RBAC_MATRIX.md` §5) — routes below are grouped under
`/school/*` and `/overseas-admin/*` regardless, since URL structure in this document is domain-based,
not division-based; the open division question does not block listing the surface itself.

| Method/Path | Auth | Scope | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /overseas-admin/schools` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin | Creates `School` + seed `school_coordinator` account, both active immediately, no approval gate (`SCH-003-AC02`/`AC04`). `ENH-003`: `coordinator_password` is no longer accepted (`422`); the Coordinator receives a 72-hour set-password link; response adds `email_status`, `expires_at` (and `development_welcome_token` in development/test only). A malformed `coordinator_email` is `422`, a duplicate `409` (a lost race also rolls the school back). **Addendum, 2026-09-22 (`ENH-009`, `DEC-SCOPE-025`, design doc `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-22-enh-009-school-profile-design.md`):** the body additively accepts the full School Profile field set — `branch`, `address`, `contact_number`, `email`, `website`, `grades_available`, `board` (`CBSE`/`ICSE`/`State`/`IB`/`Other`, `422` otherwise), `partnership_date`, `mou_reference`, `edusphere_bdm`, `monthly_visit_schedule`, `vice_principal_name`, plus the still-accepted `tier`/`tier_valid_until`. Every field is optional and every `max_length` matches its column exactly (a longer value is `422`, never a database error — the same QA-001 rule as the name limits above). An unknown field is `422` (`extra: forbid`). The server generates a unique 8-character uppercase-hex `school_code` (the business-facing School ID, same convention as `student_code`) and returns it, along with the computed `student_count`, `teacher_count`, `principal_name`, `school_coordinator_name` and `career_counsellor_names` — those five are derived from role assignments at read time, never stored on `School`. |
| `POST /overseas-admin/school-staff` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin | Creates an `academic_team` / `career_counselor` / `psychometric_team` account (`DEC-SCOPE-014`). `ENH-003`: same provisioning contract as above — `password` is `422`, malformed `email` `422`, a name longer than its column is `422` naming the field (`full_name` / `coordinator_full_name` ≤ 160, school `name` ≤ 200, `city` / `state` ≤ 120 — applies equally to `POST /admin/users` and `POST /overseas-admin/schools`; a value at exactly the limit is accepted), duplicate `409`, a 72-hour set-password link is emailed after commit, response adds `email_status` and `expires_at`. |
| `GET /overseas-admin/schools` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin | List of all partner schools (not paginated — this endpoint has never taken paging parameters; earlier wording here was wrong). **Addendum, 2026-09-22 (`ENH-009`):** each row carries the same full profile shape as `POST /overseas-admin/schools` returns, including `school_code` and the five computed fields. Those computed fields are batched across the whole list, so the query count is a small constant rather than five per school. |
| `PATCH /overseas-admin/schools/{school_id}` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin | Updates a `School`'s partnership `tier`/`tier_valid_until` (`DEC-SCOPE-017`). **Addendum, 2026-09-22 (`ENH-009`, `DEC-SCOPE-025`):** additively accepts every School Profile field listed under `POST /overseas-admin/schools` above, with the same validation. Only the fields actually present in the body are written (`exclude_unset`), so an explicit `null` clears a field while an omitted field is left untouched. `name`/`city`/`state`/`coordinator_*` stay out of scope — they were never editable here. Writes a `school.tier_update` audit row when `tier`/`tier_valid_until` change and a separate `school.profile_update` row listing the changed **field names only** (never their values) when any profile field changes; a request touching both writes both rows. 404 for an unknown `school_id`. |
| `GET /overseas-admin/schools/lookup?code=` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin only | **`ENH-009`.** Resolves a business-facing School ID (`school_code`) to the full school profile, mirroring the `GET /overseas-admin/school-students/lookup?code=` convention — deliberately **narrower** than that sibling, which also allows `counselor`: a Career Counselor has no reason to read a partner school's commercial profile (MoU reference, BDM, tier). 403 for any other role, 404 for an unknown code. |
| `POST /school/team/invites` | Authenticated | School Coordinator, own institution | Body includes target `role` (`school_principal`/`school_teacher`/`school_parent`). 422 for any other role value (`SCH-003-AC03`). Creates a single-use `SchoolAccountInvite` token; invite delivery is email only (`NOT-001`). **Addendum, 2026-09-14:** actually sent via real SMTP (`app/services/mailer.py`, `SMTP_*` env vars) in addition to the existing webhook-forwarding `send_notification` call — HTML template with the EduSphere logo, single accept link, Reply-To set to the inviting Coordinator (their name/email, not the recipient's) so replies reach them directly, since the technical SMTP sender must stay a single verified account. `email_status` in the response is `"sent"`/`"failed"`/`"not_configured"`; `development_invite_token` is still returned in a development environment regardless of email outcome. |
| `GET /school/team` | Authenticated | School Coordinator, own institution | Lists the institution's Principal/Teacher/Parent accounts and pending invites. |
| `PATCH /school/team/accounts/{user_id}` | Authenticated | School Coordinator, own institution | **Addendum, 2026-09-22 (`ENH-010`, `School CRM.md` Part B §2, `DEC-SCOPE-025` drafted).** Activates/deactivates a Principal/Teacher/Parent account. Body: `{"active": bool}` (raw `dict`, not a typed schema — extra fields are silently ignored and cannot mutate anything else, pinned by `test_toggle_ignores_extra_fields_and_changes_only_active`). `422` if `active` is missing or non-boolean. `404` if the target account doesn't exist. `403` if it exists but is outside the coordinator's own institution, is not a Principal/Teacher/Parent (blocks targeting a Coordinator, including the caller's own account, or a peer Coordinator), or the caller isn't a Coordinator. A deactivated account is rejected on its very next request anywhere in the app — `User.active` is re-checked on every call via `get_current_user`, not just at login. Writes one `AuditLog` row (`school.team_account_update`) per call with actor (`user_id`), target (`entity_id`), and the new `active` value in `metadata_json`. Returns `{"id": uuid, "active": bool}`. |
| `POST /school/invites/{token}/accept` | Public (token) | — | Single-use — an already-`accepted`/`expired`/`revoked` token returns 409, never silently re-processed (`SCH-003-AC06`). The created account's `school_id` is always the invite's own, never a value supplied in this request body — closes the same IDOR class as `AGT-002`/`UNI-001`. **Exception, `ENH-008` (2026-09-22), `school_parent` invites only:** a newly-accepted Parent account gets `profile={}` (no `school_id` at all), not the invite's `school_id` — a Parent's institution(s) are derived entirely from their `SchoolParentLink` rows, since `ENH-008` allows one Parent to be linked across more than one school. Every other invited role (`school_principal`/`school_teacher`) is unaffected: `profile.school_id` is still always the invite's own. **Addendum, 2026-09-14:** for a `school_parent` invite, acceptance also links every `SchoolStudent` at this school whose `pending_parent_email` matches the invite's own email (not just the one that triggered the invite) — covers a second child added to the roster while the first invite was still pending, `DATA_MODEL.md` §6.11 addendum. |
| `GET /school/dashboard` | Authenticated | School Coordinator / Principal (read-only), own institution | Aggregation read — full `School CRM.md` point-1 dashboard (`SCH-001` addendum, 2026-09-16): all named KPI cards, grade-wise breakdown, completion rings, global-education pipeline, visa status, upcoming activities, and explicit `tracked=false` / `value=null` markers for point-1 modules that still have no confirmed data model (digital portfolio, internship, soft-skills training, program participation). |
| `GET /school/reports` | Authenticated | School Coordinator / Principal (read-only), own institution | **Addendum, 2026-09-14 (Reports module), extended 2026-09-16 to share the dashboard aggregate.** Real, computed-from-live-data figures only, never a fabricated placeholder (`DATA_MODEL.md` §8's rule, applied identically to `RPT-001`). Returns roster/team counts, a grade-wise breakdown, career-guidance/psychometric/counselling/test-prep/language/results completion, global-education pipeline, visa status, and activity/attendance totals. `results_published` counts only `status=published` rows — a Draft/Verified result's existence stays sensitive to these two roles even in aggregate (`SCH-006-AC02`), never surfaced here in any form. Teacher/Parent get 403. |
| `GET /school/students`, `GET /school/students/{id}` | Authenticated | School Coordinator (own institution) / Principal (own institution, read-only) / Teacher (own institution, assigned students only) / Parent (own institution, own child(ren) only) | 403 (not 404) on a student that exists but is outside the acting role's scope — ownership isn't sensitive here, existence within the caller's own institution generally is not either (`SCH-001-AC02`/`AC03`, same masking convention as `TRN-002-AC02`). **Addendum, 2026-09-18 (ENH-001):** response now additively includes `academic_year_id` (string\|null, system-assigned at student creation to the then-active academic year) and `grade_level` (integer 1-12\|null, nullable). |
| `POST /school/students` | Authenticated | School Coordinator, own institution | Creates one `SchoolStudent`. `school_id` always server-derived from the acting Coordinator, never client-supplied (`SCH-001-AC04`). **Addendum, 2026-09-14:** accepts optional `parent_name`/`parent_email`. An email matching an existing `school_parent` account links immediately (`parent_status: "linked"`); a genuinely new email creates and sends a `SchoolAccountInvite` (`"invited"`); an email already pending from another roster entry is reused, not re-sent (`"invite_reused"`); an email belonging to a non-Parent account is rejected (422). **Updated, `ENH-008` (2026-09-22):** an email matching an existing Parent at a *different* school is no longer rejected — cross-school linking is the AC1 success case, and links immediately exactly like a same-school Parent (`"linked"`). The rejection error text also dropped its former "at this school"/"at your school" qualifier (`_parent_email_conflict`, `link_parent`) — it now just says the email belongs to an existing account that is not a Parent, with no school qualifier, since the school is no longer part of the check. **Addendum, 2026-09-18 (ENH-001):** returns the created student with `academic_year_id` and `grade_level` as per the list endpoint above. Accepts optional `grade_level` in the request body (1-12, nullable); 422 if provided but invalid. |
| `PATCH /school/students/{id}` | Authenticated | School Coordinator, own institution only | Same `parent_name`/`parent_email` addendum as `POST /school/students` above. **Addendum, 2026-09-18 (ENH-001):** accepts optional `grade_level` in the request body; 422 if provided but invalid. Returns the updated student with `academic_year_id` and `grade_level` as per the list endpoint above. |
| `GET /school/students/roster-template` | Authenticated | School Coordinator | Downloads the fixed-format upload template — template-download-first (`SCH-002`). Columns (2026-09-14, extended 2026-09-18 for `ENH-001`): `full_name`, `date_of_birth`, `grade_or_class`, `assigned_teacher_email`, `parent_name`, `parent_email`, `grade_level` — the middle two follow the same linked/invited/invite_reused/rejected behavior as `POST /school/students` above, per row. |
| `POST /school/students/bulk-upload` | Authenticated | School Coordinator, own institution | Idempotency key required (batch-creating action). Server-side validation before any row commits (§ `SECURITY_CONTROLS.md` §5 pattern). Creates a `SchoolRosterUploadBatch`; a row that fails validation does not block the rest of the batch — each row succeeds or fails independently (`SCH-002-AC04`), including an invalid `parent_email`. **Addendum, 2026-09-18 (ENH-001):** also accepts an optional `grade_level` column (1-12); an invalid value rejects only that row, same per-row-independent behavior as an invalid `parent_email`. |
| `GET /school/roster-uploads/{batch_id}` | Authenticated | School Coordinator, own institution | Row-level accept/reject report; `accepted_count` + `rejected_count` always equals `total_rows` once `status=completed` (`DATA_MODEL.md` §6.13). |
| `POST`/`PATCH /school/students`, `GET /school/students[/{id}]`, `…/overview` — **Addendum, 2026-09-23 (`ENH-025`, `DEC-SCOPE-029`)** | as the rows above | as the rows above | **Additive only.** Ten optional Student Master fields: `section` (≤20), `roll_number` (≤20), `gender` (`female`\|`male`\|`other`\|`prefer_not_to_say`, case-insensitive in, lower-case out), `student_mobile` (7–20 of digits/space/`+-()`, ≥7 digits), `city` (≤120), `subjects`/`career_interests`/`preferred_countries`/`preferred_courses` (string lists, ≤20 items × ≤80 chars, trimmed, case-insensitive de-dup), `global_education_interest` (JSON boolean or `null`). Control and bidi-override characters are refused. PATCH: an absent key is unchanged, `null` clears, `""`/`[]` are stored as `null`. Responses add those ten keys plus `has_photo` (bool); the storage key is never returned. Other unknown top-level keys keep today's behaviour (ignored, never applied). **Errors:** 422 `"<field> <reason>"` (one string, never echoing the value); **409** `roll_number '<r>' is already used in this grade and section for this academic year` — uniqueness is per school + academic year + `grade_level` + `lower(section)`, a blank value forming its own group, decided by the `uq_school_students_roll` index (no read-then-check). Audit metadata gains `changed_fields` (names only). |

A missing column, short row or blank cell means "not set". List cells split on `;`; `global_education_interest` accepts `yes/no/true/false/1/0` (else the row is rejected with `global_education_interest must be yes or no`). Invalid values and roll-number clashes (including two rows of the same file) reject **that row only** (`SCH-002-AC04`); a rejected row never links or invites its parent. **Photo cannot be set by CSV** (single-edit only, `DEC-SCOPE-029` item 3). |
| `POST /school/academic-team/results/bulk-upload`, `POST /school/psychometric-team/records/bulk-upload`, `POST /school/academic-team/test-prep-records/bulk-upload`, `POST /school/academic-team/language-records/bulk-upload` — **Added 2026-10-01 (`ENH-028`, `DEC-SCOPE-043`)** | Authenticated | `academic_team` (results, test prep, language) / `psychometric_team` (psychometric); each row limited to the uploader's own portfolio (`SchoolStaffAssignment`) | **Additive; the single-record endpoints are unchanged.** Multipart `file` (CSV, UTF-8, BOM allowed) + required `Idempotency-Key` (`[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,120}`), unique per uploader + module. Rows name students by `student_code`; a row whose module columns are all blank is skipped (an untouched template row). Each accepted row creates exactly what the module's single `POST` creates (Results always `draft` + `none→draft` history; psychometric `completed` iff `report_url`; same per-record audit action, plus `bulk_batch_id`). **201** `{id, target_type, status: "completed", total_rows, accepted_count, rejected_count, rows: [{row_number (file line, header = 1), status: accepted\|rejected, error_message, student_code, created_record_id}]}`. A row is rejected on its own (first failure wins): `student_code is required` / `student_code does not match a student` → outside the portfolio (`This student is at a school outside your own portfolio`) → `<column> <reason>` (bounds = the target column widths; results also `marks_obtained must not exceed max_marks`; `report_url` http(s) only) → tier (the existing `_entitlement_denial` message; one `school.tier_access_denied` audit per school + service per batch; results are not tier-gated) → duplicate (`this student already has <noun> for the same <key>` / `same student and <key> as row N of this file`; keys: results year+term+subject, psychometric type+test_date, test prep test_type, language — case-insensitive). **Whole-file errors write nothing:** 403 wrong role (checked first); 422 key missing/malformed; 413 `The file is larger than 1 MB` (1,048,576 bytes); 422 `The file must be a UTF-8 CSV` (not UTF-8, NUL, malformed quoting), `Missing required column: <name>`, `The file has no filled-in rows`, `The file has more than 500 filled-in rows`; 409 `This upload is still being processed; retry shortly` (same key in flight > 5 s) / `These students are being updated by another request; retry shortly` (row locks > 5 s). **Replay:** same key + same file (SHA-256) → 201 with the stored report, nothing written; same key + different file → 422 `Idempotency-Key was already used for a different file`; another user's key never replays. Parents of accepted psychometric/test-prep/language rows get the single create's notice **after** the commit (a failed send never undoes a record); results notify nobody. Audit `school.bulk_upload` (target, counts, file hash). No batch-read endpoint (S2): resend the same file with the same key. |

A stored name starting `=`/`+`/`-`/`@`/TAB/CR is prefixed with `'` (spreadsheet formula injection). Empty portfolio → header only. 403 for any other role. |

Average is the mean of each result's own percentage (not `sum(marks)/sum(max_marks)`, which would be wrong across subjects with different `max_marks`), computed over **all** statuses (draft/verified/published) — the Academic Team member's own exposure level, not the published-only rule that applies to Coordinator/Teacher/Parent.
