| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/snapshot` *(new)* | `{as_of (IST date), kpis: [{id "S1"…"S15", key, label, value int}]}` in §3 source order. Partner, `super_admin` → `200`; accountant and every other role → `403` ("Operational access required" / "Management access required"); signed out → `401`. One SQL statement for all fifteen counts |

## 12DC. Management ledger (`mgmt-004`) — addendum, 2026-10-12

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-189` (ML1–ML16). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-mgmt-004-management-ledger-design.md`.
  Migration `0150_management_ledger`. Every route: partner + accountant only; `super_admin` → `403` "Finance access required"; other
  roles `403` "Management access required"; signed out `401`. Amounts are decimal strings (INR).

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/ledger` *(new)* | Query `month` (YYYY-MM), `kind`, `category` (UUID), `vertical`, `status`, `limit` 1–100 (25), `offset` 0–10 000. `200` `{items[entry], total, limit, offset, totals {income, expense, net}}`; totals = approved entries (reversals negative) matching every filter but status. `422` malformed filter |
| `POST /management/ledger` *(new)* | `{kind, entry_date, category_id, amount, description, vertical, channel?, program_id?, batch_id?}` → `201` entry detail (draft). `422` field-located (amount ≤ 0 / > 2 decimals / too large, future date, inactive / wrong-kind category, channel on income, batch of another course) |
| `GET /management/ledger/{id}` *(new)* | `200` entry detail: entry + `events[{id, at, actor, event, from_status, to_status, fields, reason}]`. Entry: `id, entry_date, kind, category {id, name, active}, amount, description, vertical, channel, program, batch, status, reversal_of_id, reversed_by_id, receipt {name, content_type} \| null, created_by, updated_by, decided_by, submitted_at, decided_at, rejection_reason, created_at, updated_at, can {edit, submit, decide, reverse}`. `404` |
| `PATCH /management/ledger/{id}` *(new)* | Partial, same fields minus `kind` (`422` if sent). Draft/rejected only (`409`); a rejected entry returns to draft. No change → `200`, nothing recorded |
| `POST /management/ledger/{id}/submit` *(new)* | Draft/rejected → submitted; else `409` |
| `POST /management/ledger/{id}/approve`, `…/reject {reason}` *(new)* | Partner only (`403` accountant); never the creator (`403`); submitted only (`409`); reason 2–500 (`422`) |
| `POST /management/ledger/{id}/reverse {reason}` *(new)* | Approved original only (`409`); `409` second live reversal or reversing a reversal. `201` the new submitted reversal |
| `PUT /management/ledger/{id}/receipt` *(new)* | Multipart `file`; PDF/JPEG/PNG by content (`415`), size cap (`413`), empty (`422`); draft/rejected only (`409`). `200` entry detail |
| `GET /management/ledger/{id}/receipt` *(new)* | The file, `attachment; filename="ledger-receipt-<date>-<id8>.<ext>"`, `nosniff`; audited; `404` none |
| `GET /management/ledger/options` *(new)* | `{categories (active), channels[{value, label}], verticals[], programs[{id, name, batches[{id, name}]}]}` |
| `GET /management/ledger/categories`, `POST …/categories {kind, nature?, name}`, `PATCH …/categories/{id} {name?, active?}` *(new)* | All categories; create `201` (`409` duplicate name per kind, `422` nature rules); rename / (de)activate (`404`, `409`) |


## 12DD. Investment register + ROI (`mgmt-014`) — addendum, 2026-10-12

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-190` (MI1–MI14). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-mgmt-014-investment-register-design.md`.
  Migration `0151_investments` (after mgmt-004's `0150_management_ledger`). Partner-only (M13): every route answers `403` to the accountant and super_admin ("Only partners can open
  this") and to every other role ("Management access required"); signed out `401`. Money is INR as decimal strings.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/investments` *(new)* | Query `date_from`, `date_to` (on `invested_on`, inclusive), `vertical`, `status`, `limit` 1–100 (25), `offset` 0–10 000. `200` `{items[Investment], total, limit, offset, totals {count, invested, actual_return, roi \| null}, investors[{id, full_name}]}`; newest first; totals over every match, cancelled excluded. `422` unknown vertical/status, `date_to` before `date_from`, malformed parameter |
| `POST /management/investments` *(new)* | Body `{invested_on, investor_kind "partner"\|"company", investor_user_id?, amount > 0, purpose 2–200, vertical, expected_return?, actual_return? (≥ 0), status? ("active"), notes? ≤ 2000}`, extra fields refused. `201` Investment. `422` worded per field, or "Choose the partner who invested" / "Choose an active partner as the investor" / "A company investment has no partner investor". Audited `mgmt.investment.create` |
| `GET /management/investments/{id}` *(new)* | `200` Investment `{id, code "INV-000001", invested_on, investor_kind, investor {id, full_name} \| null, amount, purpose, vertical, expected_return, actual_return, roi \| null, status, notes, created_by, updated_by, created_at, updated_at}`; `404` unknown |
| `PATCH /management/investments/{id}` *(new)* | Any subset of the POST fields; null for a required field `422`. Row locked; only changed fields are written and audited (`mgmt.investment.update`, before/after); no change → `200`, nothing written. `404` unknown. No `DELETE` (cancel instead) |

## 12DE. Management discussions & decisions (`mgmt-027`) — addendum, 2026-10-12

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-191` (MD1–MD14). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-mgmt-027-discussions-decisions-design.md`.
  Migration `0152_management_discussions`. `/management/discussions*`: partner + super_admin only (M13 operational tier); the accountant
  → `403` "Operational management access required"; other roles `403` "Management access required"; signed out `401`.
  `/me/management-decisions*`: any signed-in user, scoped to decisions where they are the responsible person.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/discussions` *(new)* | Query `status` (open / in_progress / completed / cancelled), `responsible` (UUID), `pending` (bool: a named person, open or in progress, by deadline, none last), `limit` 1–100 (25), `offset` 0–10 000. `200` `{items[row], total, limit, offset}`; row = `id, topic, discussion_date, department, decision, responsible {id, full_name, role} \| null, deadline, status, follow_up_date, created_at, updated_at` (never the points). `422` malformed filter |
| `GET /management/discussions/options` *(new)* | `{users[{id, full_name, role}]}`: active staff users (MD6) |
| `POST /management/discussions` *(new)* | `{topic, discussion_date, department?, points?, decision?, responsible_user_id?, deadline?, status? (open), follow_up_date?, participant_ids? (≤ 30)}` → `201` detail. `422` field-located: inactive / non-staff responsible or participant, responsible or deadline without a decision, deadline / follow-up before the discussion date, future discussion date, lengths. Notifies the responsible person (in-app + email) |
| `GET /management/discussions/{id}` *(new)* | `200` detail = row + `points, participants[], attachments[{id, name, content_type, shareable, created_at}], created_by, updated_by`. `404` |
| `PATCH /management/discussions/{id}` *(new)* | Partial; explicit `null` clears an optional field; `participant_ids` replaces the set. No change → `200`, nothing recorded. A new responsible person, or a changed decision / deadline / status, notifies the responsible person (not when they are the actor) |
| `POST /management/discussions/{id}/attachments` *(new)* | Multipart `file` + `shareable` (bool, default false); PDF/JPEG/PNG by content (`415`), size cap (`413`), empty (`422`); `409` at 10 attachments. `201` detail |
| `GET /management/discussions/{id}/attachments/{aid}` *(new)* | The file, `attachment; filename="discussion-attachment-<date>-<id8>.<ext>"`, `nosniff`; audited; `404` |
| `DELETE /management/discussions/{id}/attachments/{aid}` *(new)* | `204`; the stored object is deleted after commit; `404` |
| `GET /me/management-decisions` *(new)* | `200` `{items[{id, decision, deadline, status, attachments[{id, name, content_type}] (shareable only), updated_at}]}`: the caller's decisions, not cancelled; open/in progress first by deadline, then completed. Never the topic, points, participants, department or follow-up |
| `PATCH /me/management-decisions/{id}` *(new)* | `{status: open \| in_progress \| completed}` → `200` the line. `404` not the caller's (or unknown); `409` cancelled; `422` other status |
| `GET /me/management-decisions/{id}/attachments/{aid}` *(new)* | A shareable attachment of the caller's own, non-cancelled decision; audited; else `404` |

## 12DF. Automatic ledger sources (`mgmt-005`) — addendum, 2026-10-12

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-192` (MA1–MA12). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-mgmt-005-automatic-ledger-sources-design.md`.
  No migration, no new route. Access as §12DC (finance tier only).

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/ledger` *(changed, additive)* | New query `include=auto` (any other value `422`). Without it the response is §12DC's, each item now also carrying `source: "manual"`. With it, `items` mixes manual entries and automatic lines `{source "payment"\|"agent_commission"\|"trip_expense"\|"recruiter_payment"\|"university_commission", id (the source row), entry_date (IST day the money moved), kind, vertical, category_label, amount, currency, description (no person names), counted}`, newest first, paged as before. `month`, `kind`, `vertical` filter every line; `category` and `status` keep manual entries only. `totals {income, expense, net, not_counted[{currency, kind, amount, count}]}`: approved manual entries + INR automatic lines; other currencies are only in `not_counted`. Agent deposits never appear |

## 12DG. Management to-do + staff "Management tasks" (`mgmt-028`) — addendum, 2026-10-12

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-193` (MT1–MT14). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-mgmt-028-management-tasks-design.md`.
  Migration `0153_management_tasks`. `/management/tasks*`: partner + super_admin only (M13 operational tier); the accountant → `403`
  "Operational management access required"; other roles `403` "Management access required"; signed out `401`.
  `/me/management-tasks*`: any signed-in user, scoped to tasks they own.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/tasks` *(new)* | Query `owner` (`me` or a user id; else `422`), `band` (overdue / today / week / later / completed / cancelled; else `422`), `limit` 1–200 (100), `offset` 0–10 000. Without `band`: every band but cancelled (completed = the last 7 IST days). `200` `{items[task], total, limit, offset, today, counts{overdue, today, week, later, completed}}`; task = `id, title, owner {id, full_name, role}, owner_active, priority, deadline, status, band, source, completed_at, created_by, created_at, updated_at`. Bands are IST days; This Week = after today up to Sunday (Monday–Sunday week) |
| `GET /management/tasks/options` *(new)* | `{users[{id, full_name, role}]}`: active staff users (MT6) |
| `POST /management/tasks` *(new)* | `{title (2–200), owner_user_id, priority? (medium), deadline, status? (open)}` → `201` task. `422` field-located: inactive / non-staff owner, deadline before today (IST) or more than two years ahead, lengths, unknown field (`source` is never the client's). Notifies the owner (in-app + email) unless they are the actor |
| `GET /management/tasks/{id}` *(new)* | `200` task; `404` |
| `PATCH /management/tasks/{id}` *(new)* | Partial; no field can be `null` (`422`). No change → `200`, nothing recorded. A changed owner is checked (active staff); an unchanged past deadline or departed owner is not. A new owner, or a changed title / deadline / priority / status, notifies the owner (not when they are the actor). Completing stamps `completed_at`; reopening clears it |
| `GET /me/management-tasks` *(new)* | `200` `{items[{id, title, priority, deadline, status, band, updated_at}], today, counts}`: the caller's tasks, not cancelled, completed only for the last 7 days (at most 200) |
| `PATCH /me/management-tasks/{id}` *(new)* | `{status: open \| in_progress \| completed}` → `200` the task. `404` not the caller's (or unknown); `409` cancelled; `422` other status |

## 12DH. Salary management + employee cost ratio (`mgmt-011`) — addendum, 2026-10-12

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-194` (SM1–SM14). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-mgmt-011-salary-management-design.md`.
- **Migration:** `0154_salaries` (after mgmt-028's `0153_management_tasks`).
- **Access:** finance tier only (M13), through the ledger's `require_finance`:
  - super_admin gets `403` "Finance access required";
  - every other role gets `403` "Management access required";
  - signed out gets `401`.
- **Money:** INR as decimal strings. `month` is `YYYY-MM`.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/salaries` *(new)* | **Query:** `month` (default: the current IST month). **`200`:** `{month, current_month, rows[], totals, revenue, ratio, updated_at, updated_by}`.<br>• `rows[]` = `{department {id, name, active}, entered, headcount, payable, paid, incentives, commissions, employer_cost, total_cost, warnings ["paid_exceeds_payable"]}`. They are the active departments plus any inactive department with a row in the month. A department without a row has `entered: false` and null figures.<br>• `totals` holds the same figures over the entered rows.<br>• `total_cost` = paid + incentives + commissions + employer cost (R6).<br>• `revenue` = the month's counted ledger income.<br>• `ratio` = R6 ÷ revenue × 100 (2 decimals) or null.<br>**`422`:** malformed month, or a month after the current one |
| `PUT /management/salaries?month=YYYY-MM` *(new)* | **Body:** `{rows: [{department_id, headcount 0–100 000, payable, paid, incentives, commissions, employer_cost (each ≥ 0, ≤ 2 decimals)}]}` (≤ 500 rows, extra fields refused).<br>**Effect:** replaces the month — rows sent are upserted, rows left out are removed, and `rows: []` clears it. Paid > payable is saved with a warning. A transaction-scoped advisory lock serialises saves of one month.<br>**`200`:** the month as GET shows it.<br>**`422`:** on the field (`rows.N.field`), or a duplicate department ("Each department can appear only once in a month"), an unknown department, an inactive department not yet in the month, or a future month.<br>**Audit:** one `mgmt.salary.update` (changed field names + count, no values) when something changed |
| `GET /management/departments` *(new)* | **`200`:** `{items[{id, name, active}]}`, active first |
| `POST /management/departments` *(new)* | **Body:** `{name 2–80}`.<br>**`201`:** the department.<br>**Errors:** `409` "A department with this name already exists" (case-insensitive); `422` name length.<br>**Audit:** `mgmt.department.create` |
| `PATCH /management/departments/{id}` *(new)* | **Body:** `{name?, active?}`. A change that changes nothing writes nothing.<br>**Errors:** `404` unknown id; `409` duplicate name.<br>**Audit:** `mgmt.department.update` |
| `GET /management/ledger?include=auto` *(changed, additive)* | A sixth automatic source, `salary`:<br>• one expense line per saved department month with cost > 0;<br>• dated the month's last day, vertical `shared`, label "Salaries", description "Salaries · <department>", INR, counted.<br>`LedgerAutoLine.source` gains `"salary"` |

## 12DI. Expense dashboard (`mgmt-010`) — addendum, 2026-10-12

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-195` (ME1–ME10). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-mgmt-010-expense-dashboard-design.md`.
  No migration. Finance tier only (partner, accountant); `super_admin` `403` "Finance access required"; other roles `403`; signed out `401`.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/expenses` *(new)* | Query `month` (`YYYY-MM`; default the current IST month). `422`: not `YYYY-MM`, before 2000-01, or after the current IST month ("Choose this month or an earlier one"). `200` `{month, year_start (1 April of the Indian financial year), year_label ("2026-27"), groups[{key, label, month, year, share}] (the 8 §8 heads, always all, source order), categories[{label, group, month, year}] (money in the year to date, largest first), months[{month, total}] (financial year start … selected month), totals{month, year}, not_counted[{currency, amount, count}]}`. Rupees only: approved manual expenses (reversals negative) + INR automatic expense lines; other currencies only in `not_counted`. Totals = the sum of the heads; year = the sum of the months. Read-only |

## 12DJ. Accounts dashboard (`mgmt-013`) — addendum, 2026-10-12

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-196` (AD1–AD14). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-mgmt-013-accounts-dashboard-design.md`.
- **Migration:** `0155_finance_balances` (after mgmt-011's `0154_salaries`).
- **Access:** finance tier only (M13), through the ledger's `require_finance`:
  - super_admin gets `403` "Finance access required";
  - every other role gets `403` "Management access required";
  - signed out gets `401`.
- **Money:** INR as decimal strings. `month` is `YYYY-MM`.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/accounts` *(new)* | **Query:** `month` (default: the current IST month).<br>**`200`:** `{month, current_month, as_of, income, expenses, profit, not_counted[], receivables, pending_invoices, balances}`.<br>• `income`/`expenses` = `{heads[{key, label, amount, parts[{label, amount}]}], total}`. There are 5 income heads (`abroad`, `it`, `recruitment`, `school`, `other`) and 8 expense heads (`salaries`, `rent`, `marketing`, `travel`, `trainers`, `commissions`, `operations`, `technology`), all from the month's counted ledger lines.<br>• `profit` = income − expenses (R4).<br>• `not_counted[]` = `{currency, kind, amount, count}`.<br>• `receivables` = `{total, overdue, fees, recruitment, university, not_counted[]}`, as of `as_of` (today, IST).<br>• `pending_invoices` = `{count, amount}`.<br>• `balances` = `{cash_bank, payables, tax_liabilities, updated_at, updated_by}`, where null means not entered.<br>**`422`:** a malformed month, or a month after the current one |
| `PUT /management/accounts/balances?month=YYYY-MM` *(new)* | **Body:** `{cash_bank, payables, tax_liabilities}`. Each is a decimal string or null; `cash_bank` may be negative; `payables`/`tax_liabilities` must be ≥ 0. At most 2 decimals, magnitude below 10¹², extra fields refused.<br>**Effect:** saves the month's row, and all null removes it. A transaction-scoped advisory lock serialises saves of one month.<br>**`200`:** the month as GET shows it.<br>**`422`:** on the field, or a future or missing month.<br>**Audit:** one `mgmt.balance.create`/`update` (changed field names, no amounts) when something changed |

## 12DK. IT Training revenue dashboard (`mgmt-007`) — addendum, 2026-10-13

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-197` (IR1–IR12). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-mgmt-007-it-revenue-dashboard-design.md`.
- **Migration:** none (read-only).
- **Access:** finance tier only (M13), through the ledger's `require_finance`, checked before any query:
  - super_admin gets `403` "Finance access required";
  - every other role gets `403` "Management access required";
  - signed out gets `401`.
- **Period:** `month` is `YYYY-MM` (default the current IST month; January 2000 to this month); `period` is `month` (default) or `year`
  (the Indian financial year from 1 April to the end of `month`). **Money:** INR as decimal strings.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/revenue/it` *(new)* | **Query:** `month`, `period`.<br>**`200`:** `{month, period, start, end, label, totals{revenue, costs, contribution, enrolments}, income[{key, label, amount}], costs[{key, label, amount}], courses[{program_id, title, enrolments, revenue, costs, contribution, batches[{batch_id, name, enrolments, revenue, costs}], unbatched{revenue, costs}}], unlinked{revenue, costs}, placement: null, not_counted[{currency, amount, count}]}`.<br>• Income keys `enrolment_fees`, `other_fees`, `manual`; cost keys `trainer`, `marketing`, `operational`.<br>• A course = Σ its batches + `unbatched`; Σ courses + `unlinked` = `totals` (IR5).<br>**`422`:** a malformed month or period, or a month after the current one |
| `GET /management/revenue/it/payments` *(new)* | **Query:** `month`, `period`, exactly one of `program_id`, `batch_id`, `unlinked=true`; `limit` 1–100 (default 25), `offset` ≥ 0.<br>**`200`:** `{items[{id, entry_date, kind, amount, currency, installment_no, receipt_no, enrollment_code, course, batch}], total, limit, offset}`: counted IT payment lines, newest first. **No student names or emails** (Q-32 open).<br>**`422`:** not exactly one scope; "Choose a course from the list" / "Choose a batch from the list" for an unknown id; a bad month, period or paging value |

## 12DL. Abroad Education revenue dashboard (`mgmt-006`) — addendum, 2026-10-13

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-198` (MR1–MR12). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-mgmt-006-abroad-revenue-design.md`.
  No migration. Finance tier only (partner, accountant); `super_admin` `403` "Finance access required"; other roles `403` "Management
  access required"; signed out `401`. Read-only: no write, lock or audit row.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/revenue/abroad` *(new)* | Query `month` (`YYYY-MM`; default the current IST month). `422` as `GET /management/expenses` (12DI). `200` `{month, year_start, year_label, commission_visible, kinds[{key, label, month, year}] (service_fees, other_payments, university_commission — partner only — and manual, source order), revenue{month, year}, agent_generated{month, year}, breakdowns[{key (country/university/intake/agency), label, rows[{label, month, year}], unattributed{month, year}}], expenses[{label, month, year}], expense_total, shared_expenses, contribution, not_counted[{currency, kind, amount, count}]}`. Every figure is rupees from `ledger.lines()` (vertical `abroad`); each breakdown's rows + unattributed = revenue; contribution = revenue − expense_total. For the accountant university commission is absent from every figure and `commission_visible` is `false` (M3) |

## 12DM. Recruitment & Placement revenue dashboard (`mgmt-008`) — addendum, 2026-10-13

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-199` (RR1–RR12). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-mgmt-008-recruitment-revenue-design.md`.
  No migration. Read-only: no write, lock or audit row.
- **Access:** management roles (`require_management`). partner and accountant get money; `super_admin` gets the KPIs with
  `revenue_visible: false`, `revenue: null` and `revenue_per_placement: null`; other roles `403` "Management access required"; signed
  out `401`.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/revenue/recruitment` *(new)* | **Query:** `month`, `period` (as §12DK).<br>**`200`:** `{month, period, start, end, label, kpi_end, revenue_visible, revenue{total, kinds[{key, label, amount}]} \| null, kpis{recruiters, partner_companies, open_positions, candidates, interviews, selections, joining, placement_pct, revenue_per_placement}}`.<br>• Kind keys `recruiter_invoices` (always first), then `ledger:<category>`; `total` = Σ kinds.<br>• `candidates`/`interviews`/`selections`/`joining` = `GET /recruiter/performance?from=start&to=kpi_end` Total (P4/P6/P7/P8); `kpi_end` = min(end, today).<br>• `placement_pct` = joining ÷ candidates × 100 (1 dp), `revenue_per_placement` = total ÷ joining; `null` on a zero denominator.<br>**`422`:** a malformed, pre-2000 or future month, or a bad period |

## 12DN. Applications funnel dashboard (`mgmt-024`) — addendum, 2026-10-13

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-200` (MF1–MF18). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-mgmt-024-applications-funnel-design.md`.
  No migration. Operational tier only (partner, super_admin); accountant `403` "Operational access required"; other roles `403`
  "Management access required"; signed out `401`. Read-only: no write, lock or audit row.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/applications` *(new)* | Query (all optional, AND-combined): `date_from`, `date_to` (created, India days), `agency` (org id or `none`), `counselor` (user id or `none`), `country`, `university`, `course`, `staff` (ids), `intake` (≤ 80), `status` (≤ 50). `422` malformed id/date, "Unknown filter value", "The end date must be on or after the start date". `200` `{filters{…applied, string or null}, stages[{key, label, tracked, value}] (11, source order; `value` null for under_review, accepted, cas_coe), withdrawn, options{agencies, counselors, countries, universities, courses, intakes, staff, statuses: [{value, label}]}}`. Counts only; an unknown well-formed id gives zeros |

## 12DO. BDM dashboards (`mgmt-020`) — addendum, 2026-10-13

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-201` (BD1–BD12). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-mgmt-020-bdm-dashboards-design.md`.
  No migration. Operational tier only (partner, super_admin); accountant `403` "Operational access required"; other roles `403`
  "Management access required"; signed out `401`. Read-only: no write, lock or audit row.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/bdm` *(new)* | Query: `type` (`agent` default / `school` / `college`), `from`, `to` (inclusive IST dates, each defaulting to this IST month). `422` unknown type, malformed date, "The period must start on or before its end", "The period can be at most 366 days". `200` `{type, label, from, to, as_of, bdm_count, figures[{key, label, definition, basis: "period"\|"now", tracked, value}]}` — §16 order, 9 / 7 / 6 figures; `value` null when not tracked (College `internships`). Counts only |

  access required"; signed out `401`. Read-only: no write, lock or audit row.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/pnl` *(new)* | **Query:** `month` (`YYYY-MM`, IST, default this month; January 2000 to this month), `period` = `month` (default) \| `quarter` (the financial-year quarter — Apr, Jul, Oct, Jan — to the end of `month`) \| `year` (the financial year, 1 April, to the end of `month`).<br>**`200`:** `{month, period, start, end, label, commission_visible, rows[{key, label, revenue, expenses, contribution, margin}], total{revenue, expenses, contribution, margin}, not_counted[{currency, kind, amount, count}]}`.<br>• Six rows, always, in order: `abroad`, `it`, `recruitment`, `school`, `other`, `shared` ("Shared (company-wide, not allocated)", Q-04).<br>• Counted `ledger.lines()` only (approved manual entries, reversals negative, + rupee automatic lines). `total` = Σ rows; `contribution` = revenue − expenses (may be negative); `margin` = contribution ÷ revenue × 100 (1 dp), `null` when revenue ≤ 0.<br>• University commission only for partners (`commission_visible`); for the accountant it is excluded from every figure and from `not_counted`.<br>**`422`:** a malformed, pre-2000 or future month, or a bad period |

## 12DU. Marketing budget, spend & ROI (`mgmt-012`) — addendum, 2026-10-13

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-207` (MK1–MK14). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-mgmt-012-marketing-roi-design.md`. Migration
  `0156_marketing_budgets`. Signed out `401`. The backlog's `GET …/budgets` is folded into `GET /management/marketing` (each channel
  carries its `budget`).

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/marketing` *(new)* | **Query:** `month` (`YYYY-MM`, IST, default this month).<br>**`200`:** `{month, current_month, money_visible, channels[{channel, label, budget, spend, leads, qualified, customers, conversion, revenue, roi}], totals{…same without channel/label}, groups{social, website, school, college, agent, influencer: null, events}, campaigns[{id, name, channel, label, active, leads, qualified, customers}], budgets_updated_at, budgets_updated_by{id, full_name}}`.<br>• 13 channel rows, always, in `TEL_SOURCES` order. `budget` `null` = not set. `roi`/`conversion` `null` when spend/leads are 0.<br>• `super_admin`: `money_visible: false`; `budget`, `spend`, `revenue`, `roi` and `budgets_updated_*` are `null`.<br>**`403`:** not a management role.<br>**`422`:** a malformed or future month |
| `PUT /management/marketing/budgets` *(new)* | **Query:** `month` (required). **Body:** `{budgets: [{channel, amount}]}` (≤ 13) — replaces the month; channels left out lose their budget; `[]` clears it.<br>**`200`:** the month as `GET` returns it. One `mgmt.marketing_budget` audit row naming the changed channels (none when nothing changed).<br>**`403`:** "Finance access required" (`super_admin`) / "Management access required".<br>**`422`:** an unknown channel (`loc: body.budgets.i.channel`), a negative, >2-decimal or too large amount (`…amount`), a channel twice (`body.budgets`), a future month |

## 12DW. Direct Tie-ups & agent commission (dtu-001) — addendum, 2026-10-13

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-209` (DT1–DT18). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-dtu-001-direct-tieups-design.md`.
  Migration `0157_direct_tieups` (nullable columns only). Loader: `python -m app.direct_tieups.load [--dry-run]` (one transaction;
  `--dry-run` rolls back and prints counts; a second run changes nothing). Parser (dev machine with openpyxl):
  `python -m app.direct_tieups.parse <xlsx> <out_dir>` writes `seed/direct_tieups.json` and `seed/direct_tieups_review.csv`.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `PUT /partnership/universities/{id}/agent-commission` *(new)* | **Body (required):** `{percent}` (> 0, ≤ 100, 2 dp) **or** `{amount, currency}` (INR, USD, GBP, EUR, CAD, AUD, NZD, AED), plus optional `note` (≤ 300). Body validation (`422`) runs before the role check, so a non-editor with an invalid body gets `422`, not `403`; signed-out stays `401`. **`200`:** the university envelope with `agent_commission {percent, amount, currency, note}`. One `university.agent_commission` audit row with `before` / `after` values when the rate changed.<br>**`403`:** not `super_admin` / `overseas_admin` (overseas division). **`401`** signed out. **`404`** unknown. **`409`** the university is inactive (UM10). **`422`** missing or `null` body, both or neither of percent / amount, an amount without a currency |
| `DELETE /partnership/universities/{id}/agent-commission` *(new)* | Clears all four columns; **`200`** envelope with `agent_commission: null`. Same roles, `401` / `403` / `404` / `409`; audit row only when a rate existed. Replaces the spec's "`null` clears" (R5) |
| `GET /workflows/overseas/agent/crm/partner-universities` *(new)* | Agent **Master** and **staff** of an active agency. **Query:** `q`, `country` (uuid), `limit` ≤ 100 (default 20), `offset`. **`200`** `{items: [{id, slug, name, logo_url, city, campuses, country {id, name, iso2}, course_count}], total}` ordered by name (`q` matches name or city) — only `active` + `catalogue_visible`; `agent_commission` on each item **only for a Master**.<br>**`403`:** pending / suspended agency, non-agents. **`401`** signed out |
| `GET /workflows/overseas/agent/crm/partner-universities/{id}` *(new)* | **`200`** `{university: {…the row fields, website, overview, fee_structure, deadlines, scholarships, courses: [{id, title, level, category, duration, campus, fee_text, intake_text, tuition_fee, intake}]}}` (active courses); `agent_commission` only for a Master. **`404`** unknown, hidden or inactive. Same `401` / `403` |
| Portal section `partner-universities` | `GET /portal/overseas/agent/partner-universities` (generic `/{division}/{role}/{section}`) returns the header-only section payload (title "Partner Universities"); the page then reads the endpoints above |

- **Response field changes:** university master detail / list rows, public universities (`UniversityOut`), the counselor 360 view and
  course outputs gain `logo_url`, `campuses` (list, `[]` when none) and `fee_structure`; course outputs (master `course_out`, public
  detail and `/overseas-courses`, 360 view) gain `campus`, `fee_text`, `intake_text`. `UniversityCreate` / `UniversityUpdate` accept
  `logo_url`, `campuses`, `fee_structure`; `CourseIn` / `CourseUpdate` accept `campus`, `fee_text`, `intake_text`.
- **`agent_commission` key:** present on the master detail only for `super_admin` / `overseas_admin`, and on the two agent endpoints only
  for a Master; **absent** (not `null`) everywhere else. All `UniversityEnvelope` routes set `response_model_exclude_unset`.
- **Loader counts** (`universities_created`, `universities_updated`, `courses_created`, `courses_skipped`, `scholarships_created`,
  `conflicts`, `countries_published`, `country_needs_content`); hidden destination countries with content are published (DT18 / R12 / R13),
  Malta is reported `country_needs_content: MT` and stays hidden; GBS Malta is created internal (not public) until Malta has catalogue content (R15, `universities_internal`).

## 12DX. Homepage social proof (PUB-022) — addendum, 2026-10-14

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-210` (SP1–SP9). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-pub-022-social-proof-design.md`. Migration
  `0158_social_proof`: `testimonials` gains `published`, `featured`, `display_order`, `context`, `consent_confirmed`,
  `consent_by_user_id`, `consent_at`, `photo_key`, `photo_content_type` (existing rows unpublished); new single-row `site_rating_badge`.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /public/social-proof?division=it\|overseas\|all` *(new)* | No login. `{badge, testimonials}`: `badge` null unless published; up to 6 published + featured testimonials ordered by `display_order`, `created_at`. Bad division `422`. |
| `GET /public/testimonials[?division=]`, `GET /public/testimonials/{id}` *(changed)* | Published rows only; an unpublished id is `404`. Rows gain `context`, `photo_url`. |
| `GET /public/testimonials/{id}/photo` *(new)* | Streams the photo (`Cache-Control: no-cache`, `nosniff`; `photo_url` carries `?v=<digest>` that changes when the photo is replaced); `404` when unpublished, no photo or unknown. |
| `GET /public/home` *(changed)* | `stats` key removed (SP3); `testimonials` published only. |
| `GET /cms/manage/testimonials[?division=]` *(new)* | Editors (§2.135). Division admins always get their own division. |
| `POST /cms/testimonials` *(new)* | `201`. Body (`extra` forbidden): `division`, `person_name` (≤160), `headline` (≤200), `quote` (≤2000), `rating` 1–5, `context` (≤160), `featured`, `display_order`, `consent_confirmed`, `published`. Publish without consent `422`; other division `403`. |
| `PATCH /cms/testimonials/{id}` *(new)* | Same fields except `division`; only `context` may be null. Other division `404`. |
| `DELETE /cms/testimonials/{id}` *(new)* | `204`; removes the row and its photo (a missing file does not block). |
| `PUT /cms/testimonials/{id}/photo` *(new)* | Multipart `file`; JPEG/PNG ≤ 2 MB; `413` too big, `415` wrong type, `422` empty/unreadable; replaces and deletes any old photo. |
| `DELETE /cms/testimonials/{id}/photo` *(new)* | Removes the photo; `200` with the row. |
| `GET /cms/rating-badge`, `PUT /cms/rating-badge` *(new)* | `super_admin` only. Body: `source_name` (≤60), `rating` 0.0–5.0 (1 dp), `review_count` ≥ 0, `review_url` (https, ≤500, optional), `as_of`, `published`. |

## 12DY. Student funnel dashboard (`mgmt-016`) — addendum, 2026-10-14

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-211` (SF1–SF16). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-mgmt-016-student-funnel-design.md`.
  No migration. Operational tier only (partner, super_admin); accountant `403` "Operational access required"; other roles `403`
  "Management access required"; signed out `401`. Read-only: no write, lock or audit row.

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/funnel?from&to&by` | `200` `{from, to, as_of, paths: [{key, label}] (lead, it, abroad), stages: [{key, label, path, definition, value}] (14, §11 order: leads, telecalling, counseling, registration, training, it_application, certification, placement, joining, abroad_education, abroad_application, offer, visa, enrollment), dimensions: [{key, label, tracked, stages}] (9), breakdown: null \| {by, label, tracked, stages, rows: [{value, label, counts}], unknown}}`. Each stage counts what reached it in the period (not a cohort). With `by`, each applicable stage's rows + `unknown` equal its `value`; rows largest first, then by label; a label shared by two rows carries the value's first 8 characters. `by` ∈ country, course, agent, school, counselor, source, bdm, intake, branch (branch: `tracked: false`, no stages); anything else `422`. `from`/`to` inclusive IST dates, default this IST month; start after end or > 366 days → `422` |

## 12DZ. Partner capital accounts (`mgmt-015`) — addendum, 2026-10-14

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-212` (CP1–CP15). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-mgmt-015-partner-capital-accounts-design.md`. Migration
  `0159_partner_capital`. **Partner-only** on every route: `accountant` / `super_admin` `403` "Only partners can open this", any other
  role `403` "Management access required", signed out `401` (RBAC §2.137).

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/capital` *(new)* | **Query:** `month` (YYYY-MM, January 2000 – this IST month, default this month), `period` = `month` / `quarter` / `year` (default `year`, financial year to date), `offset` 0–10 000. **`200`** `{as_of, month, period, label, net_profit, accounts: [{partner {id, full_name, active}, invested, additional, withdrawals, balance, returns, share_pct, commitment, outstanding, profit_share}], totals {same figures; share_pct = the shares in force summed}, partners: [{id, full_name}] (active, the form choices), entries {items: [{id, partner, entry_date, kind, amount, note, created_by, created_at}], total, limit: 25, offset}, shares: [{id, partner, effective_from, share_pct, commitment, in_force, updated_by, updated_at}]}`. Figures as of `as_of`; `profit_share` over the period (CP9), `null` when no share was in force. **`422`** month out of range / malformed, unknown period |
| `POST /management/capital/entries` *(new)* | **Body:** `{partner_id, entry_date, kind (initial / additional / withdrawal / profit_distribution), amount, note?}`. **`201`** the entry `{id, partner, entry_date, kind, amount, note, created_by, created_at}` + one `mgmt.capital.create` audit row with values. A withdrawal over the balance is accepted (CP11). **`422`** not an active partner ("Choose an active partner"), a future date or one before 2000, amount ≤ 0 / > 2 dp / too large, note > 500 or with a line break, unknown kind or field |
| `PUT /management/capital/shares` *(new)* | **Body:** `{effective_from: "YYYY-MM", shares: [{partner_id, share_pct (0–100, 2 dp), commitment? (≥ 0)}]}` (1–50 rows, each partner once). Sets each sent partner's row for that month (insert, or replace that month's row); others unchanged. **`200`** `{items: [...the share history as on GET]}`. Audit `mgmt.capital.create` / `.update` (changed fields, before → after); nothing for an unchanged row. **`409`** lost a concurrent first save of the same month. **`422`** month outside January 2000 – 12 months ahead, an inactive or non-partner, a repeated partner, a bad share or commitment |

## 12EA. Next month plan + target vs actual (mgmt-026) — addendum, 2026-10-14

- **Basis:** `DEC-SCOPE-213` (PN1–PN16). Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-mgmt-026-next-month-plan-design.md`. Migration
  `0160_management_plans`: `management_plans` (unique `month`, status) and `management_plan_items` (CHECKs: KPI ⇔ target, target > 0,
  no `done` on a KPI item). Access per RBAC §2.138. `month` is `YYYY-MM` (malformed `422`, unplanned `404` "Plan not found").

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/plans` | `{items[{id, month, status, label, item_count, kpi_count, done_count, created_by, updated_at}]}`, newest month first; counts over the items the caller may see |
| `GET /management/plans/options` | `{kpis[{key, label, unit: count\|inr, definition}], verticals[{key, label}], users, current_month, max_month}`; `revenue` for partners only |
| `POST /management/plans` | `{month, status?}` → `201` plan detail. Outside this month … +12: field `422` "Choose this month or one of the next 12"; duplicate `409` "This month already has a plan" |
| `GET /management/plans/{month}` | Plan detail + `items[{id, vertical, text, kpi, target, done, task_count, updated_at}]` in vertical then append order |
| `PATCH /management/plans/{month}` | `{status}` → `200`; same status records nothing |
| `GET /management/plans/{month}/progress` | `{month, label, state, start, end, rows[{item_id, vertical, text, kpi, kpi_label, unit, target, actual, achievement}], free_text{total, done}}` |
| `POST /management/plans/{month}/items` | `{vertical, text, kpi?, target?, done?}` → `201`. Unknown KPI / target without KPI / KPI without target / fractional count / done on a KPI item: field `422`; non-partner revenue `403`; closed `409` |
| `PATCH /management/plans/{month}/items/{id}` | Partial; `kpi` and `target` cleared together; no change records nothing. Another month's or a hidden (revenue) item `404` "Plan item not found"; closed `409` |
| `DELETE /management/plans/{month}/items/{id}` | `204`; tasks made from it stay; closed `409` |
| `POST /management/plans/{month}/items/{id}/tasks` | `{owner_user_id, priority?, deadline}` → `201` mgmt-028 task (`source: "plan"`); mgmt-028's owner/deadline `422`s; closed `409` |

  `management_discussions.meeting_id`. Access per RBAC §2.141. Unknown meeting `404` "Meeting not found".

| Method/Path | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|
| `GET /management/meetings` | `?date_from&date_to` (IST days, both or neither, ≤ 62 days, else `422`), `kind`, `status`. Without dates: today. `{items[{id, title, kind, starts_at, mode, location, meeting_url, status, participant_count}], date_from, date_to, today}` by start time |
| `GET /management/meetings/options` | `{users}` — active staff (the same shape as discussions' options) |
| `POST /management/meetings` | `{title, kind, starts_at, mode?, location?, meeting_url?, agenda?, participant_ids?}` → `201` detail; `status` is refused (`422`). Field `422`s: non-`http(s)` link, inactive / non-staff participant, start outside ±366 days |
| `GET /management/meetings/{id}` | Detail: the row + `agenda, minutes, minutes_recorded_at, participants, decisions[{id, decision, responsible, deadline, status, tasks[]}], tasks[{id, title, owner, priority, deadline, status}], progress{done, total}, created_by, updated_by, created_at, updated_at` |
| `PATCH /management/meetings/{id}` | Partial; `status` accepts only `cancelled`. Cancelled meeting `409`; a completed meeting's time change or cancel `409`; no change records nothing |
| `POST /management/meetings/{id}/minutes` | `{minutes}` (2–20 000) → `200` detail; before the start or cancelled `409`; first record completes the meeting |
| `POST /management/meetings/{id}/decisions` | `{decision, responsible_user_id?, deadline?, topic?}` → `201` detail; a mgmt-027 discussion with `meeting_id`. No minutes / cancelled `409`; mgmt-027's field `422`s |
| `POST /management/meetings/{id}/tasks` | `{title, owner_user_id, priority?, deadline, decision_id?}` → `201` detail; a mgmt-028 task (`source` meeting, or decision). `decision_id` not of this meeting `422`; no minutes / cancelled `409`; mgmt-028's field `422`s |
| `GET /me/management-meetings` | Any signed-in user: their own meetings from the last 7 IST days on, `{items[{id, title, kind, starts_at, mode, location, meeting_url, agenda, status}]}` — never minutes or people |
| `GET /management/discussions*` (additive) | Every discussion row gains `meeting_id` (null unless recorded from a meeting's minutes) |

## 12EK. Agent signup KYC documents (AGN-024) — addendum, 2026-10-17

`DEC-SCOPE-224` (K1–K11), migration `0164_agent_org_kyc`. Document types: `pan`, `aadhaar`, `business_license`. Statuses: `pending | verified | rejected`.

### `POST /api/v1/auth/register` (changed)

- **JSON** (`application/json`): unchanged for students. A JSON agent signup returns `422` "PAN card, Aadhaar card and business licence are required" (agents now use multipart).
- **Multipart** (`multipart/form-data`) or `application/x-www-form-urlencoded`: the same fields as the JSON body plus, for `account_type=agent`, all six of: `pan_number` + `pan_file`, `aadhaar_last4` + `aadhaar_file`, `license_number` + `license_file`.
- Any other `Content-Type` (including none, `text/plain`, etc.) returns `415` "Unsupported content type" before the body is read. JSON means `application/json` or a `*/*+json` type.
- Checks that run **before the body is parsed**: `403` "Cross-site registration is not allowed" when an `Origin` header (else `Referer`) is present and is not the origin of `settings.frontend_url`; `411` without a valid `Content-Length`; `413` above `MAX_REGISTRATION_BYTES` (3 × 5 MB + 1 MB).
- Field rules: PAN `^[A-Z]{5}[0-9]{4}[A-Z]$` (upper-cased); Aadhaar last 4 `^[0-9]{4}$`; licence `^[A-Za-z0-9/\-. ]{3,64}$`. Files: PDF, JPG or PNG, at most 5 MB each, type from the bytes, image metadata stripped.
- Errors: `422` for number/field errors and empty or unreadable files; `413` (too large), `415` (wrong type) and `422` for files, with the message prefixed by the document label (for example "Aadhaar card: the file must be at most 5 MB"); `409` duplicate email, including a concurrent signup that loses the race on commit (`IntegrityError`).
- `422` bodies never echo the submitted input (password, PAN, licence number).
- **Agent signup throttle (K11):** at most 5 agent signups per client IP per rolling hour; the 6th returns `429` "Too many registrations from this network -- try again later" with `Retry-After` (seconds). Checked after field and number validation and before any file is read or stored. Client IP = the leftmost valid `X-Forwarded-For` entry, else the socket peer. The `auth.register` audit row of an agent signup records `ip` (the IP only). Student signups are not throttled.
- `aadhaar_last4` is the only Aadhaar number stored; the uploaded `aadhaar_file` is kept as uploaded and may show the full number (K9). It is reachable only through the admin streaming route.
- Atomic: files are stored only after every check passes and are discarded if the commit fails. The user, org (`kyc_required=true`), Master, three `pending` document rows and the role assignment are created in one transaction. Audit `auth.register` gains `kyc_documents: 3`; document numbers are never written to audit metadata.
- The response is unchanged. OpenAPI no longer shows a request body for this route (the body is read manually); this section is the contract.

### Admin routes (`/overseas-admin`, roles `overseas_admin` and `super_admin`; the role gate runs before any lookup)

| Route | Request | Response |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /overseas-admin/agent-orgs/{org_id}/kyc-documents` | none | `{kyc_required, org_status, items: [{id, doc_type, label, status, rejection_reason, original_filename, content_type, file_size, updated_at, doc_number, reviewed_at}]}`. Full numbers (admin only), `Cache-Control: no-store`. Not audited. `404` unknown org. |
| `GET /overseas-admin/agent-orgs/{org_id}/kyc-documents/{doc_id}/file` | none | The file bytes, streamed through `storage.read_bytes`: `Content-Type` = stored type, `Content-Disposition: inline; filename="<doc_type>.<ext>"` (never the client file name), `Cache-Control: no-store`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`. Audit `agent_kyc.view`. `404` if the document is not in that org. |
| `POST /overseas-admin/agent-orgs/{org_id}/kyc-documents/{doc_id}/verify` | none | the admin item shape. Audit `agent_kyc.verify`. `409` if the org is not `pending`. |
| `POST /overseas-admin/agent-orgs/{org_id}/kyc-documents/{doc_id}/reject` | `{reason}` (3–500 characters) | the admin item shape. Audit `agent_kyc.reject`; queues the rejection notice (below). `409` if the org is not `pending`; `422` without a valid reason. |

Verify and reject lock the org row (serialising with approve/reject) and may change a decision (verified ↔ rejected) while the org is `pending`. Audit metadata is `{org_id, doc_type}` only. Other roles get `403`; anonymous `401`.

### Agent routes (`/agent/registration`, a Master of the agency; these deliberately skip `agent_denial_reason`)

| Route | Request | Response |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /agent/registration` | none | `{org: {name, status, kyc_required}, documents: [{id, doc_type, label, status, rejection_reason, original_filename, content_type, file_size, updated_at, doc_number_display}]}`. Works for `pending`, `rejected` and `active` orgs. `doc_number_display`: PAN `ABCDE****F`, Aadhaar `XXXX XXXX 1234`, licence in full. `no-store`. |
| `PUT /agent/registration/documents/{doc_type}/file` | multipart: `file`, optional `doc_number` (corrected number) | the agent item shape. Replaces a **rejected** document of a **pending** org only (rejection of the org is final, K10: a rejected agency registers again with a new email); status returns to `pending`, reason and reviewer cleared, audit `agent_kyc.reupload`. `409` if the org is not `pending` or the document is not `rejected`; `404` if the agency has no such document; `413`/`415`/`422` file errors; `429` + `Retry-After` after 20 re-uploads per agency in a rolling 24 h (counted from audit rows, the AGN-009 pattern). The old file is deleted after the commit. |

A user who is not a `master` of an active membership gets `403`.

### Agent org list (additive)

`GET /overseas-admin/agent-orgs` items gain `kyc_required: boolean` and `kyc_summary: {verified, rejected, pending}` (counts of documents).

### Approve gate

`POST /overseas-admin/agent-orgs/{org_id}/approve` and the legacy per-agent `POST /overseas-admin/agents/{agent_id}/approve` both reach `agent_orgs.set_org_status`. For an org with `kyc_required=true`, pending → active and rejected → active return `409` "All verification documents must be verified before approval" unless all three documents exist and are `verified`. Reinstating a suspended org is not gated, rejecting is never gated, and orgs with `kyc_required=false` approve as before.

### Notices (K6)

Rejection of a document, and approval of a `kyc_required` org, create a `Notification` and queue an email through `queue_deliveries` (the ENH-014 outbox) for the org's active Masters, in the same transaction as the action. Delivery happens after commit; a delivery failure never affects the action. Rejection carries the reason and a link to `/overseas/agent/registration-status`.

### Generic file paths

`/files/download` refuses any key starting `agent-kyc/` (`404`), and the `/local-files` static mount does the same. KYC files are served only by the admin streaming route above.

## 12EM. Counselor walk-in students (csc-002) — addendum, 2026-10-19

`DEC-SCOPE-227`. No migration (csc-001's `overseas_student_counselors`). RBAC §2.149.

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /api/v1/counselor/students` | Authenticated | `counselor` in `overseas` only; everyone else `403 "Only an overseas counselor can add walk-in students"` | Body `{full_name (1–160, not blank), email (≤255, name@domain.tld, stored lower-case), phone? (≤40)}`, `extra="forbid"` (any other field → `422`). Creates an `overseas_student` (division `overseas`, unusable password, active, unverified, a `student_code`, `profile.registration_source="counselor_walk_in"`), its role assignment, the csc-001 link to the caller and an ENH-003 welcome token; commits; then emails the set-password link. `201 {"student": {id, full_name, email, phone, student_code, setup: "pending_setup"}, "email_status", "expires_at"}` — the raw token is **never** returned (any environment). Existing email (case-insensitive) → `409 "Email already exists"`, audited. More than 20 creations + refused duplicates per counselor per rolling 24 h → `429` + `Retry-After`. A failed/unconfigured send never rolls back the account (`email_status` says so). |
| `GET /api/v1/counselor/students?limit=20&offset=0` | Authenticated | as above | The caller's linked students, newest link first: `{"items": [{id, full_name, email, phone, student_code, setup}], "total", "limit", "offset"}`; `setup` is `pending_setup`, `link_expired` or `null` (has a password). `limit` 1–50, `offset` ≥ 0 (else `422`). |

## 12EN. Counselor walk-in students: resend and fix details (csc-003) — addendum, 2026-10-19

`DEC-SCOPE-228`. No migration. RBAC §2.150. Both routes: `counselor` in `overseas` only (else `403`); the target must be a student
**linked to the caller** (else `404 "Student not found"` — another counselor's student, an unknown id and a non-student read the same);
it must still be pending (`409 "This student has already set a password"`) and not deactivated (`409 "This account is deactivated"`).
The counselor's mail budget (§12EM) now counts creations, refused duplicates, resends, email changes and refused email changes: 20 per
rolling 24 h → `429` + `Retry-After` ("You have sent 20 account emails in the last 24 hours. Try again later."). The raw token is never
returned.

| Method/Path | Behaviour |
|---|---|
| `POST /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/welcome-links` | ENH-003 Re-send rules: the first resend after creation is always allowed, another within 60 s of the newest link → `429 "A link was just sent; wait N seconds before re-sending"` + `Retry-After`. Supersedes the open link, issues a new one, commits, emails it. `201 {"student": {…, "setup": "pending_setup"}, "email_status", "expires_at"}`. |
| `PATCH /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}` | Body `{full_name?, email?, phone?}`, `extra="forbid"`; omitted = unchanged; `full_name`/`email` null → `422`; `phone` null or blank clears it; create's validation. Only changed fields are written (email compared lower-case). An email change: another account's address → `409 "Email already exists"` (audited, counted); otherwise every open link is revoked and a new one is emailed to the new address after the commit — the response adds `email_status`, `expires_at`. `200 {"student": {…}}`; an empty or no-op body is `200` with the student unchanged. |

| `GET /api/v1/counselor/students/{student_id}/notes?limit=20&offset=0` | Authenticated | overseas `counselor` (csc-001 scope: own applications' + linked students); `overseas_admin` (overseas) any `overseas_student`. Everyone else `403 "Only an overseas counselor or the Overseas Admin can see student notes"` | Out of scope / unknown / not an overseas student → `404 "Student not found"`. `limit` 1–50. `200 {"student": {id, full_name, student_code}, "applications": [{id, label}], "items": [note], "total", "limit", "offset"}`, newest first; every author's notes (the history survives a reassignment). `applications` = what the caller may attach: the counselor's own applications for this student, or all of them for the admin; `label` "University · reference". |
| `POST /api/v1/counselor/students/{student_id}/notes` | Authenticated | as GET | Body `{"body": str (trimmed, 1–4000), "application_id": uuid \| null}`; any other field `422`; blank `422`. An application the caller may not attach → `422 "Choose one of this student's applications"`. `201 note` = `{id, body, author_name, author_role, application: {id, label} \| null, created_at}`. Append-only: no edit or delete route. |
| `GET /api/v1/portal/overseas/{counselor\|admin}/students` | Authenticated | `counselor`, `overseas_admin` | Adds `notes` (the notes page href; null for a student with no login) with a `student_notes` column. Other sections unchanged. |
| `GET /api/v1/portal/overseas/admin/users` (Overseas Admin) | Authenticated | `overseas_admin` | Adds `notes` on overseas student rows (null on staff rows) with a `student_notes` column. |

No student-, agent- or university-facing route returns notes.

## 12EU. Lead notes (ccrm-002) — addendum, 2026-10-22

`DEC-SCOPE-242`. Migration `0167_lead_notes` (table `lead_notes`). RBAC §2.157. Additive: new routes and one new timeline `kind`.

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /api/v1/telecaller/leads/{id}/notes` · `POST` same | Authenticated | `telecaller`, `telecaller_manager`, `super_admin` (tel-004 T23 scope) | Lead outside the scope → `404 "Lead not found"`; other roles `403`. The lead's telecaller writes after the handover too (N1). |
| `GET /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/notes` · `POST` same | Authenticated | `counselor` (owner + division) | Another counselor's lead or another division → `404`; other roles `403`. |
| `GET /api/v1/admin/leads/{id}/notes` · `POST` same | Authenticated | `super_admin` (any), `overseas_admin` / `it_admin` (own division) | Unknown lead `404`; other division `403` (the admin timeline's answers); other roles `403`. |

`POST` body `{"body": string}` — trimmed, 1–4000 characters, extra keys refused (`422`). `201 {"id", "at", "author": {"id", "full_name"},
"body"}`. Append-only: no edit or delete (N2); a closed or returned lead takes notes. Audit `lead_note.create` (entity `enquiry`, metadata
`{note_id}` — never the body). `GET` → `{items, total, limit, offset}`, newest first, `limit` ≤ 100. Every lead timeline route
(`/telecaller|counselor|admin/leads/{id}/timeline`) gains `kind: "note"` (actor = author, `reason` = the first 200 characters).

## 12EV. Counselor lead actions (ccrm-003) — addendum, 2026-10-22

`DEC-SCOPE-243`. Migration `0169_lead_next_action` (`enquiries.next_action`, `next_action_due`). RBAC §2.158. Additive routes; one behaviour
change on `PATCH /admin/leads/{id}` (`owner_id` validated).

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/stage` | Authenticated | `counselor` (owner + division) | Body `{to_stage, reason?, next_action?, next_action_due?}`, extra keys `422`. Targets: `follow_up`, `counselling_scheduled`, `counselling_completed`, `qualified`, `not_interested`, `not_eligible`, `lost` — others `422` on `to_stage`; past Application/Enrollment `422`; closed lead `403` (no counselor reopen); closing needs `reason` (`422`) and cancels open follow-ups and appointments; an open target with no next action / due (sent or stored) → `422` on that field. `200 {id, status, status_label, stage_changed_at, next_action, next_action_due}`. |
| `PUT /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/next-action` | Authenticated | `counselor` | Body `{next_action: 1–200 trimmed, next_action_due: date}` both required; a changed date before today (IST) `422`; closed lead `409`. `200` the counselor lead detail. Audit `lead.next_action` `{fields}`. |
| `GET /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/follow-ups` · `POST` same | Authenticated | `counselor` | The tel-011 lead page and create (same bodies, `201`, rules: future due, 12 months, cap 20, closed `409`). `move_to_follow_up` applies the counselor stage rules. |
| `PATCH /api/v1/counselor/follow-ups/{id}` · `POST …/complete` · `POST …/cancel` | Authenticated | `counselor` | The tel-011 reschedule / complete / cancel on a follow-up of the counselor's lead; another counselor's `404`; done / cancelled `409`. |
| `PATCH /api/v1/admin/leads/{id}` | (unchanged) | (unchanged) | `owner_id` must be an active `counselor` of the lead's division, else `422` on `owner_id`; `null` un-assigns. |

The counselor lead detail (`GET /counselor/leads/{id}`) adds `next_action`, `next_action_due`, `next_action_overdue` and
`permissions.stage` / `permissions.follow_up`. Telecaller-side follow-up lists, the My Leads `follow_up` filter, tel-020 alerts and tel-021 /
tel-024 / tel-025 counts leave out follow-ups created by a counselor; the telecaller's per-lead list shows them with `can_change: false`.
The return (`POST /counselor/leads/{id}/return`) also cancels open follow-ups (`"Returned to telecaller"`) and clears the next action.

**Addendum, 2026-10-06 (`bdm-011`, `DEC-SCOPE-092`): trip ↔ appointment linking, itinerary, productivity, travel report.**

Sources: design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-bdm-011-trip-appointments-design.md` §4; migration `0088_bdm_appointment_trip`.
Every change is **additive**: new optional request fields, new response fields and new GET routes; no existing field changes meaning.

| Method + path | Change | Refusals |
|---|---|---|
| `POST /bdm/appointments` | optional `trip_id` | 404 "Trip not found" (not the caller's); 409 "This trip is cancelled and can't take appointments" (or completed); 422 "This appointment is on 18 Sep 2026, outside TRV-000123 (20 Sep 2026 – 21 Sep 2026)" |
| `PATCH /bdm/appointments/{id}` | optional `trip_id`; `null` unlinks; omitted = unchanged | as above; the existing owner (403) and open (409) rules apply first |
| `POST /bdm/appointments/{id}/reschedule` | a linked appointment moved outside its trip's dates is unlinked (audit `bdm_appointment.trip_unlinked`) | unchanged |
| every appointment envelope | `appointment.trip`: `{id, code, from_place, to_place, travel_date, return_date, approval_status, travel_status}` or `null` | — |
| `GET /bdm/trips` | `linkable=true`: the caller's planned / in-progress trips whose return date is today (IST) or later | unchanged |
| `GET /bdm/trips/{id}`, `GET /bdm/manager/trips/{id}` and every trip write | `itinerary[]` (`{id, code, starts_at, duration_minutes, appointment_type, status, organization: {id, name}, expected_leads, expected_revenue}`, by start) and `metrics` (`meetings_planned, meetings_completed, estimated_cost, actual_cost, cost_per_completed_meeting, expected_leads, expected_revenue, actual_leads, actual_revenue`; null = nothing to compute from; `actual_revenue` always null = not tracked) | — |
| `PATCH /bdm/trips/{id}` | a date change that would leave an open linked appointment outside → **422** "N linked appointment(s) fall(s) outside the new dates — unlink or reschedule … first"; closed ones are unlinked (named in the `bdm.trip_update` audit) | — |
| `GET /bdm/trips/{id}/report` (new) | the trip (`BdmTripOut`) once completed | 409 "The travel report is available once the trip is completed"; 404 out of scope |
| `GET /bdm/manager/trips/{id}/report` (new) | the same, team scope | 409 / 404 as above |
| `POST /admin/bdms/{id}/deactivate`, `/handover` (bdm-025) | a moved appointment's `trip_id` is cleared (trips never move) | unchanged |

Deep-link targets for the travel reminder (bdm-012): `/bdm/travel/{id}#trip-appointments`, `#trip-costs`, `#trip-remarks`.

`DEC-SCOPE-240`. Migration `0168_application_sub_status`. RBAC §2.154. Codes: `app/application_sub_status.py` (SS1).

Unknown code → `422 "Choose a sub-status from the list"`; a code of an earlier stage → `422 "That sub-status belongs to an earlier stage -- an application only moves forward"`. A later stage's code advances the stage. Withdrawn → `409`; archived agency record → `409`; counselor to `enrolled` on an agency application → `403 "Only the agency's Master confirms enrollment"`. Same value → `200 {"changed": false}`, nothing written. Otherwise one history row (`from/to_status`, `from/to_sub_status`, notes, author), audit `overseas.application.sub_status`, student notified; a stage change runs the AGT-003 commission trigger and AGN-017's status notice, a sub-status-only change tells the agency ("Application sub-status changed"). `200 {"id", "status", "sub_status", "sub_status_label", "changed"}`. |
| `POST /api/v1/workflows/overseas/agent/crm/applications/{id}/sub-status` | Authenticated | `agent` (agency gate; out of scope `404`) | Same body and rules; `enrolled` → `403` (A4). Archived/withdrawn → `409`. The EduSphere counselor is told (CC5). `200 {"application": detail}`. |
| `GET /overseas/applications`, `GET …/{id}/status`, agency list/detail | Authenticated | unchanged | Add `sub_status` (agency also `sub_status_label`); history rows add `from_sub_status`/`to_sub_status`. |
| `GET /api/v1/portal/overseas/{counselor,admin}/{students,applications}`, `…/student/applications` | Authenticated | unchanged | A `Sub-status` column (`sub_status_label`); counselor/admin rows also carry `sub_status`. Other sections unchanged. |


## 12EW. Application exception outcomes (ccrm-015) — addendum, 2026-10-23

`DEC-SCOPE-244`. Migration `0170_application_outcomes`. RBAC §2.159. Values: `app/application_outcome.py`.

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /api/v1/workflows/overseas/applications/{id}/outcome` | Authenticated | `counselor` (own applications, direct and agency), `overseas_admin` | Body `{"outcome": rejected\|withdrawn\|deferred\|on_hold\|offer_declined, "outcome_date"?, "reason"?, "requested_by"?, "next_steps"?, "document_id"?, "new_intake"?, "application_deadline"?, "offer_deadline"?, "review_date"?, "hold_owner_id"?, "another_offer_preferred"?}` (extra keys `422`). Required: rejected → reason; withdrawn → reason + requested_by (student/counselor/agent/university); deferred → new_intake; on_hold → reason + review_date; offer_declined → reason; else `422`. Future `outcome_date`, past `review_date` → `422`; offer declined before an offer → `422`; letter not of this application → `422`; hold owner not an active overseas counselor/admin → `422`. Closed (terminal outcome or withdrawn) → `409`; enrolled → `409`; archived agency record → `409`. Writes one `application_outcomes` row, a history row (`Outcome: <label> -- <reason>`), audit `overseas.application.outcome`; withdrawn also sets `status='withdrawn'`; deferred updates `intake` and the given deadlines. Student and (agency application) agency notified. `200 {"id", "status", "sub_status", "outcome", "detail"}`. |
| `POST /api/v1/workflows/overseas/applications/{id}/outcome/reopen` | Authenticated | `counselor` (own; on hold / deferred only), `overseas_admin` | Body `{"reason"}` (blank → `422`). No outcome → `409`; a terminal outcome by a counselor → `403`. Clears the outcome (a `cleared` event + history row); a withdrawn application returns to the stage and sub-status it was withdrawn from. Audit `overseas.application.outcome_reopen`. Same response. |
| `POST /api/v1/workflows/overseas/agent/crm/applications/{id}/status` | unchanged | `agent` | Adds optional `requested_by` (only with `to_status: withdrawn`, else `422`; default `agent`). A withdraw also sets the outcome and tells the EduSphere counselor ("Application outcome changed"). On hold → forward moves `409`, withdraw allowed. |
| `/advance`, `/sub-status`, `PATCH /overseas/applications/{id}` (status), counselor assign, agency sub-status / offer / enrollment | unchanged | unchanged | A terminal outcome → `409 "This application is <outcome> -- only the Overseas Admin can reopen it"` (withdrawn keeps `"This application is withdrawn"`); on hold → `409` on stage moves. |
| `GET …/{id}/status`, agency detail | unchanged | unchanged | Add `outcome` (staff: reason, requested_by, review_date, hold_owner, document_id, another_offer_preferred; student: outcome, label, date, next steps, new intake). Agency `read_only_reason` adds rejected / offer_declined / visa_refused. |
| `GET /api/v1/portal/overseas/{counselor,admin}/{students,applications}`, `…/student/applications` | unchanged | unchanged | An `Outcome` column (staff `outcome_summary`, student `outcome_label`); admin rows add `reopen` (`reopen_outcome` column) on closed rows. Create routes (direct, agency, school bridge) ignore terminal applications in the duplicate check (O3). |

## 12EX. Offer records with history (ccrm-023) — addendum, 2026-10-23

`DEC-SCOPE-245`. Migration `0171_application_offers`. RBAC §2.160. Code: `app/services/application_offers.py`, `app/api/application_offers.py`.

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /api/v1/workflows/overseas/applications/{id}/offers` | Authenticated | `counselor` (own), `overseas_admin`, `overseas_student` (own), `agent` (scope) | `200 {"offers": [...newest first], "offer_letters": [...]}`. Each offer: `id, offer_type, offer_type_label, issue_date, offer_deadline, conditions, currency, tuition_amount, scholarship_amount, deposit_amount` (strings, 2 dp), `deposit_deadline, document {id, name}\|null, status, status_reason, recorded_by, recorded_at, closed_by, closed_at`. Never `file_url`. `offer_letters` (this application's "Offer letter" documents, max 20) go to counselor/admin only, otherwise `[]`. Out of scope `403`. |
| `POST …/{id}/offer-letters` (multipart `file`) | Authenticated | `counselor` (own), `overseas_admin` | PDF or image, sniffed (`415` otherwise; `413` over the limit). Creates a pending "Offer letter" document attached to the application, owned like it. No student or agency owner → `422`. Withdrawn / archived agency record → `409`. Audit `document.upload`. `201 {"document": {id, name}}`. |
| `POST …/{id}/offers` | Authenticated | `counselor` (own), `overseas_admin` | Body `{offer_type: conditional\|unconditional\|pathway\|other, issue_date (not future), offer_deadline?, conditions? (required for conditional, refused for unconditional, ≤2000), currency? (3 letters, upper-cased; required iff an amount), tuition_amount?, scholarship_amount?, deposit_amount? (≥0, 2 dp), deposit_deadline?, offer_document_id}` (extra keys `422`; deadlines ≥ issue date). The letter must be this application's "Offer letter" (`422 "Choose an offer letter uploaded for this application"`). Withdrawn / archived → `409`. Identical to the active offer → `200 {"changed": false}`, nothing written. Otherwise the active offer becomes `superseded` and the new one is mirrored. A pre-offer stage moves to `offer`; at `offer` the sub-status is set (OF3). One history row, audit `overseas.application.offer`, student notified "Offer recorded", the agency told (CC5). `201 {"id", "status", "sub_status", "sub_status_label", "offer", "changed": true}`. |
| `POST …/{id}/offers/{offer_id}/withdraw` | Authenticated | `counselor` (own), `overseas_admin` | Body `{status: withdrawn_by_university\|expired, reason (1–500)}`. An offer of another application → `404`; not active → `409`. Clears the mirror and an offer sub-status; the stage stays. History row, audit `overseas.application.offer_close`, student and agency notified. `200` (same shape). |
| `PUT /api/v1/workflows/overseas/agent/crm/applications/{id}/offer` | unchanged | unchanged | Same contract. A real change now writes an `application_offers` row (superseding) and sets the OF3 sub-status; history carries from/to sub-status; the EduSphere counselor is told "Offer recorded". |
| `PATCH /api/v1/workflows/overseas/agent/crm/applications/{id}` | unchanged | unchanged | An `offer_deadline` change while an offer is active writes a superseding row with the new deadline. |
| `GET /api/v1/portal/overseas/student/{dashboard,offer-letters}`, `…/university-rep/offer-letters` | unchanged | unchanged | nehn-001: an application counts as an offer by AGN-010's `counts_as_offer` (stage `offer` or later, legacy `offer_received`/`accepted`, or a recorded offer). The student list adds `offer_type` and `offer_deadline` columns. |

Matches the caller's own leads (name, email, lead code, phone digits), visible students (name, email, student code, phone digits) and own applications (APP- code, student name / email / phone, account or agency record). `200 {"q", "leads", "students", "applications"}`; each `{"items": [{"id", "code", "label", "detail", "href"}] (≤10), "more"}`; an application's `href` opens the Applications list filtered to its university and intake. 30 a minute per counselor → `429` + `Retry-After`. Unknown / malformed / repeated → `422 "Unknown filter value"`; sub-status of another stage → `422 "Sub-status does not belong to that stage"`; `from > to` → `422 "The start date must be on or before the end date"`; any of them on another section → `422 "Filter not available"`. `filters` adds the applied values and `countries`, `universities`, `intakes` (from the caller's own applications), `stages`, `sub_statuses` (`{value, label, stage}`). |
