## 12FB. Structured overseas student profile (ccrm-007) — addendum, 2026-10-24

`DEC-SCOPE-249`. Migration `0174_student_profiles`. RBAC §2.164. Code: `app/services/student_profiles.py`, `app/api/student_profiles.py`,
agency read in `app/api/agent_students.py`.

Profile object: `{exists, date_of_birth, nationality, city, state, passport_number, passport_expiry, work_experience_months,
work_experience_details, preferred_countries: [{id, name}], preferred_course, preferred_level, preferred_intake, study_mode, budget_min,
budget_max, budget_currency, qualifications: [{level, qualification_name, institution, board_university, year_completed, score_type, score,
backlogs}], english_tests: [{test_type, status, overall_score, test_date, valid_until, remarks, expired}], updated_at, updated_by_name}`.
Decimals are strings as stored ("8.20"). An unsaved profile reads as empty with `exists: false`.

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/profile` | Authenticated | overseas `counselor` (csc-001 scope), `overseas_admin` (any overseas student) | `200 {student: {id, full_name, student_code}, profile}`. Unknown, out of scope or not an `overseas_student` `404`; any other role (incl. an IT counselor) `403`. |
| `PUT /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/profile` | Authenticated | same | Full replace. Body: every profile field (omitted = null / empty list), `preferred_country_ids` (≤5, distinct, must exist), `qualifications` (≤10 × {level (class_10\|class_12\|diploma\|bachelor\|master\|phd\|other), …}), `english_tests` (≤5 × {test_type (IELTS\|TOEFL\|PTE\|Duolingo\|Other), status? (not_taken\|booked\|taken), …}); extra keys `422`. Rules: DOB 1900..today; passport `[A-Z0-9]{5,20}` (upper-cased); percentage 0–100, CGPA 0–10, a score needs its type; year 1950..now+6; backlogs 0–99; experience 0–600 months; English score in the test's range (IELTS 0–9, TOEFL 0–120, PTE 10–90, Duolingo 10–160, Other 0–999); a taken test's date not in the future; `valid_until ≥ test_date`; budget needs a currency and min ≤ max. Unknown country `422 "Choose a listed country"`. Student row locked; audit `overseas_student_profile.update` `{fields, role}`. `200` as GET. |
| `GET /api/v1/overseas/me/profile`, `PUT …` | Authenticated | `overseas_student` (self) | As above for the caller; any other role `403 "Only an overseas student has this profile"`. |
| `GET /api/v1/workflows/overseas/agent/crm/students/{agent_student_id}/profile` | Authenticated | `agent` (agency scope, `load_scoped`) | `200 {profile}` without `passport_number` / `passport_expiry`; `{profile: null}` for a record with no login; out of scope `404`. |

Also: the portal Students section (counselor, admin) and the admin Users directory add a `profile` column (`type: student_profile`, the
page href, null without a login / for staff); the student portal `profile` section rows are Name, Highest qualification, Preferred
countries, Preferred intake, English test (from the table).

## 12FC. Counselor tasks (ccrm-029) — addendum, 2026-10-24

`DEC-SCOPE-250`. Migration `0175_counselor_tasks`. RBAC §2.165. Code: `app/services/counselor_tasks.py`, `app/api/counselor_tasks.py`;
the follow-up list in `app/api/telecaller_follow_ups.py`.

Task object: `{id, title, due_on, priority (high|medium|low), status (open|done|cancelled), band (overdue|today|upcoming|done|cancelled|null),
source, completed_at, link: {type (lead|student|application), id, label} | null, counselor: {id, full_name}, created_by: {id, full_name},
created_at, updated_at, can_edit}`. `band` is null for a task done more than 7 IST days ago.

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /api/v1/counselor/tasks?band=&counselor_id=&limit=&offset=` | Authenticated | overseas `counselor` (own only; `counselor_id` ignored), `overseas_admin`, `super_admin` | `200 {items, total, limit (1–200, default 100), offset, today, counts: {overdue, today, upcoming, done}}`. Without `band`: open tasks and those done in the last 7 days, open first by due date then priority. Bad `band` `422`. |
| `GET /api/v1/counselor/tasks/options?counselor_id=` | Authenticated | same | `200 {counselors: [{id, full_name}] (admin only: active overseas counselors), links: {leads, students, applications: [{id, label}]}}` — the chosen counselor's (a counselor: their own) open leads, csc-001 students and non-withdrawn applications, 200 each. |
| `POST /api/v1/counselor/tasks` | Authenticated | same | Body `{title (2–200, spaces collapsed), due_on, priority? (medium), counselor_user_id?, link?}`; extra keys `422`. A counselor naming another counselor `403`; the admin must name an active overseas counselor (`422` on `counselor_user_id`); due date today..+2 years (`422` on `due_on`); link out of the assignee's scope `422` on `link`. `201` task. Not idempotent. |
| `GET /api/v1/counselor/tasks/{id}` | Authenticated | same | `200` task; another counselor's task `404`. |
| `PATCH /api/v1/counselor/tasks/{id}` | Authenticated | same | Partial `{title?, due_on?, priority?, status?, counselor_user_id?, link? (null clears)}`; null on the others `422`. Row locked. Unchanged = nothing recorded. Cancelled task `409`. A counselor: reassigning `403`; a field other than `status` on a task they did not create `403`. Only changed fields are re-checked (an overdue task stays editable). `200` task. |
| `GET /api/v1/counselor/follow-ups?view=day\|overdue&day=&limit=&offset=` | Authenticated | `counselor` (leads handed to them, own division) | tel-011's day / overdue page (`counts: {day, overdue}`) over the counselor's own leads; other roles `403`. |

Other roles (incl. an IT counselor on `/counselor/tasks`) `403`; anonymous `401`.

## 12FD. Document status model, expiry and versions (ccrm-021) — addendum, 2026-10-24

`DEC-SCOPE-251`. Migration `0176_document_status` (after ccrm-029's 0175). RBAC §2.166. Code: `app/document_checklists.py` (vocabulary, `review_refusal`),
`app/api/workflows.py`, `app/services/document_expiry.py`, `app/worker.py` (`ccrm021-document-expiry`).

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PATCH /api/v1/workflows/overseas/documents/{id}/verify` (counselor / admin body) | Authenticated | `counselor` (own scope), `overseas_admin`, `super_admin` | **Changed.** Body `{verification_status? (default verified), notes? (≤10000), expiry_date?}`; status one of `under_review`, `verified`, `incomplete`, `reupload_required`, `not_required` (Draft/Review/Approved on CV/SOP types only), or the aliases `rejected` → `reupload_required`, `changes_required` → `incomplete`; anything else `422`. A passport verified without an expiry date, or with one before today, `422`. Archived (replaced) document `409`. Sets `verified_by_id`, `reviewed_at`, `reviewer_notes`, `expiry_date`; event named after the status; audit `document.verify` with the parsed body. Response unchanged `{id, verification_status}`. |
| same route (agent body) | Authenticated | `agent` | AGN-003 unchanged except the codes: `verified` \| `incomplete` \| `reupload_required` (aliases `rejected`, `changes_required` accepted and stored as the new codes); an archived document `409`; sets `reviewed_at`. |
| `POST /api/v1/workflows/overseas/documents/{id}/versions` | Authenticated | `counselor` (same scope as verify), `overseas_admin`, `super_admin` | **New.** Multipart `file` (PDF/JPEG/PNG by content, size limit, image metadata stripped — AGN-009's `read_upload`), `expiry_date?`. Locks the document; archived `409`; out of scope `403`; unknown `404`; bad file `415`/`422`/`413`. Creates a new document (`pending`, `version_of_id` = old id, same owner/application/type/label, the given expiry or the old one) and archives the old one (`archived_at`; events `replaced` on the old, `uploaded` on the new; audit `document.replace` `{version_of_id}`). `201 {id, verification_status, version_of_id, expiry_date}`. |
| `POST /api/v1/workflows/overseas/documents` | Authenticated | unchanged | Adds optional `expiry_date`. |
| `GET /api/v1/portal/overseas/{counselor\|overseas_admin}/documents` | unchanged | unchanged | Current versions only; rows add `reviewer`, `reviewed_at`, `expiry_date`, `replaces_earlier`. |
| `GET /api/v1/portal/overseas/student/documents` | unchanged | unchanged | Current versions only; `status` is the label ("Re-upload required"); adds `reviewed_on`, `expiry_date` columns. |
| `GET /api/v1/workflows/overseas/agent/crm/documents` | unchanged | unchanged | Current versions only; items add `reviewer`, `reviewed_at`, `expiry_date`. `PUT …/documents/{id}/file` of an archived document `409`. |

No delete route exists or is added. Daily job (Celery beat, 18:35 UTC = 00:05 IST): documents with `expiry_date` before the India date,
not archived and not `expired`/`not_required` → `expired` (event `expired`, no actor).

## 12FE. Convert a lead to a student (ccrm-008) — addendum, 2026-10-24

`DEC-SCOPE-252`. No migration. RBAC §2.167. Code: `app/services/lead_conversion.py`, `app/api/lead_handover.py`. Reuses
`counselor_students.create_student` (csc-002), `lead_handover.link_student` / `commit_link` (tel-018) and `student_profiles.write` (ccrm-007).

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/convert` | Authenticated | overseas `counselor` (the lead's owner, in its division) | **New.** Body `CounselorStudentCreate` `{full_name, email, phone?}`; extra keys `422`. Errors: another role or an IT counselor `403`; outside the counselor's scope `404`; linked or closed `409`; before Qualified `422` "Move the lead to Qualified before converting it"; an active overseas student with the same email (any case) or the same last ten mobile digits `409` "A student account already has this email or mobile. Link the existing student instead."; another user's email `409` "Email already exists" (audited); mail budget `429` + `Retry-After`. On success, in one transaction: the student is created (`overseas_student`, csc-001 link, ENH-003 token), the lead's data is copied into the profile (DEC-SCOPE-252 K3), and the lead is linked (Application/Enrollment). Then the link is mailed. `201 {student: {id, full_name, email, phone, student_code, setup}, lead: <counselor lead detail>, email_status, expires_at}`; never the token. |
| `GET /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}` | unchanged | unchanged | `permissions` adds `convert` (not linked, overseas, Qualified … Counselling Completed). |

Every application of the student (V1); `open` = not withdrawn / terminal outcome / enrolled. |

Field names only, never values. |

`GET /api/v1/portal/overseas/{counselor|admin}/students` and `…/admin/users` rows add `open` (the 360 path; `null` for a student with no

| `POST` | `/visa` | unchanged | An empty checklist takes the resolved visa template's document types. |

| `PATCH` | `/agent/crm/applications/{id}/visa` (AGN-012) | unchanged | Contract unchanged; `visa_status` follows the stage/decision; the application moves forward / closes as `visa_refused` (VC5); the counselor is notified. The detail's `visa` block adds the non-restricted lifecycle fields. |

Declined writes outcome `offer_declined` (terminal). `404` offer of another application; `409` not the current offer, on hold, closed. |
| `GET` | `/{id}/offers` | unchanged | Each offer adds `decision`, `decision_label`, `decision_date`, `decided_by`. |

Never a file key. |
| `PUT` | `/{id}/deposit` | writers | Body `DepositRecordSave`: `required`, `amount` (> 0), `currency` (3 letters), `due_date`, `payment_status` (`not_required` \| `pending` \| `paid_to_university` \| `paid_via_edusphere`), `paid_on` (paid statuses only, required there, not future), `receipt_document_id` (a "Deposit receipt" of this application, else `422`). Not required ⇒ no amount, currency, dates or receipt. `200` `{record, changed}`; identical → `changed: false`. `409` beside an AGN-011 deposit, closed; allowed on hold. |
| `POST` | `/{id}/deposit-receipts` | writers | Multipart `file` (PDF or image, AGN-009 rules) → `201` `{document:{id, name}}`, type "Deposit receipt". |

## 12FJ. Application checklist and Ready-for-Submission gate (ccrm-019) — addendum, 2026-10-24

`DEC-SCOPE-257`. All paths under `/api/v1/workflows/overseas/applications`. Writers = `counselor` (own applications, direct and agency),
`overseas_admin`, `super_admin`.

| Method | Path | Roles | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/{id}/checklist` | writers, `agent` (own agency), `overseas_student` (own) | `{checklist, template, documents}`: `checklist` = `{template_id, template_version, gate_mode, documents_completed_on, summary:{required_total, required_complete, optional_total, optional_complete, ready, missing:[{id,label,status,status_label}]}, items:[{id, position, document_type, label, name, required, notes, allowed_statuses, mark, status, status_label, removable, documents:[{id, document_type, document_label, original_filename, verification_status, status_label, created_at}]}]}` or null; an item with no mark and nothing linked is `missing` ("Missing", browser QA-01); `template` (the matching admission template, writers only, while no checklist exists) and `documents` (linkable, writers only). Never a file key. |
| `POST` | `/{id}/checklist` | writers | `201` the `GET` shape; from the matching admission template (version + gate mode frozen, documents auto-linked by type) or empty (`warn`). `409` exists, closed, archived agency record. |
| `POST` | `/{id}/checklist/items` | writers | Body = a template item (`document_type` from the upload list, `label` (needed for Other), `required`, `allowed_statuses`, `notes`). `201`. `409` no checklist. |
| `PATCH` | `/{id}/checklist/items/{item_id}` | writers | `{mark: "requested" \| "not_required" \| null}`; a mark outside the item's `allowed_statuses` `422`; another application's item `404`. |
| `DELETE` | `/{id}/checklist/items/{item_id}` | writers | Counselor-added items only (`409` for a template item). `200` the `GET` shape. |
| `PUT` | `/{id}/checklist/items/{item_id}/documents/{document_id}` | writers | Link (idempotent; clears the mark). A document that is not the application's student's current one (other student, other application, archived) `422`. |
| `DELETE` | `/{id}/checklist/items/{item_id}/documents/{document_id}` | writers | Unlink. `200` the `GET` shape. |
| `POST` | `/{id}/sub-status`, `/agent/crm/applications/{id}/sub-status` | unchanged roles | Body adds optional `override_reason` (≤ 1000). A move to `ready_for_submission` with required items incomplete: `block` → `409` `{detail:{message, gate_mode:"block", missing}}`; `warn` without a reason → `409` (`gate_mode:"warn"`); with one → `200`, history `reason` = `Override: <reason>`. |

Writes hold the application row lock, refresh `documents_completed_on`, audit `overseas.application.checklist.<create|item_add|item_mark|item_remove|link|unlink>` and commit once; allowed on hold.

## 12FK. Student journey stage (ccrm-017) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`journey` = `{stage, label, steps:[{key, label, state: "done" | "current" | "upcoming"}], application: {id, application_code, university} | null,
note: "On hold" | "Deferred" | "All applications closed" | null}`. `stage` ∈ `lead`, `counselling`, `applying`, `offer`, `visa`, `enrolled`;

## 12FL. Counselor activity on leads (ccrm-004) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-259`. Every `/counselor/...` route below is the counselor a lead is handed to, in their division (`counselor_scope`: other roles
`403`, another counselor's lead / row `404`); the bodies and answers are the telecaller routes' own.

- Calls (§12R): `GET|POST /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/calls`, `PATCH|DELETE /api/v1/counselor/calls/{call_id}`. A counselor's call never
  moves the stage (`lead` in the `201` is the unchanged stage); `next_follow_up` follows the counselor's tel-011 rules.
- Messages (§12U / §12Z): `GET /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/render?template_id=`, `GET|POST /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/messages`,
  `DELETE /api/v1/counselor/messages/{message_id}` (WhatsApp, sender, same IST day). `GET /api/v1/telecaller/templates` now also answers a
  counselor (active rows only); previews, scripts and brochures stay `403`.
- Meetings (new): `GET /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/meetings` → page of `{id, occurred_at, mode, notes, author{id, full_name},
  created_at}`, newest first. `POST` `{occurred_at (offset required), mode: "Online" | "Phone" | "In person", notes (1–2000, trimmed)}` →
  `201` the row; future or more than 7 days back `422` on `occurred_at`; closed lead `409`. Append-only; not idempotent.
- Appointment completion (§12Q): `POST /api/v1/lead-appointments/{id}/complete` takes an optional body `{outcome_notes (≤ 2000)}`; every
  appointment answer carries `outcome_notes`.
- Counselor lead detail (§12V): adds `whatsapp_to` and `permissions.activity` (lead not closed).
- Timeline (§12AH): new kind `meeting` (`from_value` = mode, `reason` = notes excerpt); a completed appointment's `reason` is its outcome notes.
- Management counseling (§mgmt-018): `calls` is a number (the calls the counselor logged in the period) and its figure is `tracked: true`.

## 12FM. Lead data fields (ccrm-005) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-260`. Additive.

- **Lead fields.** `POST /api/v1/telecaller/leads`, `POST /api/v1/counselor/walk-in-leads` (same body) and `PATCH /api/v1/telecaller/leads/{id}`
  accept optional `contact_preference` (`call` | `whatsapp` | `email` | `sms` | null), `enquiry_date` (YYYY-MM-DD, 2000 → today; omitted on
  create = today in IST; null on an edit → 422), `marketing_opt_in` (true | false | null = not asked), `referral_name` (≤ 160) and
  `referral_contact` (≤ 120). Every lead read (admin list/detail, telecaller list/detail, counselor list/detail) adds those five plus
  `marketing_consent_at` (set by the server when the consent changes).
- **`PATCH /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}`** — counselor only (403 otherwise), a lead handed to them in their division (404 otherwise). Body: the
  five fields above, nothing else (`extra="forbid"` → 422). Only changed values are written; a consent change writes `lead.consent_change`
  `{from, to}`, the rest one `lead.contact_update` `{fields}`. Answers the counselor lead detail (§12V).
- **`GET` / `PUT /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/qualification`** — the §12O body and answer on a lead handed to the counselor (404 otherwise).
- **Qualification (§12O) overseas group** adds `preferred_country_id` (an unknown country → 422 at `preferred_country_id`), `study_mode`
  (`full_time` | `part_time` | `online`), `preferred_campus` (≤ 200), `academic_cgpa` (0–10, 2 dp), `backlogs` (0–99) and `english_tests`
  (≤ 5 rows `{test_type, status, overall_score, test_date, valid_until, remarks}`, ccrm-007's §12FB rules; omitted or null = none). The read adds
  `preferred_country {id, name} | null` and `english_tests[].expired`. On an IT or `other` product these fields are 422 like any other field
  that does not apply.
- **CSV import (§12N)** — the template and accepted header gain `contact_preference`, `enquiry_date`, `marketing_opt_in` (yes/no/true/false),
  `referral_name`, `referral_contact` as the last five columns; older files without them still import.
- **Conversion (§12FE)** copies study mode, preferred country, CGPA / backlogs and the English tests into the new student's profile.

## 12FN. Offer conditions (ccrm-024) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-261`. All paths under `/api/v1/workflows/overseas/applications`. Writers = `counselor` (own applications, direct and agency),
`overseas_admin`, `super_admin` (ccrm-023's).

| Method | Path | Roles | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/{id}/offers` (§12EX) | unchanged | Additive: each offer gains `condition_items:[{id, description, owner, owner_label, due_date, status, status_label, evidence:{id,name}\|null, note, status_changed_at}]` (oldest first) and `conditions_summary:{total, satisfied, open, failed, all_satisfied}`; the payload gains `agency` (bool) and `evidence_documents` (writers only). Never a file key. |
| `POST` | `/{id}/condition-evidence` (multipart `file`) | writers | A "Condition evidence" document under the offer-letter upload rules (§12EX). `201 {"document"}`. |
| `POST` | `/{id}/offers/{offer_id}/conditions` | writers | `{description (≤1000), owner: student\|counselor\|agent, due_date?}`, extra keys `422`. Starts `open`. Another application's offer `404`; not the current offer `409`; `agent` on a direct application `422`. `201 {condition, conditions_summary, changed}`. |
| `PATCH` | `/{id}/offers/{offer_id}/conditions/{condition_id}` | writers | Partial `{description, owner, due_date, status: open\|met\|waived\|failed, evidence_document_id, note}`; description / owner / status cannot be `null`. Met without evidence `422`; waived / failed without a note `422`; evidence that is not this application's "Condition evidence" `422`; another offer's condition `404`; not the current offer `409`. Nothing different → `200 changed:false`. |

Writes hold the application row lock, are allowed on hold, write one history row (stage unchanged), notify the student, tell the agency
(CC5), audit `overseas.application.offer_condition` (`{offer_id, condition_id, action: added|updated, status}`) and commit once. Recording an
offer (§12EX `POST …/offers`, the agency `PUT …/offer`) with conditions text also creates one open student condition; the agency `PATCH`
that moves only the offer deadline carries the conditions to the new offer row.

## 12FO. Enrolment confirmation and post-offer outcomes (ccrm-027) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-262`. Under `/api/v1/workflows/overseas/applications/{id}` (router `api/application_offers.py`):

- `GET …/enrollment` — counselor (own, direct and agency), Overseas Admin; the student and the agency read their own. Returns
  `{enrollment: {status, confirmed, confirmed_at, can_confirm, enrollment_date, university_student_id, course_start_date,
  pre_enrollment_checklist_complete, document: {id, name} | null}, documents: [{id, name, created_at}]}` — `documents` (the picker) for
  writers only, `[]` otherwise. No file keys.
- `POST …/enrollment-documents` (multipart `file`) — `201 {document}`; an "Enrolment confirmation" document under the offer-letter rules
  (PDF or image sniffed; allowed on hold; closed → 409).
- `PUT …/enrollment` — body `{expected_status, enrollment_date, enrollment_document_id, pre_enrollment_checklist_complete: true,
  university_student_id?, course_start_date?, notes?}` (extra fields 422). Confirm (stage before `enrolled`): stage → `enrolled`, sub-status
  `enrolled`, `enrollment_confirmed_at`, one history row "Enrolment confirmed[ -- note]", AGT-003 commission (agency, at most one), student
  notice "Enrolment confirmed", agency notice (CC5), audit `overseas.application.enrollment`. Once enrolled: a correction (audit
  `{fields, confirmed: false}`, agency told, no history row); unchanged → `changed: false`, nothing written. Returns
  `{id, status, sub_status, enrollment, changed}`. Errors: 422 checklist false / missing document / document not this application's
  "Enrolment confirmation" / before an offer ("An offer is needed before enrollment"); 409 stale `expected_status`, on hold, closed,
  archived agency record; 403 other roles or out of scope.
- `PATCH …` (`status`), `POST …/advance` and `POST …/sub-status` to `enrolled` → **422** "Enrolment is confirmed with the enrolment confirmation
  document, not by a stage change" (E3).
- `POST …/outcome` accepts `non_arrival` and `cancelled` (reason required; 422 "An offer is needed before this outcome" before an offer;
  409 once enrolled); both terminal (reopen: Overseas Admin, §12EW).
- Management funnel (`GET /management/funnel`): the Enrollment stage counts first moves to `enrolled` whose enrolment is confirmed.

## 12FP. Sensitive documents and access logging (ccrm-022) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-263`. Classes `standard | identity | financial` (`services/document_access.py` is the one rule).

| Method | Path | Change |
|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/workflows/overseas/documents/{id}/download` (OVS-005) | Roles and scope unchanged. The `document.download` audit row's metadata gains `sensitivity`. A `403` on an identity/financial document also writes `document.download` with outcome `denied` (committed before the error). |
| `GET` | `/files/download?key=` | `404 Not found` when the key is an overseas student document's file (current or archived row, either stored shape, a replaced file's history key) or starts `agent-documents/`; other keys unchanged. |
| `GET`/`POST`/`PUT` | `/overseas-admin/checklists…` (§12FA) | Each item accepts optional `sensitivity` (omitted → the type's class; unknown → `422`) and returns it. |
| `GET`/`POST` | `/workflows/overseas/applications/{id}/checklist…` (§12FJ) | Each item returns `sensitivity` (copied from the template item; a counselor's item takes its type's unless sent); each linked / linkable document returns its effective `sensitivity`. |
| `GET` | `/portal/overseas/{counselor,admin,university}/documents` | Rows gain `sensitivity`; a university_rep gets no identity/financial rows. |
| `GET` | `/portal/overseas/student/downloads`, `/portal/overseas/agent/documents` | No file key: the student's document rows read "Download from Documents"; the agent rows drop the `file` column. |

## 12FQ. Lead stage additions and structured loss capture (ccrm-006) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-264`. Additive.

- **Stages.** The catalogue gains the closed stage `duplicate` ("Duplicate"); `wrong_number` is labelled "Invalid Contact" (key unchanged).
- **Stage moves** — `POST /api/v1/telecaller/leads/{id}/stage`, `POST /api/v1/counselor/leads/{id}/stage` and `PATCH /api/v1/admin/leads/{id}`
  (with `status`) accept optional `duplicate_of` (a Lead ID, `LD-…`, any case), `lost_reason_id` (uuid) and `lost_to` (≤ 160, trimmed).
  `duplicate` needs `duplicate_of` — a lead of the same division, not the lead itself and not itself a Duplicate (422 at `duplicate_of`).
  `lost` / `not_interested` need `lost_reason_id` of an active reason (422 at `lost_reason_id`). A closure field sent for another target → 422
  at that field. For those three targets `reason` is optional (the history keeps the reason name or "Duplicate of LD-…" instead); other closed
  outcomes still need it. The counselor's list (CC8) has no `duplicate`. Reopening clears all three fields. Call-logged closes (§12R) are unchanged.
- **Lead reads** (admin list, telecaller list/detail, counselor list/detail) add `duplicate_of {id, lead_code} | null`,
  `lost_reason {id, name} | null`, `lost_to`.
- **`GET /api/v1/telecaller/lost-reasons`** — catalogue readers (§12F P1); `active`, `q`, `limit`, `offset`; managers and super_admin also see
  inactive rows. Page of `{id, name, active, sort_order}`, ordered by sort order then name.
- **`POST /api/v1/telecaller/lost-reasons`** `{name (1–120), sort_order?}` → 201; **`PATCH /api/v1/telecaller/lost-reasons/{id}`**
  `{name?, active?, sort_order?}`. telecaller_manager / super_admin only (403); a duplicate name (any case) → 409; audit
  `telecaller.lost_reason_create` / `_update` with field names. Never deleted.
- **`GET /api/v1/telecaller/reports/lost`** (+ `.csv`) — §12AC's scope and filters; rows: leads created in the range now Lost or Not
  Interested, by reason (`label`, `leads`, `lost`, `not_interested`); no reason → "No reason recorded".

## 12FR. Arrival and orientation (ccrm-028) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-265`. Under `/api/v1/workflows/overseas/applications/{id}` (router `api/application_offers.py`):

- `GET …/arrival` — counselor (own, direct and agency), Overseas Admin; the student and the agency read their own. Returns
  `{arrival: {status, can_record, arrival_date, arrival_confirmed, confirmed_at, confirmed_by, orientation_attended, notes}}` —
  `confirmed_by` is the confirming staff member's name (no email or id).
- `PUT …/arrival` — body `{arrival_date?, arrival_confirmed, orientation_attended, notes?}` (saved whole; extra fields 422; dates
  2000–2100; notes ≤ 2000, cleaned). Turning `arrival_confirmed` on stamps who and when; keeping it on keeps the first stamp; turning it off
  clears it. A change writes one history row "Arrival updated -- arrived|expected <date>[, orientation attended]" (stage and sub-status
  unchanged), the agency notice "Application details updated" (CC5), audit `overseas.application.arrival` `{fields}`; unchanged →
  `changed: false`, nothing written. Returns `{arrival, changed}`. Moves no stage, sub-status, outcome or commission (A1). Errors: 422
  "Arrival is recorded after enrolment is confirmed" (not `enrolled`), "Enter the arrival date to confirm arrival", "A confirmed arrival
  date cannot be in the future", "Confirm arrival before recording orientation"; 409 closed or archived agency record (on hold allowed);
  403 other roles or out of scope.
## 12FS. Document requests for direct students (ccrm-020) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-266`. Staff = `counselor` (overseas, csc-001 scope) and `overseas_admin` through ccrm-007's `staff_student` gate (another student
`404`, another role `403`). A request item: `{id, document_type, document_label, name, note, review_note, status: open|fulfilled|cancelled,
due_date, overdue, application_id, application_code, university, checklist_item_id, requested_by, created_at, closed_at,
document:{id, original_filename, verification_status, status_label}|null}` — never a file key. `overdue` = open and due before today (IST);
`review_note` = the note on the rejection that reopened an open request (R1), else `null`. Lists are `{items, total, limit, offset}`
(`limit` 1–100, default 20), open requests first by due date, then the rest newest first.

| Method | Path | Roles | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/api/v1/counselor/students/{student_id}/document-requests?status=all\|open` | staff | Default `all`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/counselor/students/{student_id}/document-requests` | staff | `{document_type?, document_label?, due_date, note? (≤1000), application_id?, checklist_item_id?}`, extra keys `422`. Type from the AGN-009 request list ("Other" needs a 2–80 label, no "Offer letter"); a checklist item (needs its `application_id`, else `422`) gives the type and label and is marked `requested`. Due date before today `422`; an application that is not the student's `422`; the application through the checklist writers' entry (scope, row lock, closed `409`); an item not on it `404`; an open request for the same student + application + type + label `409`. `201 {request}`; the student is notified (never the note). Audit `document_request.create`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/counselor/students/{student_id}/document-requests/{request_id}/cancel` | staff | Not open `409`; clears the item's `requested` mark; the student is notified. Audit `document_request.cancel`. `200 {request}`. |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/overseas/me/document-requests?status=open\|all` | `overseas_student` (own) | Default `open`. |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/overseas/me/document-requests/{request_id}/upload` | `overseas_student` (own, open) | Multipart `file` (AGN-009 rules: PDF/JPEG/PNG by bytes, global size limit, metadata stripped), `expiry_date?`. Another student's `404`; not open `409`. Creates the document (`pending`, the request's application, type and label), fulfils the request, links it to the checklist item, tells the requester. `201 {request, document_id}`. Audit `document.upload`. |

Changed: `PATCH /api/v1/workflows/overseas/documents/{id}/verify` (counselor/admin and AGN-003 agent paths) — `incomplete` /
`reupload_required` (legacy `rejected` / `changes_required` included) on a document that fulfils a direct student's request reopens it (the
document stays, no longer fulfilling it; a `requested` event `fulfilled → open` with the review note). `GET …/applications/{id}/checklist`
(§12FJ) gains `student_id` (writers only) and each item `open_request:{id, due_date}|null`. AGN-009 agency request routes are unchanged.


## 12FT. Management CRM reports (ccrm-034) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-267`. `GET /api/v1/management/crm-reports?date_from&date_to&counselor&country&university&course&intake` (router
`api/management.py`, service `services/management_crm_reports.py`). Read-only, nothing stored or audited.

- Returns `{filters, as_of, options: {counselors, countries, universities, courses, intakes}, ageing, universities, visa, readiness, leads,
  forecast}`. Each report is `{definitions: [{key, label, definition}], rows: [...], total: {...}}`; the total is counted, not summed.
- Rows: ageing `{stage, stage_label, sub_status, sub_status_label, applications, students, bands: {d0_7, d8_14, d15_30, d31_60, d61_plus},
  oldest_days}`; universities `{university_id, label, applications, students, offers, offer_rate, rejections, acceptances, enrollments}`;
  visa `{country_id, label, applications, students, submitted, pending, approved, refused, enrolled}`; readiness `{stage, stage_label,
  applications, students, ready, blocked, needs_override, no_checklist}`; leads `{intake, label, leads, converted, conversion_pct, students,
  applications}`; forecast `{intake, label, applications, students, confirmed, expected, possible, lost, forecast}`. A blank intake is
  `intake: null`, label "Not recorded"; rates are `null` when nothing divides.
- Errors: 401 anonymous; 403 any role but partner, super_admin, overseas_admin; 422 end before start, malformed `counselor` (id or
  `none`), `country`/`university`/`course` not a UUID, `intake` over 80 characters.

## 12FU. CRM exports (ccrm-035) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-268`. CSV (`text/csv; charset=utf-8`, BOM) or xlsx (`application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet`),
`Content-Disposition: attachment`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. `format` is `csv` (default) or `xlsx`.

- `GET /api/v1/management/crm-reports/export?report=<ageing|universities|visa|readiness|leads|forecast>&format&date_from&date_to&counselor&country&university&course&intake`
  (router `api/management.py`): one §12FT report — the row header, its measures under their on-screen labels (rates `… (%)`, ageing bands
  one column each), then `Total`. File `crm-<report>-<IST date>`.
- `GET /api/v1/counselor/exports/applications?format&country&university&intake&stage&sub_status&from&to` (router `api/crm_exports.py`):
  the counselor's own applications, newest change first. Columns: EduSphere ID, Student, University, Agency, Intake, Status, Sub-status,
  Outcome (label only), Next action, Last updated. File `my-applications-<IST date>`.
- `GET /api/v1/counselor/exports/leads?format&stage&priority&from&to`: the counselor's own leads, newest first. Columns: Lead ID, Name,
  Interest, Stage, Priority, Source, Telecaller, Lead date. File `my-leads-<IST date>`.
- Each export writes `audit_logs` `action=crm.export`, `entity_type` `crm_report`|`counselor_list`, `entity_id` the key, metadata
  `{format, filters: {set filters → values}, rows}`, committed before the response.
- Errors: 401 anonymous; 403 wrong role (management: not partner/super_admin/overseas_admin; lists: not the overseas counselor); 422 unknown
  `report`/`format`, the §12FT / ccrm-012 filter errors (same messages), or a list over 5,000 rows ("narrow the filters"); 429 + `Retry-After`
  after 30 exports in 10 minutes.

## 12FV. Counselor's own performance report (ccrm-036) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-269`. `GET /api/v1/counselor/performance?from&to` (router `api/counselor_performance.py`) → `{from, to, as_of, period: [Figure],
now: [Figure], applications: {rows: [{stage, stage_label, sub_status, sub_status_label, applications, students}], total: {applications,
students}, definitions: [{key, label, definition}]}}`. `Figure = {key, label, definition, unit: "count"|"percent", value: int|float|null}`
(a percentage is null when nothing is due / assigned). Period keys: `leads_assigned`, `calls`, `counseling_sessions`, `follow_ups`,
`conversions`, `conversion_pct` (mgmt-018's definitions), `follow_ups_due`, `follow_ups_completed`, `follow_up_completion_pct`,
`tasks_completed`. Now keys: `overdue_tasks`, `overdue_follow_ups`, `applications`, `students`, `offers`, `visas_pending`, `visas_approved`,
`visas_refused`, `enrollments`, `documents_pending`. No revenue. Read-only, not audited.
- Errors: 401 anonymous; 403 anyone but the overseas counselor; 422 a malformed date, `from` after `to`, or a period over 366 days.

## 12FW. Counselor reminder rules (ccrm-030) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-270`. Router `api/counselor_reminders.py`, service `services/counselor_reminders.py`, beat `ccrm030-counselor-reminders`
(daily 08:15 IST). Design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-25-ccrm-030-counselor-reminders-design.md`.

- `GET /api/v1/overseas-admin/reminder-rules` → `{rules: [{key, label, description, action: task|notify, timing: assign|silent|before|after,
  min_offset, max_offset, enabled, offset_days, notify_student (null on rules that never remind the student), updated_at}]}` in catalogue order.
- `PUT /api/v1/overseas-admin/reminder-rules/{key}` body `{enabled, offset_days, notify_student?}` (extra fields 422) → the rule. 404 unknown
  key; 422 offset outside the rule's range or `notify_student: true` on a rule without a student column. Audited
  `counselor_reminder_rule.update` with field names.
- `POST /api/v1/overseas-admin/reminder-rules/run` → `{created, duplicate, skipped, failed, rules: {key: created}}`; the daily job, now.
  Idempotent: a second run creates nothing new.
- `GET /api/v1/overseas-admin/reminder-rules/log?limit=50&offset=0` (limit 1–100) → `{items: [{id, rule_key, rule_label, summary, task_created,
  recipients, created_at}], total, limit, offset}`, newest first.
- Side effects outside the routes: `POST /telecaller/leads/{id}/handover` and `PATCH /admin/leads/{id}` (owner changed to an overseas
  counselor) add the counselor's first-contact task and notice when the rule is on. Response shapes unchanged.
- Errors: 401 anonymous; 403 any role but `overseas_admin` (overseas division) and `super_admin`.

## 12FX. Reassignment with a logged reason (ccrm-033) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-271`. Contract change on three existing writes; no new route.

- `PUT /api/v1/workflows/overseas/applications/{id}/counselor` and `PUT /api/v1/admin/users/{id}/counselor`: body
  `{counselor_id, reason?}`. `reason` is trimmed, at most 500 characters, no control characters (422), blank = none. Replacing an
  existing counselor without a reason is `422 {"detail": "Give a reason for the reassignment"}` and changes nothing; a first assignment
  or the same counselor needs none. The reason is stored on the history row (application) and in the audit metadata, and appended to
  both counselors' notices ("… Reason: <text>"). Response unchanged.
- `PATCH /api/v1/admin/leads/{id}`: new optional key `owner_reason` (same rules; `reason` stays the stage move's). Replacing or removing
  an owner without it is a field-shaped 422 at `body.owner_reason`. A change writes audit `lead.owner_change` and notifies the new owner
  ("Lead assigned to you", `/{division}/counselor/leads/{id}`) and the previous one if active ("Lead reassigned").
- Reads: status history (`GET …/applications/{id}/status`, staff) returns the reason on the counsellor row; the agency detail
  (`GET /workflows/overseas/agent/crm/applications/{id}`) returns `reason: null` on it; the student 360 history item "Counselor assigned"
  carries `reason`; the lead timeline (`…/timeline`) has a `handover` row per `lead.owner_change` with `from_value` / `to_value`
  (empty = removed) and `reason`.

## 12FY. Overdue escalation (ccrm-031) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-272`. One new route; one new reminder rule.

- `GET /api/v1/overseas-admin/reminder-rules` lists `overdue_escalation` (`action: "notify"`, `timing: "escalate"`, `min_offset: 0`,
  `max_offset: 30`, `notify_student: null`); `PUT .../overdue_escalation` takes `offset_days` 0–30 (422 outside, 422 with
  `notify_student: true`). `POST .../run` counts escalated items under `rules.overdue_escalation`; the log lists one row per item
  ("<counselor> · task due <day>" / "<counselor> · follow-up <lead code> due <day>").
- `GET /api/v1/counselor/tasks/overdue-by-counselor` → `{today, threshold_days, enabled, items: [{counselor: {id, full_name}, active,
  overdue_tasks, overdue_follow_ups, past_threshold, oldest_due}]}` — counselors with at least one open task due before today or open
  follow-up past its due time; ordered by `past_threshold` desc, total desc, name. 403 `"Overseas Administrator role required"` for any
  role but `overseas_admin` / `super_admin`. Live, not paginated.
- Notification to each active Overseas Admin: title "Overdue work escalated", body "<counselor>: N tasks and M follow-ups overdue by
  more than D days.", `action_url` `/overseas/admin/counselor-tasks#overdue`.

## 12FZ. Counselor message templates and the student message log (ccrm-037) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-273`. Router `api/counselor_messages.py`, service `services/counselor_messages.py`, worker `notifications/student_email.py`
(task `deliver_student_email_task`, beat `ccrm037-sweep-stale-student-emails` every 5 minutes). Migration `0191_student_messages`. Design
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-25-ccrm-037-counselor-student-messages-design.md`.

- `GET /api/v1/counselor/templates?channel&active&q&limit&offset` → `{items: [{id, channel, name, subject, body, active}], total, limit,
  offset}`; a counselor always gets active rows only.
- `POST /api/v1/counselor/templates` body `{channel: whatsapp|email, name, subject (email only, required there), body}` → 201;
  `PATCH /api/v1/counselor/templates/{id}` body any of `{name, subject, body, active}` (channel fixed, 422). 409 duplicate name per channel;
  422 unknown placeholder (MT2), subject rules, body over 1000 / 5000. Audited `counselor_template.create|update` with field names.
- `GET /api/v1/counselor/templates/{id}/preview` → `{subject, body, missing}` with sample values; an inactive template is 404 to a counselor.
- `GET /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/messages?limit=50&offset=0` (limit 1–100) → `{items: [Message], total, limit, offset, recipient:
  {name, email, whatsapp_to}, applications: [{id, label}]}` newest first; `applications` = those a message may be about (the counselor's own
  for this student; every one for the Overseas Admin).
- `GET /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/messages/render?template_id&application_id?` → `{template: {id, name, channel}, subject, body,
  missing}`. 422 an application that is not the student's and the counselor's own; 404 an inactive or unknown template.
- `POST /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/messages` body `{channel: "whatsapp", template_id?, body ≤ 1000, application_id?}` or
  `{channel: "email", template_id?, subject ≤ 200, body ≤ 5000, application_id?}` (extra fields 422) → 201 Message. WhatsApp is the
  counselor's confirmation after wa.me; email is stored `queued` and published after the commit. 409 deactivated student / no usable number /
  WhatsApp cap; 422 application or template; 429 email cap; 503 SMTP not configured. Not idempotent. Audited `student_message.create`.
- `POST /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/replies` body `{channel: "whatsapp", body, received_at?, application_id?}` or `{channel: "email",
  subject?, body, received_at?, application_id?}` → 201 Message (`direction: "inbound"`). 422 future / older than 7 days / application;
  409 cap. Audited `student_message.reply`.
- `Message = {id, student_id, application: {id, label} | null, direction: outbound|inbound, channel, template: {id, name} | null, subject,
  body, delivery_status: queued|sending|retrying|sent|failed | null, sent_at, sender: {id, full_name}}` (for a reply, `sent_at` is when it
  was received and `sender` who recorded it).
- Errors: 401 anonymous; templates 403 any role but the overseas counselor (read), `overseas_admin` (overseas) and `super_admin`; messages
  403 any role but the overseas counselor and the Overseas Admin (who reads only: sends and replies 403); 404 a student outside the
  counselor's csc-001 scope, unknown, or not an overseas student.

## 12GA. Calendar integration, phase 1 (ccrm-038) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-274`. Migration `0192_calendar_feeds` (`calendar_feeds`, `appointments.calendar_sequence`).

- `GET /api/v1/counselor/calendar-feed` → `{active, created_at, url: null}`. `POST` (201) creates or regenerates the feed and returns
  `{active: true, created_at, url}` — `url` = `FRONTEND_URL/api/v1/calendar/feeds/<token>.ics`, returned only here. `DELETE` (204,
  idempotent) revokes it. Overseas counselor only (403 otherwise; 401 without a session). Audit `calendar_feed.create|regenerate|revoke`.
- `GET /api/v1/calendar/feeds/{token}.ics` — no session; `text/calendar; charset=utf-8`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. An unknown
  or revoked token, an inactive owner or one who is no longer an overseas counselor → 404 `Calendar feed not found`.
- Appointment invites (no contract change on the writes): `POST /workflows/overseas/appointments`, `PATCH …/{id}` (new `scheduled_at`,
  or `status` → cancelled), `POST /telecaller/leads/{id}/appointments` and `POST /lead-appointments/{id}/reschedule|cancel` queue a
  notification + email delivery (context `{kind: "appointment_invite", ics, method}`) per CA1. IT appointments send nothing.

| Method/Path | Behaviour |
|---|---|
| `GET /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/finance` | `{payments[] {id, description, amount, currency, status, status_label, due_date, created_at, installment_no, invoice_no, receipt_no, can_download}, deposits[] {application_id, application_code, university, mine, source (counselor \| agency), required, amount, currency, due_date, status, status_label, paid_on, refund {amount, refunded_on, reason} \| null, receipt {id, name, can_download} \| null}, refunds[] {kind (payment \| deposit), description, amount, currency, refunded_on, application_code}}`. Read-only. Payments = the student's own, not `agent_deposit`, newest first; `can_download` = Overseas Admin (mirrors `GET /payments/{id}/invoice\|receipt`). Deposits: the ccrm-025 record, else the AGN-011 deposit, per application; a receipt's `can_download` follows the §12FF documents rule. Amounts are 2-decimal strings; no `file_url`. |

## 12GC. Archive instead of delete; overseas consent capture (ccrm-040) — addendum, 2026-10-25

`DEC-SCOPE-276`. Migration `0193_student_archive_consent` (`overseas_student_archives`, `agreements.kind`, v1 overseas consents).

- `POST /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/archive` `{reason}` (3–500 chars, trimmed) → 200 `{archived_at, archived_by, reason}`. The
  student's csc-001 counselor or the Overseas Admin; another role 403; outside csc-001 scope 404; a counselor who is not the student's
  counselor 403; already archived 409; an open application 409 `Close or withdraw the student's open applications first: APP-…`.
- `POST /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/restore` → 200 `{id, archived: false}`. Overseas Admin only (403); not archived 409.
- `GET /api/v1/counselor/students/archived?limit&offset` → `{items: [{id, full_name, email, student_code, archived_at, archived_by,
  reason}], total, limit, offset}`, newest first. Admin: all; overseas counselor: their csc-001 students; others 403.
- `GET /api/v1/counselor/students/{id}/overview` gains `archive` (null or as above), `permissions: {archive, restore}` and
  `consents: [{kind, label, version, accepted_at}]`. History adds "Archived" (with `reason`), "Restored" and "Consent given".
- `GET /api/v1/overseas/me/consents` → `{items: [{id, kind, title, body, version, accepted_at}]}` (current agreement per kind).
  `POST /api/v1/overseas/me/consents/{agreement_id}/accept` → 201 `{id, kind, version, accepted_at}`; a repeat 200 with the first time;
  not a current overseas consent 404. Overseas student only (403).
- Changed: `POST /api/v1/workflows/overseas/applications` → 409 `This student is archived. The Overseas Admin can restore them.`; the
  portal application sections (counselor/admin), `GET /counselor/students` and `GET /lookups/overseas-students` (counselor/admin) leave
  archived students out. ccrm-037: `GET /counselor/students/{id}/messages` `recipient` gains `whatsapp_opt_in`; a WhatsApp send to a
  student who has not opted in → 409 `This student has not opted in to WhatsApp messages`.

## 12GE. Private file storage and upload policy (nehn-003) — addendum, 2026-10-26

`DEC-SCOPE-279`. Migration `0194_stored_files`.

- `POST /api/v1/files/upload?purpose=<default|assignment_submission|learning_material|signature|university_logo|career_resume>`
  (multipart `file`) → 201 `{id, purpose, filename, content_type, size, url: "/api/v1/files/{id}"}`. 401 without a session; 413 empty or
  over the purpose's limit; 415 type not allowed or bytes that do not match the type; 422 unknown purpose. The storage key is never returned.
- `GET /api/v1/files/{id}` → 200 the file (`Content-Disposition: attachment`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`) in local mode, 307 to a
  presigned URL in S3 mode. Owner or super_admin; others 403; 401 without a session; 404 unknown.
- `GET /api/v1/files/signed/{token}` → 200 the file; 404 for a forged, expired or non-download token. No session needed: it is what
  local-mode `presign_download` returns (every existing `{url, expires_in}` download response now carries this link instead of
  `/local-files/<key>`; the shape is unchanged).
- `GET /local-files/{key}` (no `/api/v1` prefix) → `public/…` keys open; any other key 404 without a session, and for a signed-in user
  the `/files/download` rules apply (KYC and overseas-document keys 404). PDFs and images are inline, any other type an attachment.
- `POST /files/local-upload` and `POST /public/career-upload`: unchanged shapes; content now sniffed (415 on a mismatch).

## 12GF. Student ID coverage and lookup (nehn-040) — addendum, 2026-10-26

`DEC-SCOPE-280`. Migration `0195_agent_student_codes`. Additive fields only; no new route.

- `GET /api/v1/admin/users` items gain `student_code` (string|null) and the request takes an optional `q` (≤ 100 chars, else 422): a
  literal, case-insensitive substring of the name, email, role or Student ID, matched before the 500-row cap (QA-01) inside the caller's
  existing division scope. `POST /api/v1/admin/users` assigns a code to an `it_student` / `overseas_student` account.
- `GET /api/v1/lookups/overseas-students` (not `purpose=link`): `q` also matches `student_code`; `detail` is `email · CODE` when the
  student has one. `purpose=link` is unchanged.
- `GET /api/v1/workflows/overseas/agent/crm/students` (list, `q` also matches the code), `GET …/{id}`, `POST` and `PATCH` responses gain
  `student_code` — the record's own code for a student with no login, the account's code for a linked one.
- `GET /api/v1/overseas-admin/agent-orgs/{org_id}/students` items gain `student_code`.

## 12GG. Permission engine: role × resource × action (nehn-005) — addendum, 2026-10-26

`DEC-SCOPE-281`. Migration `0196_role_permissions`. A key is `"<resource>.<action>"` from the catalogue (today: `payments.view`,
`payments.add`, `discounts.add`, `reports.telecaller.view`, `reports.telecaller.download`).

- `GET /api/v1/admin/permissions/catalogue` → 200 `{actions: [view, add, edit, delete, download, approve, review, issue, manage],
  resources: [{key, label, actions}]}`. super_admin only (403 for every other role, 401 without a session).
- `GET /api/v1/admin/roles/{role}/permissions` → 200 `{role, permissions: [sorted keys], editable}` (`editable: false` for super_admin,
  who holds the whole catalogue). super_admin only; 404 for an unknown role. A refused caller gets 403 first, so role names are not disclosed.
- `PUT /api/v1/admin/roles/{role}/permissions` body `{permissions: [keys]}` (replaces the whole set) → 200 same shape as GET. 403 for any
  caller but super_admin; 404 unknown role; 422 for an unknown or repeated key, or a missing `permissions`; 409 for `super_admin` (its grants
  cannot be reduced) or a concurrent edit of the same role. A real change writes an `audit_logs` row (`role_permissions.update`); a no-op
  writes none. It applies on the next request, on every API worker.
- `GET /api/v1/auth/me/permissions` → 200 `{role, permissions: [sorted keys]}` for any signed-in user; 401 without a session.
- Pilot routes now grant-driven, with unchanged shapes and unchanged 403 text: `GET /admin/payments` (`payments.view`), `POST /admin/payments`
  and `POST /admin/payments/emi-schedule` (`payments.add`), `POST /admin/payments/{id}/discount` (`discounts.add`) — "Admin role required";
  `GET /telecaller/reports/{kind}` (`reports.telecaller.view`) — "Telecaller reports are for managers and administrators"; and
  `GET /telecaller/reports/{kind}.csv` (`reports.telecaller.download`), whose 403 text is now "You don't have permission to download
  telecaller reports" (QA-01: true for a manager whose download alone is revoked).

`DEC-SCOPE-282`. No migration.

- New, each the twin of the existing `.csv` route — same query parameters, roles, scope, row cap, throttle and refusals (401/403/404/
  422/429), same audit row (metadata now also `format`), `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `Content-Disposition: attachment;
  filename="<the CSV's name>.xlsx"`, content type `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet`:
  `GET /telecaller/reports/{kind}.xlsx`, `GET /telecaller/manager/performance.xlsx`, `GET /recruiter/performance.xlsx`,
  `GET /workflows/overseas/agent/crm/reports/{kind}.xlsx`, `GET /partnership/reports/{kind}.xlsx`,
  `GET /workflows/overseas/agent/commissions/report.xlsx` (logged, not audited, as its CSV).
- The workbook: one sheet; header row bold, frozen and auto-filtered (a Total row sits below the filter); numbers and dates are typed
  cells (dates `yyyy-mm-dd`, timestamps IST `yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm`); text is literal (formula-looking text prefixed `'`, as in the CSV).
- The `.csv` routes are unchanged apart from the audit row's added `format: "csv"`.

`DEC-SCOPE-289`. Migration `0199_audit_log_indexes` (`ix_audit_logs_created_at`; `ix_audit_logs_user_id` only if missing). All additive.

- `GET /api/v1/admin/audit` gains query parameters: `actor` (user UUID), `action` (≤120, literal prefix: `payment.` matches every
  payment action), `entity_type` (≤80, exact), `entity_id` (≤80, exact), `division` (`it` | `overseas` | `global`, the actor's),
  `date_from` / `date_to` (India calendar days, inclusive), `limit` (1-500, default 500) and `offset` (≥ 0, no cap). Newest first
  (`created_at`, then `id`). **Still a JSON array**; each row gains `actor` (`{id, full_name, email, division}` or `null` for a system
  row). 422: `date_to` before `date_from` ("The end date must be on or after the start date"), an unknown `division`, a malformed
  `actor`/date, `limit`/`offset` out of range. Access unchanged (403 for non-admins). A division admin's scope (own-division actors and
  system rows) is now a SQL `WHERE`, applied before `LIMIT`/`OFFSET`; a `division` filter narrows within it. MA8 unchanged.
- `POST /api/v1/admin/audit/export` unchanged.

  `overseas_student` caller gets **404** `{"detail": "Invoices are not issued to students. Download your receipt instead."}`.
  Admins (own division; `super_admin` all) and other payers (agents) are unchanged: 200 signed URL, or 404 if not yet generated.

## 12GP. Unified student search (nehn-041) — addendum, 2026-10-26

`DEC-SCOPE-291`. No migration. New route; nothing else changes shape.

- `GET /api/v1/search/students?q=` — `q` required, trimmed 3-100 characters (422 "Type at least 3 characters" / "Use at most 100
  characters"). Optional `type` (`it_student` | `overseas_student` | `agency_student` | `school_student`), `division` (`it` |
  `overseas` | `school`) and `program` (IT program UUID → IT students enrolled in it); a bad value 422. Matching: literal,
  case-insensitive substring of name, email and Student ID; a phone-shaped `q` also matches the phone's digits (last 10 digits when
  longer); IT students also by enrolled program title, overseas students by an application's course title, agency students (no login)
  by agency name or prefix, school students by school name.
  Response `{"q": str, "groups": {<kind>: {"items": [{"id", "code", "label", "detail", "href"}], "more": bool}}}` — only the caller's
  kinds after the filters, each capped at 20 (`more` when there are more). `href` is the caller's page for that student, or `null`.
  403 for a role with no student list (see RBAC §2.202); an agent refused by the approval gate 403 with its reason. 429 + `Retry-After`
  past 30 searches a minute per user. Each search writes one `AuditLog` row (`action=student.search`, `metadata_json` = result counts per
  kind, never the search text).
- `AdminUserManagementPanel` (`/admin/users`, `/it/admin/students`, …) starts with its search set from `?q=` (web only).

## 12GQ. Assignment submission files (nehn-024) — addendum, 2026-10-26

`DEC-SCOPE-292`. Migration `0200_submission_files`. Additive; existing fields and status codes unchanged.

- `POST /workflows/it/assignments/{id}/submissions` — body gains optional `file_ids: UUID[]` (≤ 5; repeats ignored), ids from
  `POST /files/upload?purpose=assignment_submission` (nehn-003, 5 MB, PDF/DOC/DOCX/JPEG/PNG/HEIC, sniffed). Present replaces the
  submission's files; absent keeps them. Any id that is not the caller's own `assignment_submission` upload, or is attached to another
  submission → **422** `"One or more files cannot be attached to this submission"`. `file_ids` counts as "a file" for `file` and
  `text_or_file` assignments. A concurrent submit that collides → **409**. Audit `assignment.submit` metadata gains `file_count`.
- `GET /workflows/it/trainer/submissions` — each row gains `files: [{id, filename, content_type, size, url}]` (`[]` when none).
- `GET /workflows/it/submissions/{submission_id}/files/{file_id}` (new) — the file (attachment unless PDF/image, `nosniff`; 307 to a
  presigned URL in S3 mode). 401 anonymous; 403 not the submitter / batch trainer / it_admin / super_admin; 404 unknown submission or a
  file not attached to it.
- `GET /portal/it/student/{assignments|projects}` — rows gain `submission_type` (not a table column).

## 12GR. Learning material files and management (nehn-027) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-294`. Migration `0202_learning_resource_files`. The existing create route keeps its path, response keys and status codes.

- `POST /workflows/it/trainer/materials` — body takes **exactly one** of `url` (now `^https?://`, ≤ 500) or `file_id` (an id from
  `POST /files/upload?purpose=learning_material`: nehn-003, 20 MB, PDF/DOC/DOCX/XLSX/PPTX/JPEG/PNG, sniffed); both or neither → **422**.
  A `file_id` that is not the caller's own `learning_material` upload, or is already attached to a material → **422** `"This file cannot be
  attached to a material"`. `resource_type` `video`/`recording` with a file → **422** `"Upload videos to YouTube or Google Drive and add
  the link"`. For a file the response `url` is the download route below. Audit `learning_resource.create` metadata gains `kind`.
- `GET /workflows/it/trainer/materials?batch_id=` (new) — live materials: trainer → own batches, it_admin → all; ordered `sort_order`,
  then newest. Rows `{id, batch_id, batch, title, resource_type, url, sort_order, file: {filename, content_type, size} | null,
  created_at}`. 401 anonymous; 403 other roles.
- `PATCH /workflows/it/trainer/materials/{id}` (new) — any of `title`, `resource_type`, `url` (link materials only, else 422),
  `sort_order` (0-100000); an empty body → 422. Returns the row. 403 another trainer's batch; 404 unknown or deleted. Audit
  `learning_resource.update` (`fields`).
- `DELETE /workflows/it/trainer/materials/{id}` (new) — **204**, soft (`deleted_at`); repeat → 404. Audit `learning_resource.delete`.
- `GET /workflows/it/materials/{id}/file` (new) — the file (attachment unless PDF/image, `nosniff`; 307 to a presigned URL in S3 mode)
  for an it_student enrolled in the batch, the batch's trainer, it_admin, super_admin. **404** for anyone else signed in and for a
  deleted, link-only or unknown material; 401 anonymous.
- Deleted materials are hidden from `GET /portal/it/student/downloads`, `GET /workflows/it/student/learning`, the trainer `materials` and
  admin `resources` portal sections; their `url` for a file material is the download route. The student `downloads` URL column has
  `type: "link"`.

  picker offers. Existing fields and status codes unchanged.

## 12GT. Admission interviews (nehn-037 part 4) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-296`. Migration `0203_application_interviews`. New routes only; nothing existing changes.

- `GET /workflows/overseas/applications/{id}/interviews` → `{interviews: [{id, application_id, scheduled_at, mode, mode_label, location,
  student_note, outcome, outcome_label, updated_at, recorded_by?}]}`, latest first. Readers as the offers' list: counselor (own), Overseas
  Admin, the student (own), the agency (its applications). `recorded_by` for counselor / admin only.
- `POST /workflows/overseas/applications/{id}/interviews` (201) — body `{scheduled_at (offset required), mode: online|in_person|phone,
  location? ≤300, student_note? ≤500, outcome?: passed|not_successful|cancelled}` → `{interview, changed: true}`.
- `PATCH /workflows/overseas/applications/{id}/interviews/{interview_id}` — only the keys sent (null clears location / note / outcome;
  `scheduled_at` and `mode` cannot be null) → `{interview, changed}`; nothing different → 200 `changed: false`, nothing written.
- Writes: counselor of the application (direct and agency) or Overseas Admin; 403 other roles / other counselors; 404 an interview of
  another application; 409 withdrawn / terminal application or archived agency record; 422 a result before the interview time
  (`"A result can be recorded only after the interview date -- or mark it cancelled"`), the 21st interview, bad mode / outcome / length /
  time without offset or outside 2000–2100. Audit `overseas.application.interview` `{interview_id, action, fields, outcome}`.
- `GET /workflows/overseas/student/interviews` → `{items: [... same fields without recorded_by, + application_code, university]}` — the
  signed-in overseas student's own applications only (max 200); other roles 403.

## 12GU. Coordinator certificate workflow (nehn-031) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-297`. Migration `0204_certificate_workflow`. All under `/workflows/it/certificates`.

- `GET /eligibility/{enrollment_id}` — the coordinator is also admitted; the body gains `rules: {min_progress_percent,
  min_attendance_percent}` (the thresholds used) and `certificate_issued: bool`. `eligible` now compares against those thresholds.
- `GET /rules` (new) — `{min_progress_percent, min_attendance_percent}`. it_admin, coordinator, trainer, super_admin; others 403.
- `PUT /rules` (new) — same body, each 0–100, unknown fields 422. Needs `certificates.configure` (403 "You do not have permission to
  change certificate rules"). Audit `certificate_rules.update` with before/after. Applies to the next evaluation.
- `POST /{enrollment_id}/recommend` (new) — `{status: "recommended" | "not_ready", comment?}` (≤ 1000; required for `not_ready`, else 422).
  Only the batch's trainer (403 otherwise). 404 unknown enrolment; 409 a certificate already exists. 200 `{enrollment_id, status, comment}`;
  replaces any earlier one. Audit `certificate.recommended` / `certificate.not_ready`.
- `GET /queue` (new) — needs `certificates.issue`. `{can_override, rules, items: [{enrollment_id, student, batch, program, eligible,
  criteria, recommendation: {status, comment, by, at} | null}]}`: enrolments without a certificate (not pending consent, rejected,
  withdrawn or dropped) that pass the progress threshold or carry a recommendation/flag; recommended first; at most 200.
- `POST /issue` (new, bulk) — `{enrollment_ids: UUID[1..50], override?: bool, override_reason?: str ≤ 1000}`, unknown fields 422. Needs
  `certificates.issue`; `override: true` without `certificates.override` → 403 for the whole request. 200 `{issued: [{enrollment_id, id,
  certificate_no, verification_code, file_url, status}], failed: [{enrollment_id, status_code, detail}]}` — each enrolment is checked
  and issued on its own (404 unknown, 409 not eligible without override or already issued, 422 override without a reason).
- `POST /{enrollment_id}/issue` — **now needs `certificates.issue`** (trainer and it_admin 403, `DEC-ROLE-003`); `override: true` needs
  `certificates.override` (403). A second issue for the same enrolment is **409** (was 201 with the existing certificate). Body and 201
  response unchanged; `override_reason` is capped at 1000 characters. Eligibility is re-checked at issue time.

## 12GV. Lead list filters and filtered Excel export (nehn-012) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-298`. Migration `0205_enquiry_list_indexes` (indexes only).

- `GET /admin/leads`, `GET /telecaller/leads` — new optional query parameters (additive; responses unchanged):
  `created_from`, `created_to` (ISO dates, inclusive India-time days on the lead's creation time) and `owner_id` (the assigned
  counselor). Every filter is ANDed with the existing scope. **422** for a malformed date or id, or `created_from` after `created_to`.
- `GET /admin/leads.xlsx` (new) — `ensure_admin` roles, the list's division scope and the same query parameters (`limit`/`offset`
  ignored): every matching lead, newest first, as one `.xlsx` sheet "Leads" (`leads-<IST date>.xlsx`, `Cache-Control: private,
  no-store`). Columns: Lead ID, Name, Phone, Email, Source, Course interest, Stage, Telecaller, Counselor, Created, Last contact, Next
  follow-up (times in India time). **401** anonymous; **403** other roles; **422** over 5,000 rows ("narrow the filters"); **429** with
  `Retry-After` past 30 exports in 10 minutes. One audit row per file: action `crm.export`, `entity_type` `lead_list`, `entity_id`
  `admin`, metadata `{format, filters, rows}` (search text recorded only as `"q": true`). A refused export writes nothing.
- `GET /telecaller/leads.xlsx` (new) — the same for the telecaller list's scope and filters; telecaller_manager (own reports' leads and
  their teams' unassigned leads) and super_admin. A telecaller or any other role **403**; `entity_id` `telecaller`.

## 12GW. Per-user permission overrides (nehn-007) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-299`; migration `0206_user_permission_overrides`. All three routes are super_admin only (401 without a session, 403 for any
other role, checked before the target).

- `GET /admin/users/{user_id}/permissions` → 200 `{user:{id, full_name, email, role}, editable, role_permissions:[key],
  overrides:[{permission, effect:"allow"|"deny", reason, expires_at|null, created_at}], effective:[key]}`. Only overrides in force are
  listed. `editable` is false for a super_admin user. 404 unknown user; 422 malformed id.
- `PUT /admin/users/{user_id}/permissions` body `{overrides:[{permission, effect, reason, expires_at?}]}` (≤ 200 items; extra fields
  forbidden) replaces the user's whole override set → 200 with the GET body. 422: unknown key, duplicate key, effect not allow/deny, blank
  or > 500-character reason, `expires_at` without a timezone or not in the future. 404 unknown user. 409 a super_admin target, or a
  concurrent edit of the same user. A real change writes one `audit_logs` row (`user_permission_overrides.update`, entity `user`,
  before/after); an unchanged set writes nothing.
- `GET /admin/permissions/overrides` → 200 `[{user_id, full_name, email, role, permission, effect, reason, expires_at, created_at}]`,
  every override in force, by user name then key.
- Changed: `GET /auth/me/permissions` and every `require_permission` route (and the portal payments / certificates sections) now resolve
  the caller's effective set: (role grants + active allows) - active denies. Response shapes unchanged; with no overrides every answer is
  as before.

## 12GX. Field (column) policy (nehn-008) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-300`; migration `0207_field_financial_grant`.

- New catalogue key `field.financial.view` ("Financial columns"), listed by `GET /admin/permissions/catalogue`, editable through
  `PUT /admin/roles/{role}/permissions` and the per-user overrides (§12GW), reported by `GET /auth/me/permissions`.
- Changed: `GET /partnership/reports/{kind}`, `.csv` and `.xlsx` — the `commission_expected` / `commission_received` columns (Performance)
  are returned only when the caller holds `field.financial.view` (as well as the U2 rule, unchanged). A denied column is absent from
  `columns`, every item and `totals`, in the JSON and in both files alike. Routes, statuses and every other column are unchanged; with the
  seed every reader gets the same columns as before.

## 12GY. CRM report gaps (nehn-013) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-301`. No migration. Additive only: new `kind` values and one query parameter on the existing §12AC routes
(`GET /telecaller/reports/{kind}`, `.csv`, `.xlsx`); every existing kind keeps its columns in order.

- Funnel kinds `source`, `product`, `campaign` append `lost` ("Lost") and `conversion_rate` ("Conversion rate (%)", a number with 1
  decimal; the `totals` row recomputes it from the totals).
- `kind=counselor` — rows `{counselor, leads, counselling_done, enrolled, lost, conversion_rate}`: cohort leads with an owner, by owner.
- `kind=date` — rows `{period, leads, connected, qualified, counselling, enrolled, lost, conversion_rate}` in calendar order. New query
  parameter `group=day|week|month` (default `day`; IST; weeks start Monday; labels `2026-03-02`, `Week of 2026-03-02`, `March 2026`).
  Any other value → 422 `"Choose day, week or month"`. Other kinds accept and ignore `group`; when set, the export audit lists it.
- `kind=follow_ups` — rows `{telecaller, follow_ups, completed, open, overdue, cancelled}`: telecaller-side follow-ups on the caller's
  leads due in the range, by the lead's telecaller.
- `kind=pending_follow_ups` — rows `{lead, telecaller, due (IST date), reason (label), overdue_days}`: open follow-ups due in the range
  and before now, most overdue first; `totals.overdue_days` is `""`.
- Lead filters (`team`, `product_id`, `campaign_id`, `source`) apply to all four new kinds. Errors and order unchanged (403 → 404 → 422).

## 12GZ. Certificate signatures (nehn-032) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-302`. Migration `0208_certificate_signatories`. Every route needs `certificates.manage` (403 "You do not have permission to
manage certificate signatures"; super_admin only until granted). A signatory is `{id, role: "founder" | "coordinator", display_name,
designation, active, active_from, active_to}`; the storage key is never returned.

- `GET /admin/certificate-signatories` (new) — `{items: [signatory]}`: every version, founder then coordinator, the active one first, then
  newest first; at most 100.
- `POST /admin/certificate-signatories` (new, multipart) — `role`, `display_name`, `designation` (each trimmed, 1–120, else 422), `file`.
  The file passes the nehn-003 `signature` policy (PNG only 415, ≤ 1 MB 413, bytes must be PNG 415), then must decode (422), be 50–2000 px
  wide and 20–1000 px high (422) and have an alpha channel (422 "The signature must have a transparent background"). Text chunks are
  stripped. 201 `signatory`: the new active version; the slot's previous version gets `active_to` (409 if a concurrent upload won the
  slot). Audit `certificate_signatory.upload`.
- `PATCH /admin/certificate-signatories/{id}` (new) — `{display_name?, designation?}` (≤ 120; unknown fields 422). Edits the active version
  in place; 404 unknown, 409 "This signature version is no longer active". Audit `certificate_signatory.update`.
- `GET /admin/certificate-signatories/{id}/image` (new) — `image/png`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `nosniff`. 404 unknown/missing.
- `GET /admin/certificates/preview` (new) — `application/pdf` (inline, `no-store`): the layout with the active signatures and long sample
  text; nothing stored. 409 "Certificate signatures are not configured: …" when a slot is empty.
- `POST /workflows/it/certificates/{enrollment_id}/issue` and `POST /workflows/it/certificates/issue` — **now 409** "Certificate
  signatures are not configured: <slots>" (per item in bulk) while a slot is empty. Issued certificates draw both signatures, names and
  designations and record `signatory_snapshot`. Response bodies unchanged.

## 12HA. Report library (nehn-009) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-303`; migration `0210_training_report_grants`. New router `/api/v1/reports`; every response is `Cache-Control: private, no-store`.

- `GET /reports` (signed in) → `{items: [{id, title, description, filters, can_download}]}`: only the reports whose `<resource>.view` the
  caller holds (role grant ± nehn-007 override). A caller holding none gets `{items: []}`.
- `GET /reports/{id}` → `{id, title, description, columns, items, totals: null, total, page, page_size, filters, applied, options}`.
  `items` are the requested page of the report's rows in its default order; `total` counts every matching row (at most `latest`);
  `applied` echoes the filters given; `options` are the pickers within the caller's scope (`courses`, `batches`, `trainers`, `statuses`
  as `{value, label}`). Columns pass through the nehn-008 field policy.
- `GET /reports/{id}.csv` / `.xlsx` → every matching row as an attachment `"<id>-<IST date>.<ext>"`, the same columns and rows as the JSON.
- Errors, in this order: unknown id **404** `Report not found`; no grant **403** `You don't have permission to view this report` (read) /
  `… to download this report` (files); inputs **422** with a sentence naming the field (an undeclared or unknown filter: `This report can't
  be filtered by <Label>`; `'From' must be on or before 'To'`; `Enter <Label> as a date (YYYY-MM-DD)`; `Choose a <label> from the list`;
  `Latest must be a whole number from 1 to 1,000`; `Give <Label> once`); a download over 10,000 rows **422** `This report has more than
  10,000 rows; narrow the filters`; over 30 downloads in 10 minutes **429** with `Retry-After`.
- Audit: each download writes `AuditLog(action="report.export", entity_type="report", entity_id=<id>, metadata_json={filters: {name: value},
  rows, format})` before the file is returned; reads and refused downloads write nothing.
- Registered reports: `enrollments` (resource `reports.training`) — columns Enrollment code, Student code, Student, Course, Batch, Trainer,
  Enrolled on, Batch ends, Status, Progress %; every standard filter except `recruiter_id`.

## 12HB. Student feature access by payment status (nehn-018) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-304`. Migration `0211_student_access_rules`.

- `GET /workflows/it/student/access` (new) — it_student: `{state, features: [{key, label, unlocked, required_state}]}`. **403** other roles.
- `GET /workflows/it/student-access/rules`, `PUT` (new) — `student_access.configure` (it_admin; super_admin implicit), else **403**.
  Body/response `{rules: {<feature>: <state>}}` / `{rules: [{feature, label, min_state}], states: [{key, label}]}`. The PUT replaces the
  whole policy: a missing feature, an unknown feature or state is **422**. Audited `student_access_rules.update` with before and after.
- Gated for it_student below the feature's state (**403** `detail = {message, code: "payment_required", feature, label, required_state,
  current_state}`; checked before body validation): `GET /workflows/it/student/attendance`, `POST .../attendance-corrections`
  (attendance); `POST /it/assignments/{id}/submissions` (assignments); `POST /it/assessments/{id}/attempts`,
  `POST /it/assessment-attempts/{id}/submit` (assessments); `GET`/`POST /it/student/questions` (questions); `POST /it/student/feedback`
  (feedback); `GET /communications/it/live-sessions` (live_classes). After the existing ownership check: the student's own
  `GET /it/submissions/{id}/files/{fid}` (assignments), `GET /it/materials/{id}/file` (resources), `GET /it/certificates/{id}/download`
  (certificates). Every other role is unchanged.
- `GET /portal/it/student/<section>` — a locked section (attendance, assignments, projects, examinations, downloads, questions,
  feedback, certificates) answers 200 with no rows, metrics, actions or panels and an additive `locked: {feature, label,
  required_state, current_state}`; the dashboard withholds its upcoming-work rows while assignments are locked.
- `GET /workflows/it/student/learning` — locked features' lists are empty; additive `access` key.

## 12HC. Training reports pack (nehn-010) — addendum, 2026-10-27

`DEC-SCOPE-305`. Seven reports registered in the §12HA report library; every route, gate (`reports.training.view` / `.download`),
error order, paging, throttle, cap and audit row is §12HA's. `GET /api/v1/reports/{id}` (+ `.csv`, `.xlsx`):

| id | Grain | Columns | Filters |
|---|---|---|---|
| `student-courses` | enrolment | student_code, student, course, batch, trainer, enrolled_on, status, progress, attendance_pct, assignments_graded, assignments_total, assessments_passed, assessments_total, certificate | from, to (enrolled), course_id, batch_id, trainer_id, student, status, latest, completion_from, completion_to |
| `students-by-trainer` | enrolment | trainer, batch, course, student_code, student, status, progress | from, to (enrolled), course_id, batch_id, trainer_id, student, status |
| `course-completion` | batch | course, batch, trainer, batch_starts, batch_ends, students, completed, completion_pct, certificates | course_id, batch_id, trainer_id, completion_from, completion_to |
| `completions` | completed enrolment | student_code, student, course, batch, trainer, batch_ends, progress, attendance_pct, certificate | completion_from, completion_to, course_id, batch_id, trainer_id, student, latest |
| `attendance` | enrolment | student_code, student, course, batch, trainer, marked, present, absent, late, excused, attendance_pct | from, to (**class date**), course_id, batch_id, trainer_id, student, status |
| `trainers` | trainer | trainer, batches, students, attendance_pct, average_progress, pending_grading | course_id, trainer_id, completion_from, completion_to |
| `courses` | course | course, batches, enrolments, active, completed, dropped, revenue (`financial`) | from, to (enrolled), course_id, trainer_id |

- Percentages are numbers (attendance 2 decimals, completion/average 1); `null` when the base is 0. `certificate` is the enrolment's
  latest certificate status or `"Not issued"`. `revenue` is omitted (JSON and files) without `field.financial.view`.
- `GET /workflows/it/certificates/eligibility/{id}` is unchanged in shape; its attendance/assignment/assessment figures now come from the
  same calculator as the `attendance` report.

days on the day the students' agency records were created).

  `unassigned_students` are narrowed to the cohort. `pending_documents`, `pending_actions` and `commission` are not.

  the reason and the admin. A reason over 500 characters is **422**. Response adds `gross_amount`, `discount_amount`.

- `GET /payments/mine` — each row adds `gross_amount`, `discount_amount`, `paid_at` (never the reason).
- `GET /payments/emi-schedule` — each schedule adds `paid_amount`, `balance` (net); each instalment adds `discount_amount`, `paid_at`.

  student, student code, email, phone, course, batch, reference, gross amount, discount, amount paid, remaining balance, payment status
  ("Paid" / "Partially paid"); "—" where unknown. Issued once, never regenerated.
