- `GET /counselor/students` (§12EM) — now lists every `overseas_student` in the caller's caseload (csc-001 link ∪ the students of
  applications with `counselor_id == me`), not archived, each once, ordered `linked_since` desc then `id`. Additive query params:
  `source` ∈ `walk_in | lead_conversion | self_registered | agency` (anything else **422**); `q` (≤ 100 chars, else **422**) — literal,
  case-insensitive substring of name / email / student code, or the phone's digits when the search is phone-shaped; blank = no filter.
  `total` follows the filters. Each item adds `source`, `applications` (count of all the student's applications), `linked_since`
  (earliest of the caller's link and the caller's first application) and `linked` (the csc-001 link is the caller's — only then do the
  §12EN resend/edit routes apply). Existing item keys and `POST`/`PATCH` responses are unchanged.

## 12HP. Counselor document upload on the student 360 (ccrm-045) — addendum, 2026-10-28

`DEC-SCOPE-318`. No migration.

- `POST /counselor/students/{student_id}/documents` — **new**, `multipart/form-data`: `document_type` (Passport, Academic certificates,
  Transcripts, English test, CV, SOP, LOR, Financial documents, Other, Offer letter), `document_label` (only and required for Other, 2-80
  characters), `application_id` (optional; required for Offer letter), `file` (PDF/JPEG/PNG sniffed from the bytes, at most 20 MB,
  image metadata stripped, server-generated storage key). **201** `{"document": {id, name, document_type, application_id, status}}`
  (`status` `pending`). Errors: **403** another role, **404** a student outside the counselor's caseload, **409** archived student or a
  withdrawn / terminally decided application, **403** another counselor's application, **422** label / type / application rules (another
  student's application, offer letter without one), empty or unreadable image, **413** too large, **415** not a PDF/JPEG/PNG. Writes the
  document, an `uploaded` document event and a `document.upload` audit row (uploader, type, application) in one transaction; the stored
  file is discarded if it fails.
- `GET /workflows/overseas/documents/{document_id}/download` — a counselor's document without an application: allowed when the student
  is in the counselor's caseload (was: when the counselor has one of the student's applications). Other rules unchanged.
- `GET /counselor/students/{student_id}/documents` — `can_download` is `true` for the counselor on every document without an
  application (the tab's gate already proved the caseload).
- `GET /portal/overseas/student/offer-letters` — also lists an application with a current "Offer letter" document; its `offer` column is
  the legacy URL or the document's name.

## 12HT. Deposit receipt upload on the student 360's Finance tab (ccrm-048) — addendum, 2026-10-28

`DEC-SCOPE-322`. No migration.

- `POST /workflows/overseas/applications/{application_id}/deposit/receipt` — **new**, `multipart/form-data`: `file` (PDF/JPEG/PNG sniffed
  from the bytes, at most 20 MB, server-generated storage key — ccrm-025's deposit-receipt rules). Stores a "Deposit receipt" document on
  the application and sets the counselor deposit record's `receipt_document_id` (and `updated_by`) in one transaction; a replaced receipt
  stays one of the student's documents. **201** `{"record": <GET /deposit's record>}`. Errors: **401**; **403** another role or another
  counselor's application; **404** unknown application; **409** withdrawn / terminally decided application, archived agency record,
  archived student, or an AGN-011 agency deposit; **422** no deposit record or `required=false` ("Record a required deposit on the
  application first"), empty file; **413** too large; **415** not a PDF/JPEG/PNG. Also writes a status-history note ("Deposit receipt
  uploaded." / "replaced."), the student's "Deposit receipt added" notice (and the agency side's, on an agency application), the
  `document.upload` and `overseas.application.deposit_receipt` (`replaced`, `document_id`) audit rows. The stored file is discarded if
  the transaction fails.
- `GET /counselor/students/{student_id}/finance` — each deposit gains `can_upload` (additive): `true` only for a counselor-recorded,
  required deposit on an open application of a non-archived student, for that application's counselor or the Overseas Admin; always
  `false` on an agency deposit.

  the bytes, at most 20 MB, server-generated storage key — the AGN-009 upload rules). Stores a pending "Payment receipt" document of the
  student (no application; label = the payment's description) and sets the payment's `proof_document_id` in one transaction, the payment
  row locked; amount and status never change; a replaced proof stays one of the student's documents. **201**
  `{"payment": <the finance tab's payment row>}`. Errors: **401**; **403** another role; **404** unknown student or a counselor outside
  the caseload (the 360's gate), a payment that is not this student's or is an AGN-011 agency deposit ("Payment not found"); **409**
  archived student, a gateway (non-manual) payment ("An online payment already has its system receipt"); **422** empty file; **413** too
  large; **415** not a PDF/JPEG/PNG. Writes the `document.upload` and `payment.proof_attached` (`replaced`, `document_id`, `student_id`)
  audit rows. The stored file is discarded if the transaction fails.

  `can_upload_proof` (`true` for a manual payment of a non-archived student). The system `Receipt` (`receipt_no`) is unchanged.

  empty; never a file key. The file is downloaded from the student's Documents.

## 12IB. Trainer question upload from a standard Excel template (nehn-025) — addendum, 2026-10-29

`DEC-SCOPE-330`. No migration.

- `GET /workflows/it/trainer/assessments/question-template.xlsx` — trainer / it_admin (IT division) → **200**
  `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet`, `attachment; filename="question-template.xlsx"`. 401 signed out,
  403 any other role.
- `POST /workflows/it/trainer/assessments/{id}/questions/import?commit=false|true` — multipart `file` (`.xlsx` or `.csv`).
  - Gates, in order: 401; 403 role / division; 404 unknown assessment; 403 another trainer's batch; **409** a student has started an
    attempt; then the file: **415** not `.xlsx`/`.csv` or macro-enabled, **413** over 1 MB, **422** unreadable file, unknown / repeated /
    missing (`type`, `question`) columns, more than 500 rows, or `"The file has no questions"` — each a string `detail` (messages joined
    with `; `).
  - Columns: `type` (`mcq_single|mcq_multiple|text|file`, case-insensitive), `question` (2-10,000 chars), `option_1`..`option_6`,
    `correct` (option numbers, e.g. `2` or `1,3`), `marks` (whole number 1-10,000, default 1), `keywords`, `explanation` (accepted, not stored).
  - Dry run (default) → **200** `{total_rows, questions: [{row, question_type, prompt, options, correct_answers, max_score}], errors: [{row,
    message}], warnings: [{row | null, message}], created: 0}`. Nothing is written.
  - `commit=true` with no row errors → **201**, same body with `created` = the number written; positions continue after the current
    highest; one `assessment.questions_import` audit row `{count, format}`. With row errors → **422** `"N row(s) has/have errors -- fix them
    and upload the file again. Nothing was imported."`; nothing is written.

## 12IC. University logo upload and richer university cards (nehn-035) — addendum, 2026-10-29

`DEC-SCOPE-331`. No migration (reuses `universities.logo_url`).

- `PUT /partnership/universities/{id}/logo` — multipart `file`. The `PATCH` rule (session → **401**; `can_edit` + scope → **403**; unknown
  id **404**; inactive **409**). Then the nehn-003 `university_logo` policy: size 1 B-1 MB else **413**; type PNG / JPEG / WebP and matching
  magic bytes else **415** (SVG is always 415); width and height 32-2048 px, read from the header, else **422**. Stores the file, sets
  `logo_url` to `/api/v1/public/university-logos/<32 hex>.<png|jpg|webp>`, audits `university.logo` `{content_type, size, replaced}` and
  returns `UniversityEnvelope` (as `PATCH`). The previous uploaded logo is deleted after the commit.
- `GET /public/university-logos/{name}` — no session. `name` must be `<32 lowercase hex>.<png|jpg|webp>`, else **404**; a missing file is
  **404**. `Content-Type` from the extension, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`.
- `logo_url` (university create / `PATCH`) additionally accepts the uploaded shape above; everything else it accepted or refused is
  unchanged.
- `GET /public/universities` — each row gains `programs_count` (active overseas courses), `country_name`, `country_slug`, `country_iso2`.
  Other routes returning `UniversityOut` carry the defaults (`0` / `null`).

  - `next_actions[]`: `text, href | null`.

## 12IE. Keyword-based auto-scoring for written answers (nehn-026) — addendum, 2026-10-29

`DEC-SCOPE-333`. Migration `0218_assessment_keyword_scoring` (`assessment_questions.keywords` JSON NOT NULL default `[]`;
`assessment_answers.auto_score` NUMERIC(10,2) NULL, `score_source` VARCHAR(10) NULL). All changes are additive.

- `AssessmentQuestionIn` (single `POST .../questions`, bulk `.../questions/bulk`, nehn-025 import) gains `keywords: string[]` (default
  `[]`; trimmed, blanks dropped; ≤30 entries, each ≤100 chars; `|` separates synonyms). **422** when keywords are set on a non-`text`
  question ("Keywords can only be set on a written-answer (text) question").
- `GET /workflows/it/trainer/assessments/{id}/questions` — each question adds `keywords`.
- `POST .../questions/import` — preview questions add `keywords`; a filled `keywords` cell is saved (comma separated). Row errors: keywords on
  a non-text row, more than 30 keywords, a keyword over 100 characters. The unsaved-columns warning now names only `explanation`.
- `POST /workflows/it/assessment-attempts/{id}/submit` — response unchanged. A text answer to a keyword question is scored ≥80% matched →
  full marks, else proportional; the attempt stays `submitted` when any text/file question exists (unchanged).
- `GET /workflows/it/trainer/assessment-attempts/{id}` — each answer adds `auto_score` (number | null), `score_source`
  (`mcq | keyword | trainer | null`), `keywords` (string[]), `matched_keywords` (string[]), `match_percent` (number | null when no keywords).
- `PATCH /workflows/it/trainer/assessment-attempts/{id}` — request unchanged; a per-answer score different from the stored one sets
  `score_source = "trainer"`; the `assessment.attempt_grade` audit metadata adds `overrides` (keyword answers whose suggestion was replaced).
- Student routes (`POST .../attempts`, submit, `GET .../student/learning`) never include `keywords`, `auto_score` or `matched_keywords`.

`DEC-SCOPE-334`. Migration `0220_program_syllabus_image` (`programs.image_url`, `syllabus_source` (`manual|link|pdf|NULL`, CHECK),
`syllabus_modules` JSON default `[]`, `syllabus_url`, `syllabus_pdf_url`). Additive.

  `syllabus`: `{source: "manual", modules: [{title, topics[]}]}` | `{source: "link" | "pdf", url}` | `null`.
- `GET /public/program-files/{name}` (new, **no auth**) — `name` = 32 hex + `.png|.jpg|.webp|.pdf`. **200** only while an active program
  uses it as `image_url` or as its current PDF syllabus; **404** otherwise. `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Cache-Control: public,
  max-age=3600`; a PDF adds `Content-Disposition: inline; filename="<slug>-syllabus.pdf"`.

    Sets `source = "pdf"`, replaces the previous PDF (deleted after commit). Audit `program.syllabus`.

    metadata stripped (PNG/JPEG). Replaces the previous image. `DELETE /admin/programs/{id}/image` removes it. Audit `program.image`.

## 12IG. Field-change audit: before and after values (itc-001) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-335`. No migration (uses `audit_logs.metadata_json`). Authorization unchanged.

- `GET /admin/audit` — each row adds `changes` (additive): `{field: [old, new] | "changed"}` or `null`. `"changed"` stands for a
  personal-data field (phone, email, notes, remarks, reason, or a name ending in `_phone`, `_email`, … ), whose values are never stored.
  Only the dict shape written by `services/audit_changes` is surfaced; older rows that keep a list under `metadata.changes` (BDM and
  partnership targets, school attendance) return `null`. `metadata` itself is unchanged.
- `POST /workflows/it/trainer/attendance` — when a mark overwrites an existing one and its status or note changes, one extra audit row per
  such student: `action = "attendance.update"`, `entity_type = "attendance"`, `entity_id` = the attendance row, `metadata = {batch_id,
  student_id, session_date, changes}`. An unchanged or first-time mark writes none (Q-02). The `attendance.bulk_mark` row is unchanged.
  The existing row is read `FOR UPDATE`, so the old value is exact under concurrent marking.
- `PATCH /admin/leads/{id}` — the existing `lead.update` row adds `changes` (`status`, `owner_id`) only when one of them actually changed.
- A blank string and `null` are the same empty value (no change between them). Everything is written in the request's transaction, so a
  rollback leaves no audit row.

## 12IH. Notification event type and record link (itc-002) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-336`. Migration `0221_notification_event_type` (`notifications.event_type`, `entity_type` and `entity_id`, all nullable, plus
index `ix_notifications_user_read_created`). Additive.

- `GET /workflows/notifications` — each item adds `event_type` (string | null, a key of `app/notifications/events.py` `EVENTS`, e.g.
  `it.fee.payment_received`), `entity_type` (string | null, e.g. `payment`) and `entity_id` (string | null). Older notices return null for
  all three. Auth and scope are unchanged: the caller's own newest 100 notices.
- `GET /portal/it/student/notifications` (new section) — returns the header-only payload `{title: "Notifications", rows: []}` for an
  `it_student`. The page lists the notices from the read above. The section is never fee-locked.
- Side effect: issuing a new receipt to an `it_student` (`POST /admin/payments` with a paid status, or `POST /payments/{id}/verify` / the
  Razorpay webhook) creates one `it.fee.payment_received` notice. Its text is fixed and contains no amount. Delivery is in-app and by
  email, plus WhatsApp/SMS only if the student opted in. A rolled-back write sends nothing.

## 12II. Counselor ↔ IT student ownership link (itc-003) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-337`. Migration `0222_it_student_counselors` (one row per IT student, unique `student_user_id`; backfilled from linked IT
leads owned by an IT counselor). All routes **401** without a session.

- `GET /counselor/it/students?q=&limit=50&offset=0` (IT counselor only; **403** anyone else, an overseas counselor included) →
  `{items: [{id, student_code, full_name, email, phone, linked_since}], total, limit, offset}`, newest link first; `q` (≤100) is a literal
  substring of name, email or Student ID; `limit` 1–100 (**422** outside).
- `GET /counselor/it/students/{id}` (IT counselor only) → one item as above; **404** unless the student is linked to the caller.
- `PUT /admin/it/students/{id}/counselor` (IT Admin only; **403** anyone else, super_admin included) body `{counselor_id, reason?}`
  (`reason` ≤500, trimmed) → `{id, counselor_id, counselor_name, changed}`. **404** not an IT student; **422** not an active IT counselor
  ("Choose an active IT counselor") or a replacement without a reason ("Give a reason for the reassignment"). The same counselor →
  `changed: false`, nothing written. Audit `it.student.counselor_assign` `{from_counselor_id, to_counselor_id, reason?}`; in-app notices
  "Student assigned to you" / "Student reassigned" (with the reason, `action_url` `/it/counselor/students`) plus consented channels.
- `GET /lookups/it-counselors?q=&limit=20` (IT Admin only) → `{items: [{id, label, detail: null}], truncated}`, active IT counselors.
- Side effects (no contract change): `POST /counselor/leads/{id}/student-link` and `POST /admin/leads/{id}/conversion` on an IT lead owned
  by an IT counselor link that counselor to a student who has none (audit `it.student.counselor_link` `{counselor_id, lead_id}`);
  `DELETE` of either removes the link while it still points at the lead owner.
- Portal (additive): `GET /portal/it/admin/students` rows gain `counselor`, `counselor_id`, `assign` and the column type
  `assign_it_student_counselor`; `GET /portal/it/counselor/students` ("My Students") is new; `GET /portal/it/student/dashboard` gains the
  metric "Your counselor" (name or "Not assigned yet").

## 12IJ. Lead stage "Nurture" and the derived "Assigned to Counselor" (itc-004) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-338`. Migration `0223_lead_nurture_stage` (the `ck_enquiries_status` CHECK gains `nurture`). Authorization unchanged.

- The lead stage enum (`status`, `to_stage`, every `?status=` / `?stage=` filter) gains `nurture` (label "Nurture"), additive. It is open
  and outside the linear pipeline order.
- `POST /telecaller/leads/{id}/stage` and `PATCH /admin/leads/{id}` — `nurture` is a manual target for a telecaller, manager or admin from
  any open stage before Application/Enrollment; it needs `reason` (**422** at `reason`, "Add a reason for moving the lead to Nurture").
  From Application/Enrollment or Converted it is the existing **422** at `to_stage`. A counselor choosing it is the existing **422**
  ("This stage is set by the system"). From Nurture, the existing manual targets apply (no reason needed).
- System events: booking a counselling appointment, the student link and a counselor's return move a Nurture lead as from an early stage;
  calls do not move it.
- Overdue: a Nurture lead's follow-ups are never `overdue` (`GET /telecaller/follow-ups?view=overdue` and its counts, each follow-up's
  `overdue`, `GET /telecaller/leads?follow_up=overdue`, the dashboard `overdue` tile, the follow-ups report, the counselor dashboard and
  performance); the lead's `next_action_overdue` is `false`; no "Missed follow-up" alert. The "Follow-up due" alert still fires.
- "Assigned to Counselor" is not a stage: clients derive it from `counselor` on an open lead.

## 12IK. Lead distribution by source and workload (itc-006) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-339`. Migration `0224_tel_source_workload` (`tel_distribution_rules.source` + kind `source`, unique per team + source;
`tel_settings.distribution_strategy`, default `round_robin`). Additive.

- `POST /telecaller/distribution-rules` — `kind` also accepts `"source"`; body adds `source` (one of the 13 lead sources, else 422).
  A source rule with no source → 422 `Choose a source`; with a product or city → 422 `A source rule has no product or city`; a product or
  city rule with a source → 422 `A product rule has no source` / `A city rule has no source`; a second rule for the same team + source →
  409 `This team already has a rule for this source`. A telecaller on the other team stays 422.
- `GET /telecaller/distribution-rules` — `kind` filter also accepts `source`; each item adds `source` (string | null).
- `GET /telecaller/distribution-strategy` — telecaller manager / super admin (others 403, signed out 401):
  `{items: [{team, team_label, strategy: "round_robin" | "workload", editable}]}`, one per team.
- `PUT /telecaller/distribution-strategy/{team}` — body `{strategy}` (extra keys 422). 200 with the item; 404 unknown team; 403 unless
  one of the manager's direct reports is on the team (super admin: any). Audited `telecaller.strategy_update` `{from, to}`.
- Side effect on every intake (website, BDM, manager, CSV import): order source → product → city → team strategy. `lead.assign` audit
  metadata adds `rule_id` when a rule matched; methods add `source_rule` and `workload`.

## 12IL. Lead list filters and telecaller dashboard tiles (itc-008) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-340`. No migration; RBAC unchanged. Additive.

- `GET /telecaller/leads` and `GET /telecaller/leads.xlsx` — new query params: `source` (a lead source key, else 422),
  `last_contact_from`, `last_contact_to`, `next_follow_up_from`, `next_follow_up_to` (YYYY-MM-DD, India-time days, inclusive; a malformed
  date or a from after its to → 422). Each is ANDed with the caller's scope. The `crm.export` audit `filters` names them.
- `GET /telecaller/dashboard` — `tiles` adds `contact_rate` (number | null, % to one decimal), `qualified` (int) and `handovers` (int),
  all for today (IST).
- `GET /telecaller/reports/telecaller` (+ `.csv`, `.xlsx`) — columns append `contact_rate` "Contact rate (%)" (number | null per row;
  the Total row is Σreached ÷ Σcalled) and `handovers` "Handovers" (summed).

## 12IM. Merge duplicate leads (itc-007) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-341`. Migration `0225_lead_merge`. RBAC §2.241.

- `GET /telecaller/leads/{id}/merge-preview?victim=LD-…` and `GET /admin/leads/{id}/merge-preview?victim=LD-…` → **200**
  `{survivor, victim, moved}`: each side `{id, lead_code, status, status_label, name, email, phone, whatsapp_number, city, state,
  qualification, passing_year, institution}`; `moved` `{calls, follow_ups, notes, messages, meetings, appointments, enquiries, tasks}`
  (`enquiries` counts the duplicate's own enquiry). Read only.
- `POST /telecaller/leads/{id}/merge` and `POST /admin/leads/{id}/merge`, body `{victim: "LD-…", use_victim?: [field…], reason?}`
  (`extra` forbidden; `use_victim` ⊆ the fields above, else **422**) → **200** `{id, victim_id, victim_code, moved}`.
- Refusals, before any write: either lead outside the caller's scope (telecaller T23 scope; admin's division) or an unknown Lead ID →
  **404**; the lead itself → **422** at `victim`; another team/division → **409**; either lead closed → **409**; the duplicate linked to a
  student or at Application/Enrollment / Converted → **409**; a telecaller → **403** ("Only a manager can merge leads").
- Lead responses gain `merged_at` (additive; null unless merged). A merged lead's `read_only` is `true` for every role, and
  `POST /telecaller/leads/{id}/stage`, `PATCH /telecaller/leads/{id}`, `PUT /telecaller/leads/{id}/qualification` and
  `PATCH /admin/leads/{id}` on it are **409** ("This lead was merged into another lead, so it can't be changed").
- `GET /telecaller/leads/{id}/timeline` (and the admin/counselor readers of the same service) gains kind `merge` (`from_value` = the merged
  Lead ID).

## 12IN. Overdue follow-up escalation (itc-009) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-342`. Migration `0226_follow_up_escalation` (`tel_settings.escalate_after_hours` 0-168, default 0;
`lead_follow_ups.escalated_at`). Additive.

- `GET /telecaller/settings` — each item adds `escalate_after_hours` (int, 0 = off).
- `PUT /telecaller/settings/{team}` — body may add `escalate_after_hours` (StrictInt 0-168; a string, fraction, null or out-of-range value
  → 422 `Escalate overdue follow-ups after (hours): …`). Omitted keeps the stored value. The `tel.settings.update` audit `from`/`to` carry
  all three fields.
- `GET /portal/it/admin/notifications` — IT admin only (others 404 as any unknown section): header-only payload; the page reads
  `GET /workflows/notifications`.
- Beat `itc009-escalations` (900 s): notices of event `it.followup.overdue`, entity `lead_follow_up`.

## 12IO. IT counseling record (itc-010) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-343`. Migration `0227_lead_counseling_records`. Additive.

- `GET /counselor/leads/{id}/counseling?limit&offset` — `{items, total, limit, offset}`, newest first. The lead's counselor (tel-018
  scope); out of scope 404.
- `POST /counselor/leads/{id}/counseling` → 201 record. Body: `outcome` (required: `interested | needs_time | not_interested | enrolled`),
  `recommended_product_id` (an active IT `TelProduct`), `objection_ids` (≤ 20 active objections), `objection_notes` (≤ 1000),
  `fee_quoted` (decimal ≥ 0, 2 places), `expected_joining_date` and `next_follow_up` (today IST or later), `decision_maker`
  (`self | parent | spouse | employer | other`), `notes` (≤ 4000). Blank text is null. Not an IT counselor 403; closed lead 409;
  invalid 422 on the field. Not idempotent.
- `PATCH /counselor/leads/{id}/counseling/{record_id}` → 200 record. Fields sent replace the stored ones (`objection_ids` the whole set);
  `outcome: null` is 422; the C9 checks apply to changed values only; a record of another lead 404.
- `GET /telecaller/leads/{id}/counseling` — the same page, read only, for a lead in the tel-004 scope.
- Record: `id, recommended_product {id, name} | null, objections [{id, name}], objection_notes, fee_quoted (string) | null,
  expected_joining_date, decision_maker, outcome, next_follow_up, notes, author {id, full_name}, updated_by {id, full_name}, created_at,
  updated_at`.
- `GET/POST /telecaller/counseling-objections`, `PATCH /telecaller/counseling-objections/{id}` — the lost-reason list contract (ccrm-006):
  `{id, name, active, sort_order}`; a duplicate name (case-insensitive) is 409.
- Lead timeline: new kind `counseling` (`event` `created | updated`, `to_value` = the outcome).

## 12IP. Batch timetable and session calendar (itc-020) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-344`. Migration `0228_batch_timetable`. RBAC §2.244.

- Slot body `{weekday 0–6 (0 = Monday), start_time "HH:MM", end_time "HH:MM", mode "online"|"offline", room?, meeting_url?, topic?}`:
  local times in the batch's timezone; `end_time > start_time`; `offline` needs a `room` (≤120); `meeting_url` must be `http(s)://…`
  (≤500); `topic` ≤160; blank strings are null. Any breach → **422**.
- `GET /it/batches/{id}/timetable` → **200** `{batch_id, items: [slot]}`, slot `{id, batch_id, weekday, day, start_time, end_time, mode,
  room, meeting_url, topic}` (sorted by day, then start).
- `POST /it/batches/{id}/timetable` → **201** slot. `PUT /it/batches/{id}/timetable/{slot_id}` (replaces) → **200** slot.
  `DELETE /it/batches/{id}/timetable/{slot_id}` → **204** (its cancellations go with it). Unknown batch, or a slot of another batch →
  **404**. A slot whose weekday and times intersect another slot of the same batch, or a slot of another batch with the same trainer
  whose dates intersect → **409** (the detail names the clash). Audited `batch.timetable.create|update|delete` (ids + changed field names).
- `POST /it/batches/{id}/timetable/{slot_id}/cancellations` `{occurs_on: date, reason (3–500)}` → **201** `{slot_id, occurs_on, reason}`.
  Not a class date of the slot within the batch dates, or before today (batch timezone) → **422**; already cancelled → **409**. Audited
  `batch.timetable.cancel`; each active student of the batch gets the itc-002 notice `it.batch.changed` (fixed text, link
  `/it/student/timetable`).
- `GET /it/timetable?from=&to=&batch_id=` → **200** `{from, to, can_edit, items}`: inclusive dates, default today…today+13 (IST); more than
  62 days or `to < from` → **422**. Items `{id, kind "class"|"live_session", slot_id, batch_id, batch, program, date, starts_at, ends_at,
  mode, room, meeting_url, topic, status "scheduled"|"cancelled", cancel_reason}`, sorted by `starts_at`; classes are computed from the
  slots inside the batch dates, and the batches' `LiveSession`s in the window are merged in. Scope: `it_student` → batches with an active
  enrolment, cancelled items omitted, `meeting_url` null while the nehn-018 `live_classes` feature is locked; `trainer` → own batches; IT
  `counselor` → batches of their itc-003 students' active enrolments, `meeting_url` always null; `it_admin`/`super_admin` → all. Any other
  role → **403**; a `batch_id` outside the caller's scope → **404**.
- `GET /portal/it/{student|trainer|counselor|admin}/timetable` → header-only payload "Timetable" (the page reads the routes above).

## 12IQ. IT student record (itc-012) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-345`. Migration `0229_it_student_profiles` (table `it_student_profiles`, PK `student_user_id`; nullable
`enrollments.start_date`, `end_date`, `delivery_mode`). Additive; downgrade refuses while profiles or overrides exist.

- `GET /it/me/profile`, `PATCH /it/me/profile` — the IT student only (403 otherwise).
- `GET|PATCH /counselor/it/students/{id}/profile` — the IT counselor, linked students only (itc-003); other roles 403; an unlinked or
  non-IT student 404.
- `GET|PATCH /admin/it/students/{id}/profile` — the IT Admin, any IT student; other roles 403; a non-IT student 404.
- `PATCH /counselor/it/students/{id}/enrollments/{enrollment_id}` and `PATCH /admin/it/students/{id}/enrollments/{enrollment_id}` — body
  `{start_date?, end_date?, delivery_mode?}` (`Online|Offline|Hybrid`; `null` clears the override). An enrolment of another student 404;
  an effective end before the effective start 422 (`loc: body.end_date`). Audit `it.enrollment.dates_update` with before/after.
- Profile PATCH body (all optional, `extra` forbidden, only sent fields change, blank text clears): `date_of_birth` (1900..today),
  `address` ≤300, `city` ≤80, `qualification` ≤120, `graduation_year` 1950..this year + 6, `emergency_contact_name` ≤120,
  `emergency_contact_relation` ≤60, `emergency_contact_phone` (7-20 digits/spaces/+-(), ≥7 digits). Audit `it.student_profile.update`
  with `{fields, role}` only (no values).
- Response (GET and PATCH): `{student: {id, full_name, student_code}, profile: {…fields, exists, updated_at, updated_by_name},
  enrollments: [{id, enrollment_code, program, batch, status, start_date, end_date, delivery_mode, start_date_override,
  end_date_override, delivery_mode_override, batch_start_date, batch_end_date, batch_mode}]}` — effective value = override, else batch.
- `GET /portal/it/counselor/students` and `GET /portal/it/admin/students` — each row adds `profile` (the page link), column type
  `student_profile`.

## 12IR. Per-enrolment fee account (itc-013) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-346`. Migration `0230_payment_mode`. Additive.

- `GET /it/enrollments/{id}/fee-account` → `{enrollment_id, enrollment_code, course, batch, currency, course_fee, discount_approved, tax,
  total_payable, paid_confirmed, credits, balance, state}` (numbers; `state` = `not_billed | enrolled_pending_payment | partially_paid |
  fully_paid`). The student (own), the IT counselor (linked student), a `payments.view` holder in IT; out of reach 404; no access 403.
- `GET /it/student/fee-accounts` → a list of the same, the IT student's own enrolments (newest first). Any other role 403.
- `POST /it/enrollments/{id}/payments` → 201 `{payment: {id, amount, status, payment_mode, provider_reference}, account}`. Body:
  `amount` (decimal > 0, 2 places), `payment_mode` (`cash | upi | card | bank_transfer | cheque`), `provider_reference` (≤ 160, trimmed).
  `payments.add` in IT (403 without the grant, 404 outside IT); nothing owed 409; more than the oldest owed bill 422. Not idempotent.
- `POST /admin/payments` accepts optional `payment_mode` (the five offline modes; others 422). `GET /admin/payments` and
  `GET /payments/mine` rows gain `payment_mode` and `provider_reference`.
- Receipt PDF: new lines "Payment mode", "Transaction reference" (after "Amount paid"); an enrolment fee's balance is the account's.
- Portal: `it/admin/enrollments` and `it/counselor/students` gain a `fee_balance` column (`INR 1,234.00` or `Not billed`).

## 12IS. Faculty profiles and student faculty view (itc-019) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-347`. Migration `0231_trainer_profiles`. RBAC §2.247.

- Profile body (PUT replaces all fields; unknown fields → **422**) `{designation? ≤120, expertise: string[] ≤12 × ≤60 (trimmed, blanks
  and case-insensitive repeats dropped), bio? ≤2000, office_hours? ≤160, contact_route "in_app"|"email"|"phone", show_email,
  show_phone, show_photo, show_bio, show_office_hours}`; blank strings are null. `contact_route` email/phone needs that `show_*` true and
  the value on the account → **422**.
- Editor view `{trainer_id, name, …all fields, account_email, account_phone, photo_url}` (defaults when no profile yet: email/phone
  hidden, the rest shown, route `in_app`). `photo_url` is a signed link (15 min) or null.
- `GET/PUT /it/trainer/profile` (trainer, own) → **200** editor view. `PUT /it/trainer/profile/photo` (multipart `file`) → **200**;
  `DELETE /it/trainer/profile/photo` → **200**. Photo: purpose `trainer_photo` — wrong type or bytes → **415**, over 2 MB → **413**,
  outside 120–4096 px or unreadable → **422**. The replaced file is deleted. Audited `trainer.profile.update` (changed field names) and
  `trainer.profile.photo` (`removed`, `replaced`).
- `GET/PUT /it/trainers/{trainer_id}/profile`, `PUT/DELETE …/photo` (IT Admin, Super Admin) → the same; not an IT trainer → **404**.
- `GET /it/student/faculty` (it_student) → **200** `{items: [{batch_id, batch, program, faculty | null}]}`, one per active enrolment's
  batch (`faculty` null when the batch has no trainer). `GET /it/faculty/{trainer_id}` (it_student) → **200** faculty, **404** unless the
  trainer teaches one of the student's active batches. Faculty `{trainer_id, name, designation, expertise, contact_route}` plus `bio`,
  `office_hours`, `photo_url`, `email`, `phone` **only when shown** (hidden keys are absent); a route whose channel is not in the response
  reads `in_app`.
- `GET /portal/it/trainer/profile` → header-only payload "Faculty Profile". `GET /portal/it/student/course` rows gain `trainer` (name, or
  "Not assigned yet").
## 12IT. Batch, course and timetable change notifications (itc-025) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-348`. No migration; no request or response change on existing routes (the notices are a side effect in the same transaction;
a rollback sends nothing).

- `PATCH /admin/batches/{id}` — a real change of `name`, `start_date`, `end_date`, `trainer_id`, `schedule`, `timezone` or `mode` sends
  `it.batch.changed` to each `active` student of the batch and `it.batch.student_changed` to each distinct linked IT counselor (itc-003).
  An identical value, or `capacity` / `enrollment_open` / `status` alone, sends nothing.
- `POST|PUT|DELETE /it/batches/{id}/timetable[/{slot_id}]` — same recipients (summary `timetable`); a PUT with no difference sends
  nothing. `POST …/{slot_id}/cancellations` — students as before, plus their counselors (summary `cancelled class`; never the reason).
- `PATCH /admin/enrollments/{id}` with a different `batch_id` — the moved student and their counselor only; the record is the new batch
  (summary `batch`). Same batch: nothing. A rejected enrolment: nothing.
- Notice body = the itc-002 fixed text + ` Changed: <labels>.` from the fixed list (batch name, start date, end date, trainer, timetable,
  time zone, mode, cancelled class, batch). `entity_type` `batch`, `entity_id` the batch id.
- `GET /portal/it/counselor/notifications` — new header-only section for the IT counselor (PortalPage mounts `GET /workflows/notifications`).

## 12IU. Attendance approved-leave policy, marker and session link (itc-021) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-349`. Migration `0232_attendance_leave_policy`. Additive; no route added.

- `GET /workflows/it/certificates/rules` → gains `leave_policy` (`attended | excluded | absent`). `PUT` accepts optional `leave_policy`
  (same values; anything else 422); omitted keeps the stored value. The response and the `certificate_rules.update` audit
  `before`/`after` carry it. Every embedded `rules` object (eligibility `criteria.rules`, batch progress `rules`, a certificate's
  `criteria_snapshot.rules`) gains the key.
- `POST /workflows/it/trainer/attendance`: body unchanged. Each written row records `marked_by_id` (the caller) and `session_id` (the
  date's single non-cancelled live session, else null). Status values unchanged; `excused` is displayed as "Approved leave".
- `PATCH /workflows/it/attendance-corrections/{id}` approved: also writes `attendance.update` (`changes.status = [old, new]`,
  `correction_id`) and sets the row's `marked_by_id` to the reviewer. Response unchanged.
- Every attendance % (eligibility, `/it/batches/{id}/progress`, training reports a/d/e/f, placement reports, HR student view, management
  training KPIs, `/portal/it/student/dashboard|attendance`) = attended / counted under the policy; "classes marked" stays the row count.
  `/portal/it/student/attendance` rows show `approved leave` for an excused row.

## 12IV. Student resume versions, staff upload and access log (itc-026) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-350`. Migration `0235_student_resumes`. RBAC §2.250.

- Version `{version, file_name, content_type, size_bytes, visibility "hr"|"private", uploaded_by {id, full_name} | null,
  uploaded_by_role, created_at}`. List `{items: Version[] newest first, max_bytes, can_upload}`.
- `GET/POST /it/me/resumes`, `GET /it/me/resumes/{version}/file` (it_student, own).
- `GET/POST /counselor/it/students/{id}/resumes`, `GET …/resumes/{version}/file` (IT counselor, itc-003 linked students; else 404).
- `GET/POST /admin/it/students/{id}/resumes`, `GET …/resumes/{version}/file` (IT Admin; not an IT student → 404).
- `GET /hr/students/{id}/resumes` (`student_documents.view`), `GET …/resumes/{version}/file` (`student_documents.download`) — HR
  (hr_team, super_admin) in the nehn-017 population; `hr` versions only (`can_upload: false`); a `private` version is 404.
- `POST` multipart `file` + form `visibility` (default `hr`) → **201** Version. Empty → **422**; over the limit → **413** "The resume must
  be at most N MB"; not PDF/DOC/DOCX by its bytes → **415**; bad visibility → **422**. Audited `it.resume.upload` (version, type, size,
  visibility, role).
- `…/file` → **200** the bytes, `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="resume-<student code>-v<n>.<ext>"`, `Cache-Control:
  private, no-store`, `nosniff`, sandbox CSP; **404** unknown version or missing file. The `it.resume.download` audit row (version,
  role) commits first.
- `GET /admin/it/resume-settings` → `{max_mb, max_bytes, min_mb: 1, max_mb_ceiling: 10}`; `PUT {max_mb: int 1..10}` (unknown fields
  **422**) → the same; IT Admin only, else **403**. Audited `it.resume.settings_update` (`changes.max_mb` [old, new]).
## 12IW. Internal vs student-visible IT student notes (itc-028) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-351`. Migration `0234_it_student_notes` (new table `it_student_notes`; nothing existing changes). Design:
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-02-itc-028-it-student-notes-design.md`.

- `GET /it/students/{student_id}/notes?limit=1..100 (20)&offset>=0` — staff in scope (RBAC §2.251) → `{student{id, full_name,
  student_code}, items[], total, limit, offset}`; item = `{id, body, visibility, author_name, author_role, created_at, shared_at,
  can_change_visibility}`, newest first, every author's notes (N3).
- `POST /it/students/{student_id}/notes` `{body}` (1–4000 chars after trim; `extra="forbid"`, so `visibility` is refused) → **201**, the
  note, always `internal`.
- `PUT /it/students/{student_id}/notes/{note_id}/visibility` `{visibility: "internal"|"student"}` → the note + `changed`. Author or IT
  Admin only (403). Another student's / unknown note → 404. A change writes audit `it.student_note.visibility` (entity
  `it_student_note`, `changes.visibility = [before, after]`, `student_id`; never the body); sharing sets `shared_at` and sends the student
  one `it.student.update_shared` notice (fixed text, `action_url=/it/student/updates`); same value → `changed: false`, nothing written.
- `GET /it/me/updates?limit&offset` — IT student only (403 otherwise) → page of the caller's own `student` notes, newest share first,
  items **allow-listed** to `{id, body, author_name, author_role, shared_at}`.
- `GET /portal/it/student/updates` — new header-only section; `GET /portal/it/counselor/students` and `/portal/it/admin/students` rows
  gain a `notes` link column (`student_notes`).
- Errors: an out-of-scope or missing student is the same 404 (no disclosure); a role with no notes scope 403; malformed UUID 422.
## 12IX. At-risk thresholds and flags (itc-023) — addendum, 2026-11-02

`DEC-SCOPE-352`. Migration `0236_it_at_risk` (tables `it_risk_thresholds`, `student_risk_flags`). Additive.

- `GET /admin/it/risk-thresholds` (IT Admin) → every programme, by title: `{program_id, program, active, min_attendance_percent,
  min_assessment_percent, max_consecutive_absences, min_sessions, monitored, updated_at}`; a programme without a row reads all-null,
  `min_sessions` 3, `monitored: false`.
- `PUT /admin/it/risk-thresholds/{program_id}` (IT Admin) body `{min_attendance_percent?: 0-100|null, min_assessment_percent?: 0-100|null,
  max_consecutive_absences?: 1-60|null, min_sessions?: 1-60 (default 3)}` (the whole set; extra keys 422) → the row as above. 404
  unknown programme; 403 any other role. Audited `it.risk_thresholds.update` with before/after (itc-001).
- `GET /it/at-risk?student_id=&history=false` → open flags (and cleared ones with `history=true`), open first then newest, at most 200:
  `{id, student{id, full_name, student_code}, program, batch{id, name}, reasons[attendance|assessment|absences], attendance_percent,
  assessment_percent, consecutive_absences, raised_at, cleared_at, clear_reason (recovered|stopped|null), profile_url}`. Scope: IT
  Admin all; IT counselor their itc-003 students; trainer their batches (`profile_url` null); anyone else 403.
- Portal: `/portal/it/admin/at-risk` and `/portal/it/trainer/notifications` (headers only).
- Notifications (itc-002 catalogue): `it.training.at_risk` (trainer, counselor), `it.training.check_in` (student), `it.training.back_on_track`
  (all three); entity = the enrolment; fixed text, no figures.

## 12IY. Student portal fee summary and course details (itc-018) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-353`. No migration. Additive except the receipt status code.

- The fee account (§12IR: `GET /it/student/fee-accounts`, `GET /it/enrollments/{id}/fee-account`, the record-payment response's
  `account`) gains `next_due: {amount, due_date (YYYY-MM-DD|null)} | null` — the oldest owed fee bill (pending/overdue, amount > 0) by
  due date (nulls last), then age; null when fully paid, unbilled or only zero-amount bills are owed.
- `GET /portal/it/student/course` rows gain `start_date`, `end_date` (YYYY-MM-DD), `mode` (each the enrolment's itc-012 override, else
  the batch's) and `room_or_link` (text: the batch's distinct offline timetable rooms, `Online (join link in your Timetable)` for an
  online slot, `Not scheduled yet` with no slots; never a URL). Columns `Starts`, `Ends`, `Mode`, `Room or link` are added; none removed.
- **Changed:** `GET /payments/{payment_id}/receipt` by a caller who neither owns the payment nor administers its division → `404
  "Payment not found"` (was `403 "Payment belongs to another user"`), the same answer as an unknown id.

## 12IZ. HR resume-review task, assignment rules and HR workspace (itc-027) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-354`. Migration `0237_hr_review_tasks`. New routes; §12IV upload and the rec-010 / placement-profile writes gain side effects
only (no shape change).

- Stage: `resume_received | under_review | changes_requested | screening | interview_ready | completed | withdrawn` (the last two closed).
- Summary `{id, student: {id, full_name, student_code}, owner: {id, full_name, active} | null, stage, stage_label, resume_version,
  screening_date (YYYY-MM-DD|null), assigned_at, closed_at, updated_at}`. Task = Summary + `{role_preferences, skill_gap_notes,
  checklist: ("contact"|"education"|"skills"|"projects"|"formatting")[], history: [{from_stage, to_stage, reason
  ("upload"|"manual"|"withdrawn"), actor (name|null), created_at}] (newest first, ≤50)}`.
- `GET /hr/review-tasks?stage=&limit=1..100 (25)&offset=` (`hr_team`, IT) → `{items: Summary[], total, limit, offset, counts: {stage:
  int}}` — the caller's own tasks; open stages unless `stage` is given; newest activity first.
- `GET /hr/students/{student_id}/review` → Task; `PATCH` same path `{stage?, screening_date?, role_preferences? (≤300),
  skill_gap_notes? (≤4000), checklist?}` (extra keys refused; `stage` and `checklist` not null; `withdrawn` not settable) → Task. 404 when
  the caller does not own the student's task; 409 on a closed task; 422 invalid input. A stage change writes history and notifies.
- `GET /admin/it/hr-review-tasks?stage=&unassigned=bool&limit=1..200&offset=` (`it_admin`, IT) → `{items: Summary[], total, limit,
  offset}`; `unassigned=true` = no owner or a deactivated owner. `PUT /admin/it/hr-review-tasks/{task_id}/owner {hr_user_id}` → Summary;
  404 unknown task, 422 not an active IT `hr_team` user.
- `GET /admin/it/hr-assignment-rules` → `{programs: [{program_id, program, active, hr_user: {id, full_name, active} | null}], hr_users:
  [{id, full_name}]}`; `PUT /admin/it/hr-assignment-rules/{program_id} {hr_user_id: uuid | null}` (null removes) → the programme row; 404
  unknown programme, 422 not an active HR user.
- `GET /counselor/it/students/{student_id}/hr-review` (linked IT counselor) and `GET /admin/it/students/{student_id}/hr-review`
  (`it_admin`) → `{task: {stage, stage_label, owner: {full_name} | null, next_action, screening_date, resume_version, closed, updated_at}
  | null}` (never notes, preferences or checklist); 404 outside scope, 403 other roles.
- `GET /it/me/hr-review` (`it_student`) → `{status: {label (student-safe), action (text|null), updated_at} | null}`; 403 other roles.
- Side effects: a §12IV upload with `visibility=hr` opens/updates/re-opens the task (H2) and sends `it.resume.uploaded` to the owner and
  the linked counselor; rec-010 opt-out and `PUT /workflows/it/placement/profiles/{id}` with `withdrawn: true` close it. New notification
  events `it.hr_review.updated` (counselor) and `it.hr_review.assigned` (new owner); `it.placement.updated` goes to the student on a move.

## 12JA. Trainer dashboard widgets and batch filter (itc-024) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-355`. No migration. Additive.

- `GET /portal/it/trainer/dashboard?batch=<uuid>` — `batch` optional. `metrics` keeps its first four tiles (Assigned batches,
  Assignments, Upcoming sessions, Pending reviews) and adds `Today's classes` (int), `Attendance completion` (`"50% (1/2)"` or `"—"`),
  `Assessment completion` (same format) and `At-risk students` (int). `panels` gains `{title: "Today's classes", items: [string],
  empty: "No classes today"}` (each item `HH:MM–HH:MM · batch · topic · Attendance marked|Attendance not marked|Not started`). New key
  `batch_filter: {value: uuid|null, options: [{value, label}]}` (the trainer's own batches). With `batch`, every figure, the panel and
  `rows` are that batch's only.
- 422 `"Choose one of your batches"` — `batch` not a UUID, or not a batch the trainer teaches (another trainer's and a non-existent id
  read the same). 422 `"The batch filter applies to the trainer dashboard only"` — `batch` on any other role or section.
- Panels (every portal payload): optional `empty` string, shown when `items` is empty.

## 12JC. Call preference and resume malware scan (itc-033) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-357`. Migration `0239_call_preference`. Additive.

- `GET /account/notification-preferences` — response gains `calls: bool` (true = phone calls allowed; default true).
- `PUT /account/notification-preferences` — body `{whatsapp, sms, calls?}`; `calls` is an optional strict boolean (omitted = unchanged).
  Other extra fields stay 422. Changing `calls` never needs a valid phone. Audit `notification_preference.update` before/after include
  `calls`. The data-request export's `notification_preferences` gains `calls` and `calls_opted_out_at`.
- `GET /counselor/it/students/{id}/profile`, `GET /admin/it/students/{id}/profile`, `GET /it/me/profile` — `student` gains
  `calls_allowed: bool`.
- `POST …/resumes` (itc-026, all portals) — with `CLAMAV_HOST` set: 422 `"The file failed a virus scan and was not uploaded"` (audit
  `it.resume.scan_rejected`); 503 `"Virus scanning is unavailable; please try again later"` when the scanner gives no verdict. Nothing
  is stored in either case. Unset: unchanged.
## 12JD. Overdue marking and fee reminders (itc-016) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-358`. Migration `0240_fee_reminders`. Additive.

- `GET /it/enrollments/{id}/fee-account` and `GET /it/student/fee-accounts` (§12IR) — each account gains `installments: [{id,
  installment_no|null, amount, due_date|null, status: "pending"|"paid", overdue: bool, reminders: [{kind: "due_soon"|"overdue",
  offset_days, sent_on}]}]`, the fee rows in `record_payment`'s order. `overdue` = pending and `due_date` before today's IST date.
  `next_due` (§12IY) is unchanged (the first pending installment). Same readers and errors as §12IR.
- `GET /admin/it/fee-reminder-settings` → `{days_before, overdue_days}` (no row = `{3, [1, 7, 14]}`). `PUT` with the same body
  (both required): `days_before` 0–30 (0 = off), `overdue_days` ≤ 6 integers 1–90 (empty = off), de-duplicated and sorted in the
  response; 422 otherwise. IT Admin (IT) only: 403 for every other role, 401 signed out. Audited `it.fee_reminders.settings_update`.
- Beat `itc016-fee-reminders` (03:00 UTC = 08:30 IST) → notifications `it.fee.payment_due` / `it.fee.payment_overdue` (student,
  `action_url` `/it/student/fees`) and `it.fee.student_overdue` (linked IT counselor, `action_url` the student record); entity
  `payment`. No amount in any notice.

## 12JE. Unified IT student lifecycle (itc-031) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-359`. Migration `0241_enrollment_lifecycle`. Additive except the admin enrolment PATCH status check.

- `GET /counselor/it/students/{id}/lifecycle` (linked counselor), `GET /admin/it/students/{id}/lifecycle` (IT Admin) — `{enrollments:
  [{enrollment_id, enrollment_code, program, batch, stage, stage_label, components: {fee, training, placement} (each `{stage,
  stage_label}` or null), override: {stage, stage_label, reason} | null, history: [{from_stage, from_label, to_stage, to_label, cause,
  actor, created_at}] (newest first, 50)}]}`. Stages: `enrolled`, `fee_pending`, `partially_paid`, `paid`, `training_active`, `at_risk`,
  `completed`, `resume_submitted`, `hr_screening`, `interview`, `placed`, `not_placed`, `closed`. Causes: `enrolment`, `fee`, `certificate`,
  `hr_review`, `placement`, `override`, `daily_check`; `actor` null = the system. Out of scope 404; another role 403.
- `PUT /admin/it/students/{id}/enrollments/{enrollment_id}/lifecycle-override` (IT Admin) — body `{stage, reason?}`; `stage` is a stage
  name or null (clears); `reason` 3–500 characters, required when setting. Only `not_placed` / `closed` are stored: `placed` → 409
  `"Placed is set by a joining record, never by hand"`; any other stage → 409; `not_placed` on a placed student → 409. Returns the
  lifecycle body. Audit `it.enrollment.lifecycle_override` (before/after).
- `GET …/it/students/{id}/profile` (staff) and `GET /it/me/profile` — each enrolment gains `lifecycle: {stage, stage_label}`; the
  student's own reads the safe subset (`at_risk` → `training_active`).
- `GET /admin/enrollments` — each row gains `lifecycle: {stage, stage_label}`.
- `PATCH /admin/enrollments/{id}` — `status` must be one of `pending_consent`, `active`, `rejected`, `withdrawn`, `dropped`, `completed`
  (422 otherwise; it was free text).
## 12JF. IT student 360 (itc-029) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-360`. No migration. Additive (two new read routes; two portal tables gain a column).

- `GET /it/students/{id}/360` — `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. The IT `counselor` (itc-003 linked students), `hr_team` with
  `student_progress.view` (the nehn-017 pool) and the `it_admin` (any IT student). Response `{student: {id, full_name, student_code,
  active}, viewer: "counselor"|"admin"|"hr", sections: [...], ...one key per section in sections}`. Sections: `contact {email, phone,
  counselor}`, `profile {city, qualification, emergency_contact_name, emergency_contact_relation, emergency_contact_phone, calls_allowed}`,
  `lead {id, lead_code, stage, stage_label, owner, created_at, converted_at, href} | null`, `enrollments [{id, enrollment_code, program,
  batch, trainer, status, start_date, end_date, delivery_mode, progress_percent, attendance_percent?, attendance_marked?}]`, `fee {accounts:
  [itc-013 account], balance: number|null}`, `risk [{enrollment_id, batch, reasons, attendance_percent, assessment_percent,
  consecutive_absences, raised_at}]`, `feedback [{kind, title, score, max_score, feedback (≤300 chars), by, at}]` (5 newest), `resumes
  {items: [itc-026 version]}`, `hr_review {task}` (itc-027 staff view), `notes {items, total}` (5 newest, itc-028), `notifications [{id,
  title, event_type, sent_at, read}]` (10 newest, never the body). HR's `sections` omit `profile`, `lead`, `fee`, `risk`, `notifications`
  (the keys are absent); HR's `enrollments` carry the attendance keys only with `attendance.view`, and HR's `resumes` only `hr` versions.
  Errors: 401 no session; 403 any other role (trainer included) or HR without `student_progress.view`; 404 a student out of scope, not an
  IT student, or missing; 422 a malformed id. A fixed number of queries (≤ 22) whatever the number of enrolments.
- `GET /it/students/{id}/360/lead-timeline?limit=20&offset=0` — the tel-015 timeline page of the IT lead the student was converted from
  (`{items, total, limit, offset}`; empty when there is none). The counselor and the IT Admin (scope as above); HR 403.
- `GET /portal/it/counselor/students` and `GET /portal/it/admin/students` — rows gain `open` (the 360 page path), column
  `{key: "open", label: "", type: "student_360"}`.

## 12JG. One-step IT conversion and the onboarding checklist (itc-011) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-361`. Migration `0243_it_conversion`. Additive.

- `GET /counselor/leads/{id}/convert-it` (owner IT counselor), `GET /admin/leads/{id}/convert-it` (IT Admin) — `{prefill: {full_name,
  email, phone, fee (latest counseling fee_quoted or null), recommended_program_id, recommended_course}, batches: [{id, name, program_id,
  program, fee, start_date, end_date, schedule, mode, available, recommended}] (open IT batches with a seat, recommended course first),
  checklist: [label]}`.
- `POST /counselor/leads/{id}/convert-it`, `POST /admin/leads/{id}/convert-it` — header `Idempotency-Key` (required, 1–120 of
  `[A-Za-z0-9._:-]`, else 422). Body `{student: {full_name, email, phone?} | student_id, batch_id, fee (>= 0), installments?: [{amount >
  0, due_date}] (max 12)}`; exactly one of `student` / `student_id` (422). 201 `{student, enrollment: {id, enrollment_code, status, course,
  batch}, fee_account (§12IR shape), checklist: [{id, label, position, done, done_at}], lead (the counselor detail, or the admin row),
  replayed: false, email_status?, expires_at?}` (`email_status` only for a new account). The same key again on that lead → 200, same
  shape, `replayed: true`, nothing created. Refusals (nothing written): lead closed / linked / outside Qualified…Counselling Completed /
  not IT → 409; batch missing 404, closed or full 409; installments not summing to the fee, given for a fee of 0, or due before today
  (IST) → 422 on `installments`; a new account matching an active IT student by email or mobile → 409 `{code: "student_exists", message}`;
  an email of any other account → 409 `"This email belongs to another account. Use a different email."`; mail budget 429 (csc-002).
  The enrolment starts `pending_consent`; the lead moves to Application/Enrollment. Audit `lead.convert_it` (ids, counts).
- `GET /counselor/leads/{id}` — gains `permissions.convert_it` and `it_conversion: {enrollment_id} | null`.
- `GET /it/enrollments/{id}/onboarding` — `{enrollment_id, items: [{id, label, position, done, done_at}]}`. `PATCH
  /it/enrollments/{id}/onboarding/{item_id}` — body `{done}`; returns the same body; unknown item 404; audit `it.onboarding.item_update`.

## 12JH. IT status labels (itc-005) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-362`. `Entry = {code, label, default_label, sort_order, enabled, can_disable}`; each list is ordered by `sort_order`, then the
catalogue position (default order = (position + 1) × 10).
- `GET /it/status-labels` — IT staff (division `it`, not a student) and super_admin; others 403, anonymous 401. Returns
  `{lead: [Entry], lifecycle: [Entry]}`.
- `GET /admin/it/status-labels` — the IT Admin only (403 otherwise). The same with `in_use` (IT leads in the stage / enrolments carrying
  the override), `updated_at` and `updated_by {id, full_name} | null` per entry.
- `PUT /admin/it/status-labels/{kind}/{code}` — the IT Admin only. `kind` ∈ `lead`, `lifecycle`. Body `{label (trimmed, 1–60),
  sort_order (0–9999), enabled}`, all required, extra fields 422. Returns the admin Entry, 200 (idempotent; an unchanged save writes no
  audit row). Unknown kind/code 404; a label another code of the kind uses (case-insensitive) 422 at `label`; turning off a state that
  cannot be turned off 422 at `enabled`. Audit `it.status_label.update` (entity `it_status_label`, id `kind:code`, before/after).
- Changed behaviour: `POST /telecaller/leads/{id}/stage`, `POST /counselor/leads/{id}/stage` and `PATCH /admin/leads/{id}` on an IT lead to a turned-off stage → 422 at
  `to_stage` ("This status is turned off by the IT Admin"); `PUT /admin/it/students/{id}/enrollments/{eid}/lifecycle-override` to a
  turned-off stage → 409 (clearing with `null` always allowed). Lifecycle `stage_label` / history labels and the IT 360 `lead.stage_label`
  carry the configured words. Follow-up rows' `lead` gains `division` (additive).

## 12JI. Discount and waiver approval (itc-014) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-363`. Migration `0245_fee_discount_requests`. Additive. A request item is `{id, enrollment_id, enrollment_code, student: {id,
full_name}, course, amount, reason, status: pending|approved|rejected, requested_by: {id, full_name}, created_at, decided_by: {id,
full_name} | null, decided_at, decision_note, own}` (`own`: the caller made it).

- `GET /it/enrollments/{id}/discount-requests` — the enrolment's requests, newest first.
- `POST /it/enrollments/{id}/discount-requests` — body `{amount (> 0, 2 dp), reason (1–500)}` → 201 the request. Nothing owed → 409;
  amount above the balance still owed → 422 naming it; a request already pending → 409. Audit `it.fee.discount_requested`.
- `GET /it/discount-requests?status=pending|approved|rejected` (default `pending`, oldest first; decided newest first; at most 200) —
  the approval queue.
- `POST /it/discount-requests/{id}/approve` — body `{note? (≤ 500)}` → `{request, account}` (§12IR shape: `discount_approved`,
  `total_payable` and `balance` lowered). The amount comes off the owed bills latest due first; a bill brought to 0 gets status `waived`
  (a new `Payment.status` value, seen in `/payments/mine` and `/admin/payments`; never payable). Already decided → 409; more than is
  now owed → 409 (the request stays pending). Audit `it.fee.discount_approved` with old/new figures.
- `POST /it/discount-requests/{id}/reject` — body `{note (1–500)}` → `{request}`. Already decided → 409. Audit `it.fee.discount_rejected`.

## 12JJ. Refunds, adjustments and payment correction (itc-015) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-364`. Migration `0246_fee_adjustments`. Additive.

- `POST /it/enrollments/{id}/adjustments` — `payments.refund`, IT division (or every division). Body `{kind: credit_note | write_off |
  refund, amount (> 0, 2 dp), reason (1–500), payment_mode? (cash, upi, card, bank_transfer, cheque; required for a refund, else 422
  "Choose how the refund was paid (payment mode)"), reference? (≤ 160)}`. 201 `{adjustment, account}` (`account` = §12IR shape).
  Refusals, nothing written: nothing billed 409; a credit note over what is still payable, a write-off over the balance owed, a refund
  over the credit (or with no credit) 422 naming the maximum; an unknown enrolment 404, a typed non-UUID 422.
- `POST /it/enrollments/{id}/payments/{payment_id}/correction` — same gate. Body `{amount, payment_mode, provider_reference?, reason}`.
  201 `{payment (the replacement), account}`. Another enrolment's payment 404; already corrected, unpaid, or paid through the gateway
  409; nothing changed, or an amount above the reopened bill 422 (rolled back whole).
- `GET /it/fee-adjustments/{id}/document` — `{url, expires_in}` (signed PDF); the itc-013 read gate (student own, linked IT counselor,
  `payments.view` in IT), otherwise 404 / 403; a reversal has no document (404).
- `GET /it/enrollments/{id}/fee-account`, `GET /it/student/fee-accounts` — each account gains `refunded` and `adjustments: [{id, kind,
  amount, reason, payment_mode, reference, document_no, created_at}]`; `credits` now counts credit notes and write-offs. A corrected
  payment reads `status: "reversed"` in `/payments/mine`; a bill a credit covered whole reads `waived`.
- `GET /management/ledger` (`include=auto`) — a new automatic `source: "fee_refund"` line (IT income, negative amount).

## 12JK. Modules, labs, computed progress, remediation (itc-022) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-365`. Migration `0249_it_training_progress`. Additive: existing payloads gain fields only.

- `GET /admin/it/progress-plans` — IT Admin. 200 `[{program_id, program, active, modules, topics, weights|null, computed}]`.
- `GET /admin/it/progress-plans/{program_id}` — IT Admin. 200 `{program_id, program, modules:[{id, title, topics:[{id, title}]}],
  weights: {topics, assignments, assessments}|null, computed, copy_source:[{title, topics[]}]}`; 404 unknown programme.
- `PUT /admin/it/progress-plans/{program_id}` `{modules:[{id?, title(1-200), topics:[{id?, title}] ≤50}] ≤30, weights|null}` — replaces
  the plan (ids kept, missing ones deleted with their completions); refreshes the programme's batches. 200 = the GET body. 422 weights
  not summing to 100 ("The weights must add up to 100"), an id from another programme, a blank title. Audited (`it.progress_plan.update`).
- `GET /it/batches/{batch_id}/topics` — the batch trainer or IT Admin. 200 `{batch_id, computed, modules:[{id, title, topics:[{id, title,
  completed_enrollment_ids[]}]}]}`; 403 another trainer / other roles; 404 unknown batch.
- `PUT /it/batches/{batch_id}/topics/{topic_id}/completions` `{enrollment_ids[1..500], completed}` — idempotent bulk mark/unmark.
  200 `{topic_id, completed_enrollment_ids}`; 404 topic not in the batch's programme; 422 "N of the selected students is/are not an
  active student of this batch" (nothing written). Audited (`it.topic_completion.update`, counts only).
- `PATCH /workflows/it/trainer/enrollments/{id}/progress` — body gains optional `reason` (≤500); response gains `override_reason`. On a
  computed programme a reason is required (422 "Give a reason for overriding the computed progress") and the value becomes an override
  (audited `enrollment.progress_override`). A manual programme is unchanged.
- `DELETE /workflows/it/trainer/enrollments/{id}/progress/override` — the batch trainer or IT Admin; recomputes. 200 `{id,
  progress_percent, override_reason: null}`.
- `GET /workflows/it/batches/{id}/progress` — gains `progress_mode` (`computed|manual`) and per student `override_reason`,
  `computed_percent`, `topics_completed`, `topics_total`, `labs_graded`, `labs_total`.
- `POST/PATCH /workflows/it/trainer/assignments` — `assignment_type` accepts `lab`.
- `GET /it/students/{student_id}/remediation-plans` — batch trainer (seated), linked IT counselor, IT Admin. 200 `{student, items:[{id,
  actions, due_date, status, owner_name, batch, created_at, updated_at, enrollment_id, owner_user_id, risk_flag_id}], enrollments (where
  the caller may add), owner_options, viewer_id}`; HR/others 403; out of scope 404.
- `POST /it/enrollments/{enrollment_id}/remediation-plans` `{actions(1-2000), due_date, owner_user_id?}` — batch trainer or IT Admin.
  201 the plan (linked to the open itc-023 flag). 422 not an active enrolment ("…only be created for an actively enrolled student"), a
  past due date (IST), an owner who is not the batch trainer / the student's IT counselor / an IT Admin; 404 out of scope.
- `PATCH /it/remediation-plans/{id}` `{actions?, due_date?, owner_user_id?, status?}` — batch trainer / IT Admin; the owner status only
  (403 otherwise). 409 "This plan is closed" (completed/cancelled); 404 out of scope. Audited before/after.
- `GET /it/me/progress` — IT student. 200 `{courses:[{enrollment_id, course, batch, status, progress_percent, mode
  (computed|override|manual), weights, topics_completed, topics_total, labs_graded, labs_total, assignments_graded, assignments_total,
  assessments_passed, assessments_total, modules:[{title, topics:[{title, completed}]}]}], remediation_plans:[{id, actions, due_date,
  status, owner_name, batch, created_at, updated_at}]}`; other roles 403.
- `GET /portal/it/admin/progress-plans` — IT Admin header-only section.

## 12JM. IT counselor dashboard (itc-030) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-367`. No migration. Additive (no existing contract changes; the portal payload of `/portal/it/counselor/dashboard` is
unchanged).

- `GET /counselor/it/dashboard?program=&batch=&from=&to=&stage=&owner=` — an IT counselor (their own book) or the IT Admin (every IT
  counselor's; `owner` narrows to one). `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. 200 `{filters, options: {programs, batches (with program),
  stages, owners (IT Admin only, else [])}, today, widgets: {assigned_leads, follow_ups (+overdue), pipeline {count, stages:[{stage,
  label, count}]}, conversions, enrolled_students, outstanding_fees (+total), at_risk, pending_hr, ready_to_convert}}`; a list widget is
  `{count, more, items: [{id, label, detail, date, overdue, href}]}` with at most 10 items and `count` the full count.
  Refusals: anyone else 403 "Only an IT counselor or the IT Admin has this dashboard"; anonymous 401; 422 "Unknown filter value" (a
  non-UUID or unknown course/batch, an unknown stage, a malformed date), "The start date must be on or before the end date", "The batch
  is not in that course", "The owner filter is for the IT Admin" (a counselor sending `owner`), "Choose an IT counselor" (an owner
  that is not an IT counselor).
- `GET /portal/it/admin/counselor-dashboard` — IT Admin header-only section (the page's board reads the endpoint above).
## 12JN. IT accounts workspace reports (itc-017) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-368`. Migration `0248_accounts_grants`. Additive; read-only.

- `GET /it/accounts/reports/{report}` — `report` ∈ `collections | outstanding | overdue | receipts | adjustments` (else 404). Gate:
  `payments.view` in the IT division or every division (it_admin, accountant, super_admin); anyone else 403 "You do not have access to
  IT accounts". Query: `program_id?`, `batch_id?` (unknown 422 "Choose a course/batch from the list"; a batch outside the course 422),
  `date_from?`/`date_to?` (IST days; default this month to today; from after to 422; over 366 days 422; used by collections, receipts,
  adjustments), `group` (`day | month | course`, collections), `mode` (cash, upi, card, bank_transfer, cheque, razorpay; collections,
  receipts), `kind` (credit_note, write_off, refund, reversal; adjustments), `state` (`enrolled_pending_payment | partially_paid |
  overdue`; outstanding), `limit` 1–200 (default 50), `offset` ≥ 0. 200 `{report, items, total, limit, offset, totals, filters,
  courses: [{id, title}], batches: [{id, name, program_id}]}` (+ `by_course` on outstanding). `totals` cover the whole filtered list:
  collections `{payments, amount}`; outstanding `{enrolments, balance, overdue_amount}`; overdue `{installments, amount}`; receipts
  `{receipts, amount}` (a reversed payment's receipt is listed, not summed); adjustments `{count, credit_note, write_off, refund, reversal}`.
- `GET /it/accounts/reports/{report}/export` — same gate and filters; `text/csv` attachment `it-<report>-<IST date>.csv` with every
  matching row (over 10,000 → 422). Text cells neutralise formula prefixes. Audited `it.accounts.export` `{report, filters, rows}`.

## 12JO. Management IT reports (itc-032) — addendum, 2026-11-03

`DEC-SCOPE-369`. No migration. Additive; read-only.

- `GET /management/it/reports` — 200 `{reports: [{key, label}]}`: the reports the caller may open, in tab order; none → 403.
- `GET /management/it/reports/{report}` — `report` ∈ `funnel | conversions | revenue | training | placement | activity` (else 404).
  Gate: operational reports — partner, super_admin, it_admin (IT); revenue — partner, or `payments.view` in IT / every division
  (it_admin, accountant, super_admin); anyone else 403 "You do not have access to this IT report"; anonymous 401. Query: `program_id?`,
  `batch_id?` (unknown 422 "Choose a course/batch from the list"; a batch outside the course 422), `owner_id?` (not a lead owner, caller,
  converter, follow-up completer or IT counselor → 422 "Choose an owner from the list"), `date_from?`/`date_to?` (IST days; default this
  month to today; from after to 422; over 366 days 422), `status?` (a lead stage on funnel/conversions/activity, an IT lifecycle stage on
  revenue/training/placement; else 422 "Choose a status from the list"), `limit` 1–200 (50), `offset` ≥ 0. 200 `{report, totals, groups,
  items, total, limit, offset, filters, definitions: [{key, label, definition}], options: {courses, batches, owners: [{id, name}],
  statuses: [{value, label}]}}`. `totals` cover the whole filtered report and equal Σ `groups`; `total` is the drill-down row count.
- `GET /management/it/reports/{report}/export` — same gate and filters; `text/csv` attachment `it-<report>-<IST date>.csv` with every
  row (over 10,000 → 422). Text cells neutralise formula prefixes. Audited `management.it_reports.export` `{report, filters, rows}`.

## 12JP. Concurrent-edit protection (hrc-003) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-370`. No migration. Additive: a new optional body field on four writes; a request without it behaves as before.

- `PATCH /recruiter/requirements/{id}`, `PATCH /recruiter/candidates/{id}`, `PATCH /recruiter/interviews/{id}` — optional
  `expected_updated_at` (ISO timestamp; the `updated_at` the form was opened with, sent back unchanged; not a timestamp → 422).
  `POST /recruiter/requirements/{id}/jd` — optional `expected_version` (int ≥ 0; the current JD version the form showed, 0 = none).
- Order: scope 404 → role 403 → token → the existing rules. A different stored token → **409**
  `detail = {code: "record_changed", message: "This record changed since you opened it", changed_by: {id, full_name} | null,
  changed_at, changed_fields: [..], current}`. `current` is the record as that route's GET returns it to the caller (requirement detail,
  candidate detail, interview, or the JD with its versions). `changed_fields` are field names from the record's audit rows after the
  token (a status move reads `status`, an archive `archive`; downloads are not changes); for the JD, the exact difference between the
  two versions (`file` for a new file). Nothing is written on a 409.
- The interview shape gains `updated_at`. A requirement change that only touches its skills now moves the requirement's `updated_at`.

## 12JQ. Recruitment access-control hardening (hrc-001) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-371`. Migration `0250_compensation_grant` (seed only). **Breaking for out-of-scope callers** (behaviour narrowing; in-scope
shapes unchanged except where noted).

- `POST /workflows/it/jobs`, `PATCH /workflows/it/jobs/{id}`, `PATCH /workflows/it/job-applications/{id}`, `POST|PATCH
  /workflows/it/interviews[/{id}]`, `POST|PATCH /workflows/it/offers[/{id}]`: 404 when the record (or the named company) is outside the
  caller's `/recruiter/*` scope; 403 for `hr_team` and for a `placement_team` user without a recruiter profile. A `placement_team`
  caller's new requirement (and a company it creates here) gets `assigned_recruiter_user_id` = the caller.
- `GET|PATCH|POST /recruiter/candidates[/{id}][/archive|/restore]`: `current_salary` and `expected_salary` are omitted (not null) without
  `candidates.compensation.view`; a create/update body carrying either key → 403 `Your role cannot view or change candidate salary`.
- `POST /recruiter/candidates/search` and `GET /recruiter/pools/{id}/candidates`: cards omit `expected_salary` without the grant; a
  search with `salary_min` or `salary_max` → 403.
- `GET|PUT /recruiter/applications/{id}/screening`: `screening.expected_salary` omitted without the grant; a PUT that sends it → 403; one
  that omits it keeps the stored value.
- `GET /employer/interviews`: no `result`; `status` reads `completed` for `selected|rejected|on_hold`; new `status_label`.
- `GET /portal/it/student/interview-schedule`: the `result` column is removed.
- `GET /files/download?key=` and `GET /local-files/{key}`: 404 for `candidate-resumes/`, `career-resumes/`, `student-resumes/`,
  `job-offers/`, `job-joinings/`, `recruiter-contracts/` (served by their own routes, unchanged).

## 12JT. Candidate compensation (hrc-011) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-375`. **Candidate create / PATCH / detail** (`/api/v1/recruiter/candidates`) gain `current_salary_currency`,
`current_salary_period`, `expected_salary_currency`, `expected_salary_period`, `minimum_salary`, `minimum_salary_currency`,
`minimum_salary_period`, `salary_negotiable` (bool | null) and the read-only `current_annual_inr`, `expected_annual_inr`,
`minimum_annual_inr`. Currency: three letters, upper-cased; not INR and no rate → 422 naming the currency. Period: `year` | `month`
(else 422). An amount without currency/period keeps the stored one, else INR / year; a null amount clears its currency, period and annual
figure. Sending an `*_annual_inr` key is 422 (unknown field). Without `candidates.compensation.view` every one of these keys is absent and
sending one is 403 (§12JQ). **Search / pool cards** add `expected_salary_currency`, `expected_salary_period`, `expected_annual_inr` (same
grant); `salary_min` / `salary_max` now compare `expected_annual_inr`.
**Exchange rates:** `GET /api/v1/recruiter/catalogue/exchange-rates` → `{items: [{currency, inr_per_unit, updated_at, updated_by}]}` by
currency (candidate readers). `PUT /api/v1/recruiter/catalogue/exchange-rates/{currency}` `{inr_per_unit}` (placement_manager,
super_admin): idempotent upsert; 422 for INR, a code that is not three letters, or a rate outside (0, 10,000] / more than 4 decimals;
recomputes the annual figure of every candidate amount in that currency; audit `recruiter.fx_rate_set` {old, new, recomputed}.

## 12JU. Recruitment record history (hrc-004) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-376`. **New:** `GET /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/history` and `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/{id}/history`
(`?limit=1..100` default 20, `&offset`) → `{items, total, limit, offset}`, newest first; each item `{id, action, created_at,
actor: {id, full_name} | null, changes: {field: [old, new] | "changed"} | null, fields: [name] | null}`. A requirement's rows are its
`recruiter_requirement.*` and `job_description.*` audits; a candidate's its `candidate.*` audits; `*download` rows are excluded. Access =
the record's own GET (requirement: out of scope 404, other roles 403; candidate: non-reader 403, unknown 404); anonymous 401;
`Cache-Control: private, no-store`. **Changed audit rows** (shape additive; no field removed): `recruiter_requirement.update|status|assign`
(incl. rec-037 bulk reassignment, one row per job), `job_description.create|upload`, `candidate.update`, `recruiter_application.status`,
`recruiter_interview.update|reschedule|status|feedback` add `metadata.changes`. Compensation, contact and feedback text are `"changed"`.
## 12JV. Recruitment roles and grant keys (hrc-002) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-374`. No migration. Additive.

- `POST /admin/users`: accepts `role` `hr_manager`, `placement_officer`, `interviewer` with `division` `it` (another division → 422).
  `hr_manager` by a super_admin only — anyone else → 403 `Only a Super Admin can create HR managers`.
- `GET /portal/it/hr-manager/dashboard`, `/portal/it/placement-officer/dashboard`, `/portal/it/interviewer/dashboard`: the role's own
  portal payload (403 for any other role, 404 for any other section). Rows `{job_id, role, company, location, status}` (open requirements;
  HR Manager and Placement Officer); metrics `Open requirements` (+ `Applications`, `Interviews`, `Offers` for the HR Manager); the
  Interviewer's payload has no rows or metrics.
- `GET /admin/permissions/catalogue`: `roles` gains the three roles; `actions` gains `publish`, `export`, `import`, `merge`, `release`;
  `resources` gains `jobs` (approve, publish), `candidates` (export, import, merge), `results` (release). `PUT
  /admin/roles/{role}/permissions` accepts the new keys and roles.
- `GET /auth/me/permissions`: `[]` for the new roles by default.


## 12JW. Candidate duplicate review and merge (hrc-014) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-377`. **New:** `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-duplicates?status=open|dismissed|merged&limit&offset` → `{items, total, limit,
offset}`; item `{id, signals: ["name_location_college"|"phone_variant"], status, created_at, decided_at, decided_by, candidate, other}`
(sides `{id, candidate_code, name, location, college, status, archived}`, older first; no contact data). `POST .../scan` → `{created}`.
`POST .../{id}/dismiss` `{note?: ≤500}` → the pair (409 when not open, 404 unknown). `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/{id}/merge?victim=CAN-…`
→ `{survivor, victim, fields, conflicts: [{application_id, job_id, job_title, status, blocking}], moved}`; `POST .../merge`
`{victim_code, use_victim: [field], reason?: ≤500}` → `{id, victim_id, victim_code, moved: {applications, share_items, calls, messages,
consents, documents, document_requests, skills, resumes}, kept_applications}` (hrc-015's documents and requests move too; a victim's
pending request of a type the survivor is also waiting for is cancelled). Errors: 401; 403 (not a candidate writer or no `candidates.merge`); 404 (either side
unknown or outside the pool); 422 (self, unknown field, bad body); 409 (archived/merged side, student login, open application on the same
requirement, lost race). **Changed:** `CandidateDetail.merged_into` (`{id, candidate_code}` | null); `POST /candidates/{id}/restore` of a
merged candidate is 409; create/edit queue probable duplicates (response unchanged). Reads are `Cache-Control: private, no-store`.

## 12JX. Candidate documents, document requests and signed links (hrc-015) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-379`. **Resume upload** (`PUT /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/{id}/resume`) also accepts DOC (`application/msword`, judged by the
bytes; else 415 "Upload a PDF, DOC or DOCX file"). Resume entries in the candidate detail gain `preview_status` (`pending` | `ready` |
`failed` | null; null for a PDF). **Links** (`Cache-Control: private, no-store`, audited, `{url, expires_in: 300}`):
`GET …/candidates/{id}/resume/{version}/link?mode=download|preview` and `GET …/candidates/{id}/documents/{document_id}/link?mode=` (readers);
preview of a DOC/DOCX whose rendition is pending → 409, failed → 422; an unknown mode → 422; another candidate's document → 404. The URL
serves the file (download: attachment named `resume-CAN-…-vN.ext` / `<type>-CAN-…-<id8>.ext`; preview: inline PDF or image) and expires.
`POST …/resume/{version}/extract` of a DOC reads its rendition (409 pending, 422 failed).
**Documents:** `GET …/candidates/{id}/documents` → `{documents: [{id, document_type, title, file_name, content_type, size_bytes,
preview_status, request_id, uploaded_by: {id, full_name}, created_at}], requests: [{id, document_type, note, due_date, status, created_at,
received_at, cancelled_at, document_id, requested_by}]}` (readers; requests pending first). `POST …/candidates/{id}/documents` multipart
