`file`, `document_type`, `title?` (≤ 120; required for `other`), `request_id?` → 201 document (writers; PDF/DOC/DOCX/PNG/JPEG ≤ 5 MB else
415/413; unknown type 422; a request that is not this candidate's 404, not pending 409, of another type 422; archived 409).
`POST …/candidates/{id}/document-requests` `{document_type, note? (≤ 500), due_date? (not before today, IST)}` → 201 (writers; a pending
request of that type 409). `POST …/candidates/{id}/document-requests/{request_id}/cancel` → 200 (writers; not pending 409).
**The candidate's own** (IT student, else 403): `GET /api/v1/account/candidate-documents` → `{available, requests, documents}` (not in the
pool: `available: false` and empty lists; no cancelled requests; no staff names — `uploaded_by` is `"you"` | `"placement_team"`, no
`requested_by`). `POST /api/v1/account/candidate-documents` (same multipart; not in the pool 409). `GET
/api/v1/account/candidate-documents/{document_id}/link?mode=` (own documents only, else 404).

## 12JY. Vacancy approval and the Filled status (hrc-007) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-380`. Migration `0257_job_approval`. Design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-04-hrc-007-vacancy-approval-design.md`.

- Requirement statuses gain `pending_approval` (Pending Approval) and `filled` (Filled); neither is open (no apply, not listed to
  students/public). `GET /recruiter/requirements/statuses` lists 13, each with `phase` / `phase_label` (Draft, Pending Approval, Open,
  On Hold, Filled, Closed, Cancelled). Rows and details gain `phase`, `phase_label`, `source` (`recruiter` / `employer` / `legacy`); the
  detail gains `approval {required, submitted_by, submitted_at, can_submit, can_decide}`. While approval applies, `allowed_statuses`
  leaves out the open statuses and `POST …/{id}/status` into one is 409 ("needs approval").
- `POST /recruiter/requirements/{id}/submit` `{note?}` — the requirement's writer (scope 404, role 403); Draft / On Hold → Pending
  Approval when the source's setting is on and the job was never live; else 409. Audited `recruiter_requirement.submit`.
- `POST …/{id}/approve` `{note?}` → Requirement Received; `POST …/{id}/reject` `{reason}` (required, 422 when blank) → New. `jobs.approve`
  holder (403 `You cannot approve job requirements`), any job (404 if missing), 409 when not pending, 403 on one's own (submitter,
  creator or assigned recruiter). History note `Approved: …` / `Rejected: …`; audited `approve` / `reject`.
- `GET /recruiter/requirement-approvals?limit&offset` (`jobs.approve`, 403 otherwise): pending jobs, oldest submission first,
  `{items, total, limit, offset}`; items = the list row + `description`, `submitted_by`, `submitted_at`, `can_decide`.
- `GET /recruiter/requirement-approvals/settings` (`jobs.approve`): `{settings: {employer, recruiter, legacy}, can_edit}`; seeded
  employer on, the rest off. `PUT …/settings` `{employer?, recruiter?, legacy?}` — super_admin only (403), unknown keys 422; audited
  `recruiter_requirement.approval_settings`. Turning a source off leaves its pending jobs pending.
- Legacy writers: `PATCH /employer/jobs/{id}` and `PATCH /workflows/it/jobs/{id}` `status: "open"` submit for approval while it applies
  (the employer's `status` stays `draft`, `requirement_status` = `pending_approval`); Pending Approval reads `draft`, Filled `closed`.
  `POST /workflows/it/jobs` starts as `pending_approval` (response `status`) while legacy approval is on.
- An application becoming Joined fills the job (`filled`, note `All vacancies filled`) when the joined count reaches `vacancies`.
## 12JZ. Vacancy fields and possible duplicates (hrc-005) — addendum, 2026-11-04

DEC-SCOPE-381. `POST /api/v1/recruiter/requirements` and `PATCH /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}` accept, and every requirement body
returns, `requisition_reference` (≤ 80), `lead_source_id` (an active `rec_lead_sources` value; returned as `lead_source: {id, name} | null`; not hrc-007's create-path `source`),
`certifications` (≤ 1000, multi-line), `country`/`state`/`city` (≤ 120), `relocation_required` (bool | null), `travel_requirement`
(≤ 120), `salary_currency` (three letters, upper-cased; null = INR), `salary_period` (`year` | `month`; null = a year), `benefits`
(≤ 1000), `hiring_manager_user_id` (an active `interviewer`; returned as `hiring_manager: person | null`) and `expected_joining_date`.
The body also returns `updated_by: person | null`. `location` is no longer required on create: blank is derived as "City, State,
Country"; neither = 422 "Enter the job location or its city". Bad references are 422 ("Choose an active lead source" / "… hiring manager").
**Possible duplicate:** create, and an edit that changes title, city, location or requisition reference, answer 409 `{detail: {code:
"possible_duplicate", message, matches: [{id, code, title, location, status_label, requisition_reference}] (≤ 10), total}}` when another
`closed`/`cancelled`-excluded job of the same company has the same normalised title and city (else location), or the same requisition
reference. Re-send with `duplicate_reason` (≤ 500) to save; the audit row `recruiter_requirement.duplicate_override` keeps the reason and
the matched codes. A stale `expected_updated_at` is still 409 `record_changed` first.
`GET /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/hiring-managers?q=&limit=` (≤ 50) → `{items: [{id, full_name, email}], total}` — active interviewers;
recruiter, placement manager and super_admin only (else 403).
## 12KA. Candidate ownership (hrc-012) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-382`. **New:** `PUT /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/{id}/ownership` `{owner_user_id?, placement_officer_user_id?,
collaborator_user_ids?: [≤10]}` (partial; a key sent as null clears it — except the primary recruiter, which can be changed but not cleared;
the collaborator list is the whole new list) → `CandidateDetail`; a no-op writes nothing. Errors: 401; 403 (not a candidate writer, or a
recruiter who is not the primary recruiter); 404 (unknown / outside the pool); 409 (archived); 422 (bad body, "Choose an active
recruiter" / "Choose an active placement officer" / "Choose active recruiters or placement officers", "A candidate keeps one primary
recruiter", "Shared ownership is switched off", "The primary recruiter is already an owner"). `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/assignees
?kind=recruiter|officer|collaborator&q=` → `[{id, full_name, role}]` (≤20, active eligible people; candidate writers). `GET
/api/v1/recruiter/candidate-ownership-settings` → `{shared_ownership, can_edit}` (candidate readers); `PUT` `{shared_ownership: bool}`
(placement managers, super_admin; audited). **Changed (additive):** `GET /recruiter/candidates?owner=me|unassigned`; each item and the
detail carry `owner` (`{id, full_name, active}` | null); the detail carries `ownership` `{owner, placement_officer, collaborators[],
shared_ownership, can_assign}`; `POST /recruiter/candidates` makes a recruiter creator the owner; `GET /recruiter/manager/team` `work` and
`POST /recruiter/manager/reassign` `moved` gain `candidates`; the reassign moves them and its 409 reads "no companies, requirements or
candidates to reassign"; `PATCH /admin/users/{id}` `{active:false}` of a recruiter owning a non-archived candidate is 422; the placement
officer dashboard gains the "Your candidates" metric. Notice `it.candidate.assigned`.

## 12KB. Candidate profile completion (hrc-010) — addendum, 2026-11-04

DEC-SCOPE-383. `POST /api/v1/recruiter/candidates` and `PATCH /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/{id}` accept, and the candidate detail returns:
`education: [{degree, specialisation, institution, year, grade}]` (≤ 10; a degree or an institution), `employment: [{employer,
designation, industry, employment_type, start_date, end_date}]` (≤ 20; employer and start required; end null = current; no future dates;
end before start = 422 "Employment N: the end date must be on or after the start date"), `certifications: [{name, issuer, issued_on,
expires_on, credential_id}]` (≤ 20), `country`/`state`/`city` (≤ 80), `preferred_contact` (`phone` | `whatsapp` | `email` | null),
`relevant_experience_months` (0–600), `tools`/`languages` (≤ 20 texts), `preferred_roles` (≤ 5), `work_modes`/`employment_types`/`shifts`
(job enums), `available_from` (date), `willing_to_relocate` (bool | null). A sent list replaces the whole set; omitted = unchanged.
The detail also returns `email_verified_at` (read only; sending it is 422), `is_fresher` (experience 0) and `completeness: {percent,
items: [{key, label, weight, met}]}`. While employment rows exist `experience_months` and `current_company` are derived; education row 1
sets `qualification`/`college`/`passing_year`; city/state/country set `location`; `preferred_roles[0]` sets `preferred_role` (an old
client's `preferred_role` replaces the first role). A rows-only save is a new `updated_at` (hrc-003). Candidate skills add/PATCH accept
`is_primary` (bool; null = 422) and skill items return it. The merge response's `moved` adds `education`, `employment`, `certifications`.

## 12KC. Vacancy co-owners and ownership history (hrc-006) — addendum, 2026-11-04

DEC-SCOPE-384. Every requirement body gains `owners: [{user: {id, full_name, active, email}, role: "placement_officer" | "collaborator",
role_label, added_by: person, added_at}]` (the placement-officer owner first, then collaborators by name; the primary stays
`assigned_recruiter`) and `permissions.can_manage_owners` / `permissions.can_view_candidates`. **Scope change:** a recruiter's and a
placement manager's list and reads also cover requirements they (or their team) co-own; `placement_officer` is no longer 403 on
`GET /recruiter/requirements`, `/statuses`, `/{id}`, `/{id}/history`, `PATCH /{id}`, `POST /{id}/status|submit` and the `/{id}/jd*`
routes — it sees only the requirements it co-owns (others 404). Every other recruiter route still refuses it (403).
- `POST /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/assignments` `{user_id, role: "placement_officer" | "collaborator"}` → 201 `{requirement}`.
  403 unless the primary owner, a placement manager in scope or super_admin; 409 cancelled, "This person is the primary owner",
  "Already an owner of this requirement", "This requirement already has a placement officer owner"; 422 an ineligible person (inactive,
  wrong role, a recruiter without a profile) or `role: "primary"`. Audit `recruiter_requirement.owner_added`.
- `DELETE /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/assignments/{user_id}` → 200 `{requirement}`; 409 "A placement manager must reassign the
  primary owner first"; 404 not an owner. Audit `recruiter_requirement.owner_removed`.
- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/assignment-options?role=&q=&limit=` (≤ 50) → `{items: [{id, full_name, email, role}], total}` —
  eligible active people not already on it; owner managers only (else 403).
- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/assignment-history?limit=&offset=` (≤ 100) → `{items: [{id, user, role: "primary" | …,
  role_label, action: "added" | "removed", reason: null | "reassign" | "deactivated" | "backfill", changed_by: person | null, created_at}],
  total, limit, offset}`, newest first, `Cache-Control: no-store`; the requirement's readers.
`POST /{id}/assign` and `POST /recruiter/manager/reassign` now also write the primary's history (and drop the new primary's co-owner row);
`PATCH /admin/users/{id}` with `active: false` removes the user's co-ownerships.

## 12KD. Vacancy visibility (hrc-008) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-385`. **New:** `PUT /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/visibility` `{visibility: "internal"|"portal"|"segment"|"public",
segment_pool_id?}` (a segment needs an active talent pool, and only a segment names one) → `{requirement}` (the detail); the same values
write nothing. Errors: 401; 403 "You cannot change who sees job requirements" (no `jobs.publish`; checked before the body); 404 (unknown
job); 422 (bad body, "Choose an active talent pool"). **Changed (additive):** the requirement detail carries `visibility` `{key, label,
segment_pool: {id, name, active} | null, can_change}` and every requirement row carries `visibility` (the key). **Changed (behaviour):**
`GET /public/jobs` lists public jobs only and `POST /public/jobs/{id}/apply` answers 404 "Open job not found" for any other; for an
`it_student`, `GET /workflows/it/jobs/open`, `POST /workflows/it/jobs/{id}/apply` and the portal's job-applications "Open jobs" panel cover
portal, public and the student's segment jobs (staff callers of `/workflows/it/jobs/open` still see every open job); the employer job
payload's `visible_to_students` is false for an internal job. Existing jobs are public (migration 0262), so nothing changes until a
`jobs.publish` holder narrows a job.

## 12KE. External candidate portal (hrc-022) — addendum, 2026-11-04

DEC-SCOPE-386. Public (no session), JSON only: `POST /api/v1/candidate/register` `{full_name (2–160), email, phone?, password (10–128),
consent_version}` → **202** `{accepted: true}` for every email (development adds `development_verification_token`); a wrong consent
version or an invalid phone 422; more than 5 from one client IP in an hour **429** with `Retry-After`. `POST /candidate/resend-verification`
`{email}` → 202 always (at most 3 links per account per 15 minutes; over it nothing is sent). `POST /candidate/verify-email` `{token}` →
200 `{ok, purpose: "verify_email" | "email_change"}`; an unknown, used, superseded or expired token is one **400** "This link is invalid
or has expired"; an email change whose address was taken meanwhile 409. `POST /auth/login` for an unverified candidate: **403** "Verify
your email address first …" (after the password matched).
Candidate only (`role=candidate`; others 403, anonymous 401), always the caller's own record: `GET /candidate/me` → `{candidate_code,
name, email, email_verified, mobile, pending_email, fields: {name, city, state, country, linkedin, current_company, notice_days,
preferred_roles, tools, languages, available_from, willing_to_relocate, preferred_contact}, editable: [...]}` (404 when no candidate is
linked). `PATCH /candidate/me` takes those fields (recruiter validation and messages); any other key 422 "<Field> cannot be changed in
the portal"; a field not allow-listed **403** "<Field> cannot be changed here -- ask your recruiter"; audited as `candidate.update` with
`self_service: true` and old/new values. `POST /candidate/me/email` `{new_email, current_password}` → 202 (a link to the new address;
an address in use: the same 202, nothing sent); wrong password 400 (counts toward the change-password lockout); same address 422; more
than 3 requests in 15 minutes **429**. `GET /candidate/me/privacy` → `{opted_in, consent: {version, text}, history: [{action,
consent_version, created_at}]}`; `PUT` `{opted_in, consent_version}` → the same. `GET /recruiter/candidate-self-service-settings` →
`{fields, available, can_edit}` (candidate readers); `PUT {fields}` (placement_manager, super_admin; a field outside `available` 422).

## 12KF. Candidate notes, tasks and the complete timeline (hrc-016) — addendum, 2026-11-04

DEC-SCOPE-387. Candidate readers = `placement_team`, `placement_manager`, `super_admin`, `hr_team`; writers = the first three (rec-009).
Another role 403, anonymous 401, a candidate outside the pool 404.
- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/{id}/notes?limit=&offset=` → `{items: [{id, body, author: {id, full_name}, created_at}], total, limit,
  offset}`, newest first, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`; readers. The only route that serves a note's text.
- `POST /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/{id}/notes` `{body}` (1–2000 after trimming) → 201 note; writers; archived/merged candidate 409
  "Restore this candidate first". Append-only. Audit `candidate_note.create` (ids only).
- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/{id}/tasks?limit=&offset=` → page of tasks, open by due time then closed newest first; readers.
- `POST /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/{id}/tasks` `{task_type: follow_up | resume_screening | interview_coordination | employer_feedback,
  title (1–200), notes?, due_at (aware; future, within 12 months), assignee_user_id, application_id?, job_id?}` → 201 task; writers.
  422 "Choose an active recruiter or manager" (inactive, other role, unknown), "Choose an application of this candidate", "The application
  is for another requirement", "Choose a requirement"; archived 409. Audit `candidate_task.create`; notice `it.candidate.task_assigned`.
- `PATCH /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-tasks/{task_id}` (any of `task_type`, `title`, `notes`, `due_at`, `assignee_user_id`) → task; equal
  values are no change. `POST …/complete`, `POST …/cancel` → task. Writers; done/cancelled 409; unknown 404. Audit
  `candidate_task.update` (field names) / `.complete` / `.cancel`.
- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-tasks?due=today|overdue|upcoming&limit=&offset=` → `{items, total, limit, offset, day, counts: {today,
  overdue, upcoming}}`: the caller's own open tasks, oldest due first (IST days, the rec-024 rule). Writers; others 403 "Your role has no
  candidate tasks".
- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-tasks/assignees?q=` → `[{id, full_name, role}]` (≤ 20); writers.
- Task body: `{id, task_type, title, notes, due_at, status, overdue, assignee: {id, full_name, active}, candidate: {id, candidate_code,
  name}, application_id, requirement: {id, code, title} | null, created_by, created_at, completed_at, completed_by, cancelled_at,
  cancelled_by, can_change}`.
- `GET /recruiter/candidates/{id}/timeline` (rec-027) gains kinds `note` (reason = excerpt), `task` (event `scheduled` | `done` |
  `cancelled`, from_value = type, subject = title, to_label = assignee, scheduled_for = due), `assignment` (event = hrc-012 history kind,
  from_label / to_label = people, status = reason), `profile` (event `create` | `update` | `archive` | `restore` | `merge` |
  `merged_into`, to_value = changed-field count, subject = the other candidate's code on a merge) and `screening` (to_value / to_label =
  result, subject = requirement, context = company). Additive.
- `POST /recruiter/candidates/{id}/merge` (hrc-014): `moved` gains `notes` and `tasks`.

## 12KG. Skills taxonomy domains and a job skill's minimum level (hrc-017) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-388`. **Changed (additive):** every skill category row (`GET/POST/PATCH /api/v1/recruiter/skill-categories`) carries `domain`
(`"it"|"non_it"`); `POST` takes an optional `domain` (default `"it"`) and `PATCH` may change it (an explicit null or another value: 422,
"Domain …"; a non-writer: 403). `GET /recruiter/skill-categories` and `GET /recruiter/skills` take `?domain=it|non_it` (422 otherwise; a
skill matches through its primary category or a tag). Each item of `PUT /recruiter/requirements/{id}/skill-weights` may carry `min_level`
(`"beginner"|"intermediate"|"advanced"|"expert"|null`; omitted = unchanged, null = any level); a level on a skill outside the Skills Master
is a 422 "Only a skill in the Skills Master can have a minimum level"; the audit row gains `changes` `{"<skill> minimum level": [old, new]}`.
The requirement detail's `skills[]` and the matches `criteria.skills[]` carry `min_level`. **Changed (behaviour):** in
`GET /recruiter/requirements/{id}/matches` a skill with a minimum level counts only for a candidate holding it (or a related skill) at that
level or above. No level is set by migration 0265, so nothing changes until a writer sets one.

## 12KH. Website registration → candidate create/match (hrc-023) — addendum, 2026-11-04

DEC-SCOPE-389. `POST /api/v1/public/jobs/{job_id}/apply` (public) keeps its body and **201** `{id, tracking_code, status}` and adds
optional `consent_version` (a version other than the current `candidate-v1` 422), `utm` `{utm_source, utm_medium, utm_campaign,
utm_term, utm_content}` (other keys dropped, values trimmed to 100) and the honeypot `website` (filled: the same 201, nothing stored).
Fields over their column length, an invalid email or an unparseable phone 422. More than 10 applications from one client IP in an hour
**429** with `Retry-After`. The application creates or matches (exact email) a candidate with source "Website"; the answer never says
which. `POST /api/v1/candidate/register` (§12KE) adds optional `utm`. `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/{id}` adds `utm` and
`website_applications: [{job_title, tracking_code, status, created_at}]`; the merge preview/result `moved` adds `career_applications`.

## 12KI. Eligibility-change review (hrc-009) — addendum, 2026-11-04

DEC-SCOPE-390. Additive; every route keeps rec-017's requirement scope (out of scope = 404) and writer gate.
- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/candidates?needs_review=true` → the review list (flagged applications only). Every response
  of this route gains `needs_review_count` (the requirement's flagged applications, whatever the filter).
- Every application item (`/candidates` list, `/applications/{id}/status`, `/screening`, `/confirm-review`) gains `needs_review_since`
  (ISO time or null).
- `POST /api/v1/recruiter/applications/{id}/confirm-review` (no body) → `{application}` with `needs_review_since: null`, status unchanged;
  writers (`placement_team` on the requirement, `super_admin`); manager/BDM 403; not flagged 409 "This application does not need a
  review". Audit `recruiter_application.review_confirmed` (status and the flag's time).
- `PATCH /recruiter/requirements/{id}`, `PATCH /employer/jobs/{id}`, `PATCH /workflows/it/jobs/{id}`, `PUT /recruiter/requirements/{id}/
  skill-weights`: an eligibility change (required skills with their hrc-017 minimum level, experience range, qualification, matched
  location) flags the job's open applications and sends `it.requirement.review_needed`
  ("Applications to review: N. …", link `/recruiter/requirements/{id}`) to its owners but the editor. Responses unchanged.
- Any application status move clears `needs_review_since`.

## 12KJ. Candidate bulk import (hrc-013) — addendum, 2026-11-04

DEC-SCOPE-391. Every route needs a candidate writer holding `candidates.import` (else **403**, anonymous **401**).
`GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-imports/template` — the CSV header row (`no-store`). `POST /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-imports/preview`
(multipart `file` .csv/.xlsx ≤ 1 MB ≤ 1,000 rows, `source_id`) — **200** `{total_rows, new_count, created_count (0), matched_count,
error_count, rows: [{row_number, status: new|matched|error, name, candidate: {id, candidate_code, name}|null, same_as_row, reason}]}`;
writes nothing. `POST /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-imports` (same form + required `Idempotency-Key`) — **201** the report `{id, file_name,
source, uploaded_by, total_rows, created_count, matched_count, error_count, created_at, replayed, rows: [{row_number, status:
created|matched|error, candidate, reason}]}`; the same key with the same file and source, or the same file and source under another key,
replays (`replayed: true`); the key with another file or source **422**. Bad files: wrong type **415**, too large **413**, unknown /
repeated / missing columns, non-UTF-8 CSV, no rows or too many rows **422** (one `detail` string listing every problem); an inactive
source **422**; another import running past 5 s **409**. `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-imports?limit&offset` — the caller's imports
(placement manager and super_admin: all), newest first. `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-imports/{id}` — the report; **404** outside
that scope. `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-imports/{id}/report.csv` — `row_number, status, candidate_code, name, reason`, every text
cell formula-escaped.

## 12KK. Configurable matching engine with explanations (hrc-018) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-392`. **New:** `GET /api/v1/recruiter/match-settings` → `{"weights": {skills, experience, qualification, location, role,
salary}, "defaults": {…}, "can_edit": bool}` for candidate readers (others 403). `PUT /api/v1/recruiter/match-settings`
`{"weights": {…six whole numbers 0–100}}` → the same body; super_admin only (403 "Only a super admin can change the match weights"); a
missing/extra factor, a fraction, a value outside 0–100 or a total other than 100 is 422; one audit row
`recruiter_matching.weights` `{changes: {factor: [old, new]}}`. **Changed (additive):** `GET /recruiter/requirements/{id}/matches` —
`criteria.factors[]` (`key, label, weight, applies, points`) and `criteria.hard_filters` (`{experience|qualification|location|salary:
{on, available}}`); each item gains `explanation[]` (`key, label, text, points, max, matched`, one per applicable factor) and `skills`
(`required_matched`, `required_missing`, `preferred_matched`, `preferred_missing`: names); the body gains `disclaimer`. `breakdown[]`
keeps its shape and gains the kinds `qualification`, `role`, `salary`; a caller without `candidates.compensation.view` gets the salary
text "Salary details are restricted". `PUT /recruiter/requirements/{id}/skill-weights` takes optional `hard_filters` (`{experience?,
qualification?, location?, salary?: bool}`; omitted = unchanged; an unknown key is 422; turning one on without the job value is 422
"Set … on the requirement before making it a hard filter"); the audit row's `changes` gains `{"Hard filter: <key>": [old, new]}`.
**Changed (behaviour):** scores use the six weighted factors (rec-016's fixed experience and location points are gone), and an enabled
hard filter excludes candidates who miss it or have not recorded the value.

## 12KL. Candidate routing rules and unassigned queue (hrc-024) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-393`. **New** (placement managers and super_admin; every other role 403 "Only a placement manager can see or change candidate
routing", anonymous 401): `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-routing` → `{"default_recruiter": {id, full_name, active} | null, "strategy":
"round_robin" | "least_workload", "rules": [{id, kind: "source" | "skill_category" | "location", value_id, value_label, recruiter: {id,
full_name, active}, created_at}], "can_edit": true}` — rules in routing order (source, skill category, location; oldest first).
`PUT /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-routing` `{"default_recruiter_user_id": uuid | null, "strategy": …}` (both required) → the same body; a
default that is not an active recruiter is 422 "Choose an active recruiter"; audit `candidate.routing_settings`.
`POST /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-routing/rules` `{"kind", "source_id" | "skill_category_id" | "location" (≤ 80, trimmed),
"recruiter_user_id"}` → 201 one rule; the kind's value missing or unknown is 422 ("Choose a candidate source", "Choose a skill category",
"Enter a location"), the other kinds' values are ignored, an inactive or non-recruiter is 422, the same kind/value/recruiter again is 409
"This recruiter already has this rule"; audit `candidate.routing_rule_add`. `DELETE /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-routing/rules/{id}` → 204
(404 "Routing rule not found"); audit `candidate.routing_rule_delete`. **Reused:** the queue is `GET /recruiter/candidates?owner=unassigned`
and assignment is `PUT /recruiter/candidates/{id}/ownership` (§12KA). **Changed (behaviour):** `POST /public/jobs/{id}/apply` and the
candidate portal's email verification route a candidate they create (no response change); the candidate timeline's `assignment` rows may
have `status: "route"` and no actor. New notification event `it.candidate.unassigned` ("Candidate needs a recruiter").
## 12KM. Application record completion (hrc-026) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-394`. **Changed (additive):** `GET /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/candidates` items, and the `application` in every
recruiter application response, gain `code` (`JA-000001`), `source` (`portal|recruiter|employer|drive`), `source_label`, `owner`
(`{id, full_name}` or null), `rejection_reason` and `rejection_reason_label` (null unless rejected); the list gains
`rejection_reasons[]` (`{key, label}`) and each `statuses[]` entry gains `candidate_visible` and `candidate_label`.
`GET /recruiter/applications/{id}/history` items gain `rejection_reason_label`. `GET /recruiter/candidates/{id}/applications` items gain
`code`. `POST /api/v1/workflows/it/jobs/{id}/apply` → `{id, code, status}`. `GET /api/v1/portal/it/student/job-applications` gains the
column `code` ("Application ID"); its `status` is the candidate-visible stage in the candidate's words. **Changed (behaviour):**
`POST /recruiter/applications/{id}/status` takes optional `rejection_reason` (one of the eleven keys); a move to `rejected` without one is
422 "Choose a rejection reason", `other` without a note is 422 "Add a note explaining the rejection reason", and a reason on any other
move is 422. `PATCH /workflows/it/job-applications/{id}` applies the same rule through `rejection_reason` in its body. The audit row's
metadata carries `rejection_reason`. A student's status notice is sent only when the candidate-visible stage changes (never for Profile
Shared), and a portal apply sends an "Application received" notice with the code.
## 12KO. Candidate consent, retention, deletion and access revocation (hrc-035) — addendum, 2026-11-04

`DEC-SCOPE-396`. **New:** `POST /api/v1/candidate/me/erasure-request` (role `candidate` only; no body) → the Privacy body; 201 for a new
request, 200 when one is already open. `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-data-requests?state=open|decided` (`candidates.erase`; others
403) → `{"items": [{id, status: in_progress|rejected|fulfilled, requested_at, decided_at, rejection_reason, candidate: {id,
candidate_code, name, email, anonymised} | null, open_applications, held}]}` — open oldest first, decided newest first (100).
`POST /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-data-requests/{id}/decision` `{"decision": "approve"|"reject", "reason"?}` → one item; 404 unknown /
not a candidate's; 409 already decided; 409 "This candidate has an offer in progress…" (held); 422 a reject without a reason or over 500
characters; audit `candidate.erasure_approved|rejected` (ids and a file count only). `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidate-retention`
(`candidates.erase`) → `{months|null, range: [6, 120], due_now, last_run_at, last_run_count, can_edit}`; `PUT` `{"months": int|null}`
super_admin only (403 otherwise; 422 outside 6–120), audit `candidate.retention_settings`. **Changed (additive):** `GET|PUT
/candidate/me/privacy` gain `erasure` (`{status, requested_at, decided_at, rejection_reason}` | null); `POST /recruiter/candidates`
takes an optional `lawful_basis` (`consent` | `provided_for_placement`; other values 422); `POST /recruiter/candidate-imports` takes
an optional form field `lawful_basis`; `GET /recruiter/candidates/{id}` gains `consent` (`{action, source, lawful_basis,
consent_version, recorded_at}` | null). **Changed (behaviour):** an anonymised candidate is 404 on every recruiter route and absent from
lists (archived too); `PATCH /admin/data-requests/{id}` answers 409 for a `candidate` subject.

## 12KP. Candidate engagement status (hrc-021) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-397`. **Changed (additive):** `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidates` items, `GET /recruiter/candidates/{id}` and the
`POST /recruiter/candidates/search` cards (and so talent-pool member cards) gain `engagement`: `{key, label}` with `key` one of `new`,
`uncontacted`, `contacted`, `screened`, `shortlisted`, `active`, `inactive`, `placed`, `not_interested`, `do_not_contact` — derived, read
only, never accepted in a body. `GET /recruiter/candidates` takes an optional `engagement=<key>`; `POST /recruiter/candidates/search`
takes an optional `engagement` key (label "Engagement"); any other value is 422. The stored `status` field and its filter are unchanged.
No new endpoint, permission or migration.
## 12KQ. Candidate-facing job recommendations and apply (hrc-027) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-398`. **New:** `GET /api/v1/candidate/jobs?view=recommended|matching|all&q=&limit=&offset=` (an `it_student` or `candidate`
session; other roles 403, anonymous 401; `view` other than the three, `q` over 100 characters, `limit` outside 1–100 or a negative
`offset` → 422) → `{view, q, items[], total, limit, offset, counts: {recommended, matching, all}, reason: "no_skills"|null, disclaimer,
threshold}`. Each item: `{id, title, company, location, work_mode, employment_type, required_skills[], preferred_skills[],
experience_min_months, experience_max_months, closes_on, match: {score, explanation[]}|null, skills_text, matches_skills, recommended,
application: {code, status_label}|null, can_apply, blocked[]}` — no salary field. `POST /api/v1/candidate/jobs/{job_id}/apply` (no body)
→ 201 `{id, code, status_label}`; a job outside the caller's visibility, closed or past its date → 404 "Open job not found"; already
applied → 409 "Already applied"; a switched-on hard filter not met → 422 naming it. **Changed (behaviour):**
`POST /api/v1/workflows/it/jobs/{id}/apply` applies the same hard-filter gate (422). A portal candidate's application notices carry
`action_url` `/it/careers/portal/jobs`.

## 12KR. Advanced multi-filter resume search (hrc-019) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-399`. **Changed (additive):** `POST /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/search` accepts these optional body fields (all default to
"no filter"; labels in `CANDIDATE_SEARCH_LABELS`; an unknown key is still 422):
`preferred` (≤ 10 skill terms, counted in the 20-term cap; never filters unless it is the only skill input), `category_ids` (≤ 10 ids),
`min_level` (`beginner`…`expert`), `skill_min_months` (0–600); `degree`, `specialisation`, `institution` (contains, ≤ 120),
`passing_year_min`/`_max` (1950–2100), `cgpa_min`/`_max` (0–10); `fresher` (bool), `relevant_min_months`/`_max`, `designation`,
`industry`, `employment_types` (job employment types); `country`, `state`, `city`, `preferred_location`, `willing_to_relocate` (bool),
`work_modes`; `preferred_role`, `preferred_employment_types`; `owner` (`me` | `unassigned`), `registered_from`/`_to` (`YYYY-MM-DD`, IST);
`has_resume` (bool), `document_types`, `application_statuses`, `interviewed` (bool); `current_min`/`_max`, `minimum_min`/`_max` (annual
INR), `package_unknown` (bool); `sort` (`relevance` default, `match`, `experience`, `package`, `registered`, `activity`, `completeness`).
A search needs a skill term (`all`/`any`/`preferred`), a category or resume text. Inverted ranges are 422.
**AuthZ:** any of `salary_min`, `salary_max`, `current_*`, `minimum_*`, `package_unknown: true` or `sort: "package"` without
`candidates.compensation:view` is **403** before anything is read. **Response (additive):** each card gains `match`:
`{required: [{term, skill, held}], preferred: [{term, skill, held}]}`. Talent-pool member cards are unchanged. No new endpoint or
permission; migration `0275` adds indexes only.

## 12KS. Interview completion, cancellation, link history and calendar (hrc-028) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-400`, migration `0276_interview_completion`. **Changed (additive):** `POST /api/v1/recruiter/interviews` and
`PATCH /recruiter/interviews/{id}` accept `round_number` (1–20), `ends_at` (after `scheduled_at`, ≤ 12 h later; else 422 on `ends_at`),
`time_zone` (IANA name, default `Asia/Kolkata`; unknown 422; PATCH cannot clear it), `platform` (`google_meet`, `microsoft_teams`, `zoom`,
`zoho_meeting`, `other`), `phone_details` (≤ 200), `instructions` (≤ 2000, multi-line), `documents_required` (≤ 1000, multi-line). PATCH
also takes `notify` (default true): when a candidate-visible field changed, the candidate (and the contact) are notified and the reply is
the interview plus `notifications`; otherwise the reply is unchanged. Every interview item gains those fields plus `local_when` (the time in
the candidate's zone), `platform_label`, `cancel_reason` and `can_cancel`; `history` rows gain `old_meeting_url`, `new_meeting_url`,
`old_location`, `new_location` (set on the new `details` event). A PATCH of a cancelled interview is 409. A reschedule moves `ends_at` with
the start.

**New:** `POST /api/v1/recruiter/interviews/{id}/cancel` `{reason (required, ≤ 500), notify (default true)}` → 200
`{interview, notifications}`; status `cancelled` (label "Cancelled"); 409 unless Scheduled, Confirmed, Rescheduled or On Hold; 422 on a
blank reason; 403 for readers, 404 out of scope. Cancelled interviews list under `view=closed`.

Notices (schedule, reschedule, update, cancel) carry an iCalendar invite: the student's email delivery has context
`{kind: "interview_invite", ics, method}`; a recruiter email row stores `calendar_ics`, attached as `invite.ics` by the worker.
`GET /api/v1/portal/it/student/interview-schedule` rows are now `{id, title, round, role, company, when, mode, link, details, status}`
(`link` null once cancelled; `status` "Cancelled — <reason>" for a cancel; decisions still read Completed).

## 12KT. Saved candidate searches and the Find Candidates export (hrc-020) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-401`. **New.** All under `/api/v1/recruiter/candidates`, for Find Candidates readers (recruiters, placement managers,
super_admin, hr_team); other roles 403 before the body is read, anonymous 401.
- `GET /saved-searches` → `{items: [{id, name, filters, ignored, shared, mine, can_edit, owner: {id, name}, updated_at}]}` — the caller's
  own and every shared search (own first, by name). `filters` is a Find Candidates body (§12KR); `ignored` names keys the search no longer
  declares and, without `candidates.compensation:view`, the package filters / package sort that were removed.
- `POST /saved-searches` `{name (1–80), filters, shared?}` → **201** the row. `filters` is validated as a search body (its 422 sentences);
  package filters or sort without the compensation grant 403; a duplicate name (per owner, case-insensitive) 409; more than 50 → 422.
- `PATCH /saved-searches/{id}` `{name?, filters?, shared?}` (at least one) → 200 the row. `DELETE /saved-searches/{id}` → 204. Owner or
  super_admin only: an invisible id is 404, a shared search of someone else 403.
- `POST /search/export?format=csv|xlsx` with a search body → the file (`candidates.csv` / `.xlsx`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`).
  Needs `candidates.export` (403 "Your role cannot export candidates", checked before the body); package filters without the compensation
  grant 403; more than 5,000 rows 422; more than 30 exports in 10 minutes 429 with `Retry-After`. Columns: Candidate ID, Name, Preferred
  role, Current company, Experience (years), Location, Notice (days), Skills, Source, Status, Engagement, and Expected salary (₹ a year)
  only with the compensation grant; never contact details. One audit row `crm.export` (`entity_type` `candidate_search`, `entity_id`
  `find_candidates`, metadata `{format, filters, rows}`). Migration `0277` adds the table and seeds the grant.
## 12KU. New Website Registrations workspace (hrc-025) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-402`, no migration. **New:** `GET /api/v1/recruiter/candidates/website-registrations?view=&limit=&offset=` (declared before
`/{candidate_id}`) → `{items, total, limit, offset, sla_hours}`. `view` ∈ `unassigned`, `new`, `contacted`, `screened`, `active`, `inactive`,
`duplicate_review` (else 422); `limit` 1–100 (default 50). Each item: `{id, candidate_code, name, registered_at, location, skills[] (≤ 3,
primary first), skill_count, completeness (0–100), owner {id, full_name, active} | null, engagement {key, label}, first_contact_at,
next_follow_up_at, conversion (registered | applied | placed), contact_sla (met | pending | breached), duplicate_review, can_open}`. Only
candidates with source Website, in the pool, not archived; newest first. No email or phone. Read only (no audit row). A `placement_team`
caller gets their own and the unassigned ones; 403 for roles outside §2.295; 401 signed out.

## 12KV. Commercial-data permissions (pob-001) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-403`. **Changed (additive):** `GET /api/v1/admin/permissions/catalogue` lists `commercials.client` (view, edit),
`commercials.vendor` (view, edit) and `candidates.contact` (view). Contract responses (`GET|POST|PATCH /recruiter/companies/{id}/contracts`,
`PUT …/contracts/document`) leave out `fee_basis`, `fee_value`, `payment_terms` and `replacement_policy` without
`commercials.client.view` (absent, not null); `permissions` gains `can_view_terms` and `can_edit_terms`, and the company's contracts gain
top-level `can_view_terms` and `can_edit_terms`. `GET /recruiter/performance/by-recruiter` gains `revenue_visible`; without the grant
`revenue` is absent from every row and the total. **Changed (behaviour):** a contract POST/PATCH carrying any terms field (null included)
without effective edit → 403 "Your role cannot view or change client commercial terms"; the contract/MoU upload needs effective edit and
the download needs view (403, after the scope's 404). Every rec-031 revenue route (`GET /recruiter/requirements/{id}/revenue`,
`PUT …/fee`, `POST /recruiter/invoices`, `…/payments`, `…/cancel`, `GET /recruiter/companies/{id}/revenue`, `GET /recruiter/invoices`)
→ 403 "Your role cannot view client commercials" without view; `PUT …/fee` also needs edit (403) and `permissions.can_set_fee` follows it.

## 12KX. Interview panel and the interviewer's workspace (hrc-029) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-405`. **New (recruiter):** `GET /api/v1/recruiter/interviews/{id}/interviewer-options` → `{items: [{id, full_name, email, kind
(internal|employer), kind_label}]}` — active `interviewer` users, then active employer users of the requirement's company; rec-017 writers
only (403 for the manager/BDM readers, 404 out of scope). `PUT /api/v1/recruiter/interviews/{id}/interviewers {interviewers: [{user_id,
is_lead}]}` (≤10, distinct, ≤1 lead; 422) replaces the panel → `{interview}`; 422 on `interviewers` for an ineligible user, 409 on a
Selected/Rejected/Cancelled interview or when removing a member who has submitted feedback; new members get an in-app notification.
**Changed (additive):** every recruiter interview item carries `interviewers: [{id, full_name, kind, kind_label, is_lead, feedback:
{attendance, attendance_label, score, recommendation, recommendation_label, notes, updated_at} | null}]` (lead first) and `can_assign`.
**New (panel member — roles `interviewer` and `employer`, any other role 403, signed out 401):** `GET /api/v1/interviewer/interviews
?view=upcoming|past&limit&offset` → `{items, total, limit, offset, counts: {upcoming, past}}`; `GET /api/v1/interviewer/interviews/{id}`
(not assigned, or an employer no longer at the requirement's company → 404); each item: `id, code, round_label, round_number,
scheduled_at, ends_at, mode, platform_label, meeting_url (null once cancelled), location, phone_details, instructions, status,
status_label (decisions read Completed), cancel_reason, is_lead, candidate {code, name, location, qualification, experience_months,
current_company, skills}, requirement {code, title}, company {name}, has_resume, feedback, can_feedback, feedback_overdue`.
`GET /api/v1/interviewer/interviews/{id}/resume` → the resume version shared at assignment (attachment, `private, no-store`), audited as
`interviewer.resume_download`; 404 "Resume not available" without one, once cancelled, for an archived candidate or after the interview
end + 7 days. `PUT /api/v1/interviewer/interviews/{id}/feedback {attendance: attended|no_show, score 1–5, recommendation:
strong_yes|yes|no|strong_no, notes ≤2000}` → the item; attended needs score and recommendation (422 on each field), no-show clears them;
409 before the interview time, when cancelled or decided. Panel feedback never changes the interview or the application. No candidate,
student or employer-list route returns it.

## 12KZ. Company business context, last contact and documents (pob-003) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-406`, migration `0283`. **Changed (additive):** `POST /api/v1/recruiter/companies` and `PATCH /recruiter/companies/{id}` accept
`products_services` (≤ 2000), `departments_hiring`, `hiring_locations`, `target_roles` (lists of ≤ 20 strings ≤ 120; trimmed, blanks and
case-insensitive repeats dropped; `null` 422 — send `[]`), `branch_locations` (≤ 50 `{city (required), state?, country?}`, replaced whole)
and `skill_demand_ids` (≤ 30 distinct skill ids, replaced whole; a newly added inactive/unknown skill 422 "Choose an active skill"). The
company detail returns those fields (`skill_demand` as `[{id, name, active}]`) and `last_contacted_at`; list rows gain
`last_contacted_at`; `GET /recruiter/companies` accepts `sort=name|last_contacted` (default `name`; else 422). `last_contacted_at` is the
latest company call, message or completed meeting and changes only through those writes. **New:** `GET /recruiter/companies/{id}/documents
?kind=&meeting_id=&limit=&offset=` → `{items, total, limit, offset, can_upload, can_upload_agreement}`, newest first; item `{id, kind, name,
content_type, size_bytes, meeting {id, code, starts_at} | null, uploaded_by, created_at, can_download}`. `POST /recruiter/companies/{id}/
documents` multipart `kind`, `file`, `meeting_id?` → 201 `{document}`: 403 without `can_edit` (or, for `recruitment_agreement`, effective
`commercials.client.edit`), 409 archived or 200 documents, 422 bad kind / empty file / meeting of another company, 413 too large, 415 not
PDF/JPEG/PNG by content, 429 + `Retry-After` past 20 uploads an hour. `GET /recruiter/companies/documents/{document_id}/download` → the
bytes as an attachment named `<kind>-<company code>.<ext>` (`no-store`, `nosniff`, sandbox CSP); an agreement needs
`commercials.client.view` (403). Out of scope = 404 everywhere. Audit `recruiter_company_document.uploaded|downloaded`.

## 12LA. Requirement revisions, hiring process and per-requirement terms (pob-002) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-407`. **New:** `GET /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/revisions?limit&offset` → `{items: [{revision, author, reason,
created_at, changed_fields: [{field, label}]}], total, limit, offset}`, newest first; `GET …/revisions/{n}?compare_to=m` → `{revision,
author, reason, created_at, compared_to, values: [{field, label, value}], changes: [{field, label, before, after}] | null}` (default
compare n-1; none for revision 1). Both: the requirement's read scope (404), an unknown revision 404, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`;
commercial fields are left out without `commercials.client.view`. **Changed (additive):** `POST|PATCH /recruiter/requirements` accept
`interview_rounds`, `screening_steps` (≤10 × ≤120), `decision_maker_contact_id`, `expected_tat_days` (1–365), `payment_trigger`,
`payment_days` (1–365, with and only with `days_after_joining`), `payment_terms`, `replacement_terms` (≤2000), `backup_owner_user_id`, and
PATCH `revision_reason` (≤500). The requirement detail gains `backup_owner`, `interview_rounds`, `screening_steps`, `decision_maker`
`{id, name, designation, active}`, `expected_tat_days` and, with the grant, `commercial_terms` `{fee: {basis, value, inherited},
payment_trigger, payment_trigger_label, payment_days, payment_terms: {value, inherited}, replacement_terms: {value, inherited}, can_edit}`.
`GET …/assignment-options` accepts `role=backup`. **Errors:** a terms field without effective edit → 403 "Your role cannot view or change
client commercial terms"; a backup change by anyone but the primary, the backup or super_admin → 403; the primary as backup → 409; an
ineligible backup or decision-maker, or a days/trigger mismatch → 422.

## 12LD. College placement profiles (pob-013) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-410`. A college-module college or university organization (bdm-002) gains a placement profile kept by the placement team; the
organization row, its MoU and its commercials stay the BDM's. Readers: `placement_team` (with a recruiter profile) and `placement_manager`
see every active one; `bdm`/`bdm_manager` their bdm-002 scope; `super_admin` all; other roles 403; out of scope (or archived, for the
placement team) 404. Writers: `placement_team`, `super_admin` and the assigned BDM; managers 403; archived 409 "Restore this organization
first". Every write locks the organization row, writes one `bdm_organization.placement_*` audit row and returns `{college}`.

| Method | Path | Body | Success |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/recruiter/colleges?q=&city=&course=&pass_out_year=&limit=&offset=` | — | 200 `{items:[{id, code, name, org_type, city, state, assigned_bdm_name, course_count, next_pass_out_year, archived}], total, limit, offset}` |
| GET | `/recruiter/colleges/{org_id}` | — | 200 `{college}` |
| PUT | `/recruiter/colleges/{org_id}/profile` | `{drive_process?, eligibility_rules?}` (≤ 2000, multi-line; omitted = cleared) | 200 |
| POST | `/recruiter/colleges/{org_id}/courses` | `{department?, course}` (≤ 120) | 201; duplicate 409 "This course is already listed"; > 100 409 |
| DELETE | `/recruiter/colleges/{org_id}/courses/{course_id}` | — | 200; with batches 409 "Remove this course's batches first" |
| POST | `/recruiter/colleges/{org_id}/batches` | `{pass_out_year (2000–2100), course_id?, pass_out_date? (in that year), student_count? (0–100,000), notes? (≤ 500)}` | 201; another college's course 422 |
| DELETE | `/recruiter/colleges/{org_id}/batches/{batch_id}` | — | 200 |
| POST | `/recruiter/colleges/{org_id}/calendar` | `{title, kind, starts_on, ends_on? (≥ start), notes?}`; kind ∈ pre_placement_talk, campus_drive, assessment, interview, exam, holiday, other | 201 |
| DELETE | `/recruiter/colleges/{org_id}/calendar/{event_id}` | — | 200 |
| POST | `/recruiter/colleges/{org_id}/interactions` | `{channel, occurred_at (aware, ≤ now + 5 min), contact_id?, summary (≤ 2000), next_step? (≤ 500)}`; channel ∈ meeting, call, email, whatsapp, visit, other | 201; future 422; another college's contact 422 |
| PATCH | `/recruiter/colleges/{org_id}/contacts/{contact_id}` | `{office_details? (≤ 300), preferred_contact_method? ∈ email, phone, whatsapp, in_person, linkedin}` | 200 |

`college`: `{id, code, name, org_type, city, state, website, student_count, affiliation, college_type, courses_note, relationship_stage,
assigned_bdm_name, archived, permissions: {can_edit}, profile: {drive_process, eligibility_rules, updated_at, updated_by_name}, contacts[]
(bdm-002 fields + office_details, preferred_contact_method), courses[], batches[] (with the course name), calendar[] (by start date),
interactions[] (latest 50, newest first, with logged_by_name), interaction_total, drives[] (rec-029, ≤ 20: drive_code, drive_date, job_role,
status)}`. No MoU, pipeline, assignment, phone/email of the organization or commercial field. `relationship_stage` is the bdm-004 stage
label (the §6.1 status mapping is `NEEDS_CONFIRMATION`). The bdm-002 contact output is unchanged.

## 12LC. Interview result approval and release (hrc-030) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-409`, migration `0289`. **New:**
- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/interview-results?state=pending_approval|released&limit&offset` — `results.release` holders (else 403). Page
  `{items, total, limit, offset, counts: {pending_approval, released}}`; waiting oldest first, published newest first. Item: `id, outcome
  (selected|rejected), outcome_label (Selected|Not selected), is_final, state, state_label, candidate_message, release_mode (auto|approval|
  null), released_at, interview {id, code, round, scheduled_at}, candidate {id, code, name}, requirement {id, code, title}, company {id,
  name}, decided_by {id, full_name}|null, decided_at, can_release` (false on the caller's own decision).
- `POST /api/v1/recruiter/interview-results/{id}/release {candidate_message?: string ≤1000}` — holders; 404 unknown; 403 own decision; 409
  already published (final). Blank/omitted message = the default text. 200 = the published queue item (as above). Notifies the candidate (in-app "Interview result" or a queued email).
- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/interview-results/settings` — holders: `{auto_release, can_edit}`. `PUT` `{auto_release: bool}` — super_admin only
  (else 403); 422 for a non-boolean.
- `GET /api/v1/candidate/results` — a candidate or IT student (else 403; 401 signed out): `{items: [{id, round, role, company, outcome,
  outcome_label, message, released_at}]}`, the caller's own released results only.

**Changed (additive):** every recruiter interview read (`/recruiter/interviews*`, `/recruiter/applications/{id}/interviews`) gains
`result_release` (the result object or null; the legacy `result` field is unchanged). `POST /recruiter/interviews/{id}/status` and `PUT
…/feedback` into Selected/Rejected record the result. `POST /recruiter/applications/{id}/offer` and the legacy `POST /workflows/it/offers`
answer 409 "Publish the interview result before making an offer" while the application's final result is unpublished. The student's
interview schedule shows the published outcome; the student's job applications and the candidate's jobs show Selected/Rejected only after
publishing.
## 12LE. Contact status, decision-maker, duplicates and per-contact timeline (pob-005) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-411`. **Changed (additive):** `POST /recruiter/companies/{id}/contacts` and `PATCH /recruiter/contacts/{id}` accept
`is_decision_maker` (bool), `source` (`linkedin|company_website|referral|job_portal|event|inbound|other`|null), `verified_on` (date, not in
the future) and `status` (`not_contacted|contacted|replied|meeting_scheduled|not_interested|do_not_contact`); `preferred_channel` adds
`linkedin`. Each list item gains those fields plus `status_changed_at` and the derived `follow_up_due`. Message items gain `replied_at`.
`GET /recruiter/companies/{id}/timeline` and `GET /recruiter/companies/{id}/follow-ups` accept `contact_id` (another company's contact →
404); the timeline adds kinds `message_reply` (both views) and `contact_status` (per-contact view only; `event` is the cause).
**New:** `GET /recruiter/companies/{id}/contacts/duplicates?mobile=&email=&exclude=` → `{matches: [{id, name, active, company: {id, name},
same_company, on: ["mobile"|"email"]}], others}` (≤10 matches, in the caller's scope only; `others` counts the rest; company read scope,
404). `POST /recruiter/messages/{id}/reply` `{replied_at?}` → the message (contact messages only, else 422; not before `sent_at`, not in
the future → 422; set once, a repeat returns it unchanged; `can_edit` 403, out of scope 404, archived 409). **Errors:** a Do Not Contact
contact → 409 "This contact has asked not to be contacted" on `POST /recruiter/messages`, an outgoing `POST /recruiter/calls`, a profile
share to them, and a meeting create/edit that adds them.

## 12LF. Candidate portal completion (hrc-031) — addendum, 2026-11-06

`DEC-SCOPE-412`. **New:** `GET /candidate/applications` and `GET /candidate/interviews` (`candidate` or `it_student`, else 403) → a portal
payload `{title, subtitle, columns, rows, metrics, actions, panels}` of the caller's own records; no id is taken. Application rows:
`code, role, company, location, status` (the candidate-visible stage), `applied_on` ("06 Nov 2026", IST), `next_action`. Interview rows as
`GET /portal/it/student/interview-schedule`. **Changed (additive):** `GET /portal/it/student/job-applications` gains the `applied_on` and
`next_action` columns; `GET /portal/it/student/{results|documents|privacy}` are new header-only sections; `.../notifications` gains a
"Notification preferences" action; `GET /auth/me` gains `email_verified`. **Widened to `candidate`:** `GET|POST
/account/candidate-documents`, `GET /account/candidate-documents/{id}/link` (a candidate needs no pool consent; `available: false` when
archived or merged; someone else's document 404) and `GET /workflows/it/student/offers[/{id}/letter]` (someone else's 404). **Changed:**
`action_url` of a candidate's new application, result and offer notices is `/it/careers/portal/applications`, and of interview notices
`/it/careers/portal/interviews`; a student's are unchanged. The application and interview payloads carry `stack: true` (labelled rows on a
phone). No migration.

## 12LG. Recruitment notifications and delivery exceptions (hrc-032) — addendum, 2026-11-06

`DEC-SCOPE-413`. Both routes need `notifications.retry` (403 otherwise; signed out 401).

- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/notification-exceptions?type=&channel=email|whatsapp|sms&limit=1..100&offset=` → `{items, total, limit, offset, types: [{key, label}]}`.
  Newest failure first. An item is `{source: "delivery"|"email", id, type, type_label, recipient, channel, error, attempts, failed_at}`; `error` has URLs
  masked and is null for an email. Never a body, subject or address. An unknown `channel` is 422; an unknown `type` matches nothing.
- `POST /api/v1/recruiter/notification-exceptions/{source}/{id}/retry` → `{id, source, status: "queued"}`. Out of scope or missing 404; no longer failed
  409 (a double click sends once). Audited as `notification.retry`.
- Additive: notices gain event types (`GET /workflows/notifications`' `event_type`). Changed text: `it.application.updated` and
  `it.interview.result_published` are fixed text (see `DEC-SCOPE-413` N4/N5).
- Beat: `hrc032-feedback-overdue` and `hrc032-new-matches`, hourly.
## 12LH. Screening forms, question bank, sessions and comparison (pob-020) — addendum, 2026-11-06

`DEC-SCOPE-414`. All under `/api/v1/recruiter`; every route 401 signed out. rec-018's `GET/PUT /applications/{id}/screening` keep their contract;
each changed PUT also appends a `legacy` session.

**Bank:** `GET screening/questions?q=&category=&include_archived=&limit=&offset=` → `{items, total, categories, formats}`. `POST screening/questions`
`{category, text, format, choices?, evaluation_criteria?, expected_answer?}` → 201 `{question}` (a choice question needs two different choices,
others none; 422; the same text in a category 409). `PATCH screening/questions/{id}` (any of those, `active`) → `{question}`; 404 unknown. Forms
copy a question, so an edit never changes a form.

**Templates:** `GET screening/templates?include_archived=&limit=` → `{items: [{id, name, is_default, version, archived, questions}], categories, formats}`.
`POST screening/templates` `{name, questions[1..20]}` → 201 `{template}`; `PATCH screening/templates/{id}` `{name?, questions?, archived?}` → `{template}`.
A duplicate name 409 (case-insensitive); archiving the default template 409.

**A question in a form or template:** `{question_id, is_knockout?}` (copied from the bank; must be active) or `{category, text, format, choices?,
evaluation_criteria?, expected_answer?, is_knockout?}`. 422: a knock-out that is not yes/no, in Compensation, or expecting other than yes/no; the same
text twice.

**A requirement's form:** `GET requirements/{id}/screening-form` → `{form|null, templates, can_edit, can_approve, categories, formats}`. `POST` `{template_id?}`
→ 201 `{form}` from that template or the default (422 archived/unknown; 409 already has one). `PUT` `{questions}` → `{form}`, a new version unless the
questions are identical (409 no form yet). Both 409 on a closed or cancelled requirement. `POST .../approve-knockouts` `{version}` → `{form}` (403 unless
Team Lead; 409 stale version or nothing pending). `form` = `{id, name, version, knockout_status: none|pending|approved, knockout_status_label,
knockout_approved_by, knockout_approved_at, questions}`.

**Sessions:** `GET applications/{id}/screening-sessions` → `{form|null, sessions (newest first), can_run, sees_compensation, recommendations}`. `POST` `{form_version,
answers: [{question_id, answer, score?}], notes?, recommendation: pass|hold|reject, knockout_override?, override_reason?}` → 201 `{session}`. Session =
`{id, kind: form|legacy, form_version, screened_by, created_at, notes, recommendation, recommendation_label, knockout_triggered, knockout_overridden, override_reason,
answers: [{question_id, question, category, format, is_knockout, answer, answer_hidden?, score}], scorecard: {total, max, percent}|null, checklist|null}`.
**Errors:** 409 application not open, no form, or `form_version` not current; 422 an answer outside its format or not on the form, a repeat, a score outside 0–5,
an approved knock-out unanswered, an override without a reason or a failed knock-out; 403 a Compensation answer without the grant.

**Comparison:** `GET requirements/{id}/screening-comparison?version=` → `{version, versions, questions, rows, truncated}`; each candidate's latest session on
that version (default current), best score first, at most 50 (most recently screened when capped). 404 unknown version; no form or no sessions is an empty 200.


## 12LI. Operational recruitment dashboards (hrc-033) — addendum, 2026-11-06

`DEC-SCOPE-415`. **New**, all under `/recruiter/recruitment-dashboards`, `{board}` one of `funnel|vacancy|recruiter|officer|interview` (else 422), and
query `from`, `to` (IST dates: at most 366 days, never future, else 422), `owner`, `employer`, `job` (UUIDs, else 422), ANDed with the caller's scope:

- `GET /{board}` (`reports.recruitment.view`) → `{board, label, note, owner_label, date_from, date_to, filters, sections: [{key, label, figures: [{key, label,
  definition, value}]}], options: {owners, employers, jobs}, can_download}`. `date_from` and `date_to` are null on the vacancy board when no date is given.
- `GET /{board}/drill/{figure}?limit=1..100&offset` (`view`) → `{figure, total, limit, offset, columns: [{key, label, type}], items: [{id, href, ...}]}`, and
  `total` equals the figure. An unknown figure is 404. `href` is null when the caller's role cannot open the record.
- `GET /{board}.csv` and `.xlsx` (`reports.recruitment.download`): `Section, Figure, Count, Definition`; one `recruitment_dashboard.export` audit row,
  committed before the file is returned.

All are `no-store` and read-only. Migration `0295_recruitment_report_grants` seeds the grants.

## 12LJ. Skill demand, portal analytics and data quality (hrc-034) — addendum, 2026-11-06

`DEC-SCOPE-416`. All routes are `GET`, under `/api/v1/recruiter/recruitment-insights`, need `reports.recruitment_insights.view` (401 signed out, 403
otherwise) and only read. `limit` is 1-100, default 50; `offset` is at least 0.

| Route | Query | Returns |
|---|---|---|
| `/skill-demand` | `sort` = `demand` or `supply`; `limit`; `offset` | `figures`, `items` (`skill_id`, `name`, `category`, `required_jobs`, `preferred_jobs`, `open_jobs`, `supply`, `zero_supply`), `total`, `limit`, `offset`, `sort` |
| `/skill-demand/vacancies` | `n` = 1-100, default 5; `limit`; `offset` | `n`, `items` (`id`, `code`, `title`, `company`, `matches`, `reason`), `total`, `limit`, `offset`, `checked` (at most 200), `open_jobs` |
| `/portal-analytics` | `from`, `to`: default this IST month; at most 366 days; never future (422) | `from`, `to`, `registrations` (`total`, `verified`), `completion` (`label`, `count`), `funnel` (`key`, `label`, `events`, `people`, `dropped`), `first_recorded` |
| `/data-quality` | | `figures` (`key`, `label`, `definition`, `value`, `href`: null without the action page's grant) |
| `/data-quality/{figure}` | `limit`; `offset` | `figure`, `columns`, `items`, `total` (equals the figure), `limit`, `offset`; an unknown figure is 404 |

`GET /candidate/jobs` now writes `job_view` events for the cards it returns, and `POST /candidate/jobs/{id}/apply` and `POST /workflows/it/jobs/{id}/apply`
write `apply_start` and `apply_submit`. Their requests and responses are unchanged.

## 12LK. Company and contact import, and authorised merge (pob-006) — addendum, 2026-11-07

Additive. `GET /recruiter/companies/{id}` gains optional `merged_into` `{id, code, name}`; contact lists omit merged-away contacts. Nothing else changes.

| Route | Body | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /recruiter/company-imports/template` | | CSV header row |
| `POST /recruiter/company-imports/preview` | multipart `file` | 200 counts and `rows`; writes nothing |
| `POST /recruiter/company-imports` | multipart `file`, `Idempotency-Key` | 201 the report; `replayed: true` for a repeated key or a same file that imported cleanly. 422 bad file, bad key, key reused for another file. 409 another import still running |
| `GET /recruiter/company-imports` | `limit`, `offset` | own batches; a manager's team's too; super_admin all |
| `GET /recruiter/company-imports/{id}` | | the report, or 404 |
| `GET /recruiter/company-imports/{id}/report.csv` | | formula-escaped CSV, or 404 |
| `GET /recruiter/companies/{id}/merge?victim=CMP-x` | | both sides, `fields`, `moved`, `blocked` |
| `POST /recruiter/companies/{id}/merge` | `{victim_code, use_victim[], reason?}` | 200 `{id, code, victim_id, victim_code, moved}`. 404 out of scope; 422 self; 409 archived, merged, employer's account, both with a contract in effect |
| `GET /recruiter/contacts/{id}/merge?victim=<id>` | | both sides, `fields`, `moved` |
| `POST /recruiter/contacts/{id}/merge` | `{victim_id, use_victim[], reason?}` | 200 `{id, victim_id, company_id, moved}`. 404 out of scope or another company's; 422 self; 409 merged, company archived |

Import routes need `crm.import`, merge routes `crm.merge` (403). A row outcome is `created`, `matched` or `error` (`new` in a preview) with a `reason`.
## 12LB. Template library governance and copy mode (pob-023) — addendum, 2026-11-05

`DEC-SCOPE-408`, migration `0298`.

**Changed (additive):**
- `GET /api/v1/recruiter/templates`:
  - Accepts `channel=whatsapp|email|linkedin|call`, `category`, `status=draft|approved|archived`, `language` (case-insensitive) and
    `mine=true`.
  - `usable=true` returns what the caller may use in a composer: approved shared templates and their own non-archived personal ones.
  - `q` matches the name or the purpose.
  - The caller sees their own personal templates and the shared ones they may see: a recruiter sees approved ones and their own drafts;
    a manager or super_admin sees all of them.
  - Item fields add `category`, `purpose`, `language`, `department`, `industry`, `role_type`, `status`, `visibility` (`shared` or
    `personal`), `steps`, `version`, `created_by`, `updated_by`, `created_at`, `updated_at`, `last_used_at`, `can_edit`, `can_approve`,
    `can_archive` and `can_restore`.
  - `active` stays and means approved; the `active` filter still works.
- `POST /recruiter/templates`:
  - Open to `placement_team` as well. A recruiter's shared template is a `draft`; a manager's or super_admin's is `approved`.
  - New fields: `category` (omitted = derived from the channel and kind), the metadata, and `visibility` (default `shared`).
  - `steps`: a `call` template needs 1–20 steps of ≤ 500 characters; any other channel with steps is 422.
  - LinkedIn and call templates have no `kind` and no `subject`.
  - Body limits: LinkedIn 2000, call 5000.
  - Every `{…}` must be one of the 12 placeholders: `name`, `company`, `recruiter`, `designation`, `job_title`, `job_location`,
    `package_range`, `candidate`, `interview_date`, `interview_time`, `interview_mode`, `interview_link`.
- `PATCH /recruiter/templates/{id}`:
  - A template the caller cannot see is 404.
  - The channel and visibility are fixed (422).
  - A content change needs `can_edit`: an approved shared template is the manager's (403); an archived one is 409. It increments
    `version` and writes a version.
  - `active: true` approves and `active: false` archives a shared template (manager only; personal 422).
- `GET /recruiter/messages/render`:
  - Accepts `job_id` **or** `interview_id`; both is 422.
  - A requirement must be in the caller's scope and, for a contact, at the contact's company (404 "Job requirement not found").
  - An interview must be in scope and the candidate's own, or at the contact's company (404 "Interview not found").
  - `package_range` is empty without `commercials.client.view` and is then listed in `missing`.
  - The response adds `steps`.
- `POST /recruiter/messages`: accepts any usable template of the same channel, and sets the template's `last_used_at`.

**New:**
- `POST /recruiter/templates/{id}/approve | archive | restore` → the template. Approve: manager or super_admin, shared drafts only (409
  otherwise). Archive and restore: the manager for shared templates, the author for their own personal template or unapproved draft.
  Restore returns a template to `draft`.
- `GET /recruiter/templates/{id}/versions` → `{items: [{version, created_at, edited_by, name, kind, category, subject, body, steps,
  purpose, language, department, industry, role_type}], total}`, newest first.
- `POST /recruiter/messages/copies`:
  - Body `{channel: linkedin|call, template_id, contact_id | candidate_id}` → 201 `{template_id, channel, copied_at}`.
  - The send's party rules apply (scope 404, right 403, state 409, and pob-005's Do Not Contact 409). A template that is not usable or of
    another channel is 422.
  - It writes an audit row `recruiter_template.copy` (ids only) and is not a message: no last-contacted change, no cap.

Audit actions `recruiter_template.approve|archive|restore|copy`.


## 12LL. Requirement validation checklist (pob-016) — addendum, 2026-11-07

`DEC-SCOPE-419`; the rights are `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §2.311.

| Method and path | Body | Answer |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `GET /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/checklist` | | The checklist below |
| `PUT /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/checklist/items/{key}` | `{status: "confirmed" \| "not_applicable" \| "missing"}` | The checklist. The same answer again changes nothing. An unknown key or status is 422 |
| `POST /api/v1/recruiter/requirements/{id}/checklist/override` | `{reason}` (required, up to 500 characters) | The checklist. 409 when nothing is being blocked: the rule is off, the checklist is complete, sourcing has started, it is already overridden, or the requirement is cancelled |
| `PUT /api/v1/recruiter/companies/{id}/checklist-rule` | `{block_sourcing: true \| false \| null}` (must be stated; null = the global rule) | `{block_sourcing}` |
| `PUT /api/v1/recruiter/requirement-checklist/settings` | `{block_sourcing: true \| false}` | `{block_sourcing}` |

**The checklist:** `items: [{key, label, hint, status (null = not reviewed), follow_up: {id, status, due_at, contact} | null}]`, `summary: {total, confirmed, not_applicable, missing, not_reviewed, complete}`,
`blocking: {on, company_rule, global_rule}`, `override: {reason, by, at} | null`, `sourcing_started`, `warning` (text while sourcing has started and items are outstanding, else null), `company_id`, `can_edit`, `can_override`, `can_set_company_rule`, `can_set_global_rule`.

**Changed behaviour:** `POST /recruiter/requirements/{id}/status` and `/workflows/it/jobs` status moves into `sourcing`, `shortlisting`, `profiles_shared`, `interviewing` or `selected` can now answer
`409 {detail: {message, code: "checklist_incomplete", missing: [{key, label, status}]}}` when the rule applies, the checklist is incomplete, the requirement has never started sourcing and it has not been overridden. With the rules off (the default) nothing changes.
**Added vocabulary:** follow-up reason `clarification`.

## 12LM. Vendor master, onboarding and agreements (pob-010) — addendum, 2026-11-07

`DEC-SCOPE-420`. All routes keep the company scope (out of scope 404).

- `GET /recruiter/companies`: new `relationship=client|vendor|all` (default `client`) and `vendor_status`. Rows add `partner_type`, `vendor_status`, `vendor_status_label`.
- `POST`/`PATCH /recruiter/companies[/{id}]`: new `partner_type` (`recruiter|vendor|vendor_client`), `vendor_specialisations`, `vendor_service_categories`, `vendor_locations`. Vendor lists on a non-vendor 422; a change of relationship the records don't allow 409.
- `GET /recruiter/companies/{id}`: adds `vendor` (null for a non-vendor): lists, `onboarding_form_on_file`, `nda_on_file`, `agreement`, `next_statuses`, `history`.
- `POST /recruiter/companies/{id}/vendor-status` `{status, from_status, reason?}`: 200 the company. 403 a manager's step; 409 not a vendor, archived, stale (`vendor_status_changed`) or a missing form, NDA or signed agreement; 422 no such step or no reason.
- `GET|POST|PATCH /recruiter/companies/{id}/contracts` and `PUT .../contracts/document`: new `?party=client|vendor`; unsent = the client side, or the vendor side of a vendor-only company; a side the company lacks is 422. Bodies add `direction`, `confidentiality_terms` (vendor side only, else 422; `direction` required from Signed). Output adds `party`. Vendor terms and documents need `commercials.vendor.*`.
- `POST /recruiter/requirements`, `POST /recruiter/drives`, `POST /workflows/it/jobs`: a vendor-only company is 422. `GET /recruiter/pipeline` and `GET /public/home` partners leave it out. Merging a vendor is 409.
- Company documents: new kind `nda`.

## 12LN. Interview coordination automation (pob-022) — addendum, 2026-11-07

`DEC-SCOPE-421`.

- `POST /recruiter/interviews` and `POST /recruiter/interviews/{id}/reschedule`: new optional `template_id`, which must be an approved shared email template of kind `interview_confirmation` (otherwise 422 on `template_id`). It is ignored with `notify: false`. Queued emails keep `template_id`.
- `GET /recruiter/interview-automation/settings`: returns `first_reminder_hours`, `second_reminder_hours`, `reminder_template_id`, `chase_after_hours`, `escalate_after_hours` and `chase_template_id`. Only `placement_manager` and super_admin may read it; anyone else gets 403.
- `PUT /recruiter/interview-automation/settings`: a full replace with the same body. Reminder hours are 1–168 and the other hours 1–720, or null; the second reminder must be sooner than the first; templates must be approved shared email templates. A rule break returns 422 on its field. The response is the saved settings.
- Notices: `it.interview.reminder` (tagged, candidate) and `it.interview.feedback_escalated` (catalogued, Team Lead).

## 12LO. BD activity completeness (pob-007) — addendum, 2026-11-07

`DEC-SCOPE-422`. Migration `0302_bd_activity`.

- `POST /recruiter/messages/linkedin` (201): `{contact_id | candidate_id, action: connection_request|message, sent_at?, template_id?, body?, next_follow_up?}`. The send's party rules (scope 404, `can_edit` 403, inactive/archived 409, Do Not Contact 409); `body` required for `message` (422); `sent_at` not ahead and at most 7 days back (422 on `sent_at`); a usable LinkedIn template (422); 300 per sender per IST day (409); `next_follow_up` on a contact only. Moves Last contacted and the contact status. Audit `recruiter_message.create` (`channel: linkedin`).
- `POST /recruiter/messages/{id}/outcome` (200): `{outcome, next_follow_up?}` on a contact message, once (409 again; 422 on a candidate message). Audit `recruiter_message.outcome`.
- Message output adds `linkedin_action`, `outcome`, `follow_up {id, due_at, reason}`. Timeline message rows: `event` = `linkedin`, `status` = the action.
- `POST /recruiter/calls`, `PATCH /recruiter/calls/{id}`: new `job_id` (a requirement of the contact's company; contact calls only; 422 on `job_id`), `objections` (catalogue keys, each once), `objection_notes` (≤ 1000), `next_action` (≤ 500). Output adds them plus `requirement {id, code, title}`.
- `POST /recruiter/meetings/{id}/outcome`: new `hiring_forecast` (≤ 10 `{role, count, timeframe}`), `action_items` (≤ 10 `{action, due_at, reason}`, each a follow-up; 422 on `action_items.N.due_at`), `commercial_notes` (needs `commercials.client.view`, else 403). Meeting output adds `hiring_forecast`, `action_items`, `commercial_notes` (null without the grant) and `commercial_notes_visible`. Meeting type `company_visit` (BD visit).
- Follow-ups: create/PATCH add `priority` (high|medium|low, default medium) and `remind_at` (future when set; not after `due_at`, 422 on `remind_at`). Output adds both.

## 12LP. Follow-up reminders and outreach cadences (pob-008) — addendum, 2026-11-07

`DEC-SCOPE-423`. Migration `0304_outreach_cadences`.

- `GET /recruiter/cadences?include_inactive=false` (200): `{items: [{id, name, description, active, steps: [{day, channel, template {id, name} | null}], updated_at, can_edit}], can_edit}`. Recruiter-CRM roles (rec-003 `caller_scope`), else 403.
- `POST /recruiter/cadences` (201) / `PATCH /recruiter/cadences/{id}` (200): `{name (1-120), description? (≤ 500), steps: [{day, channel, template_id?}] (2-10), active? (PATCH)}`. `placement_manager` / super_admin, else 403. 422 on `steps` (day 0 first, strictly increasing, ≤ 90; template approved, shared, of the step's channel); duplicate name (case-insensitive) 409; unknown id 404. Audit `recruiter_cadence.create` / `.update`.
- `POST /recruiter/messages/{id}/cadence` (201): `{cadence_id}` on a contact message. Scope 404, `can_edit` 403, archived 409, candidate message 422, inactive/unknown cadence 422 on `cadence_id`, Do Not Contact / Not interested / inactive contact / already replied / already running 409. Returns the message with `cadence`. Audit `recruiter_cadence.start`.
- `POST /recruiter/messages/{id}/cadence/stop` (200): the active run on that message ends `manual`; none running 409. Audit `recruiter_cadence.stop`.
- Message output (company messages, start/stop) adds `cadence {id, cadence_id, name, status, step, steps, next_step_at, end_reason, ended_at, can_stop} | null`. Follow-up output adds `reminded_at`. Follow-up reasons add `first_outreach`, `cadence_step`.
- Notification event `it.recruiter.follow_up_due` (entity `recruiter_follow_up`, fixed text, action `/recruiter/follow-ups`); in `RECRUITMENT_EVENTS`.
- `POST /recruiter/companies` and `POST /recruiter/companies/{id}/contacts` also create one open `first_outreach` follow-up (due in 24 hours) in the same transaction.
