`DEC-SCOPE-424`. Migration `0305_po_targets_reports`.

- `GET /recruiter/targets?month=YYYY-MM` (PO), `GET /recruiter/manager/targets?month&limit&offset` (Team Lead: each PO's `kpis[{key, target, achieved, percent}]`, `response_rate`), `GET /recruiter/manager/targets/{user}?month` (one PO's sheet: `kpis[{key, label, definition, tracked, target, achieved, percent}]`, `contacts_reached`, `response_rate`, `territory`, `target_companies[]`, `editable`, `month_status`). Achieved is null for a future month.
- `PUT /recruiter/manager/targets` `{month, items[{recruiter_user_id, kpi_key, target|null}]}` → `{month, changed}`; past month 422 (super_admin may), > 12 ahead 422, unknown KPI 422, other team 404, inactive PO 422. Audit `recruiter_target.set`.
- `PUT /recruiter/manager/team/{user}/territory` `{territory|null}`. Audit `recruiter_territory.set`.
- `POST /recruiter/manager/target-companies` (201) `{recruiter_user_id, month, company_ids[] | territory}` → `{month, added}`; exactly one source (422); a company outside the Team Lead's scope 404. `DELETE /recruiter/manager/target-companies/{id}` (204).
- `GET /recruiter/daily-plan?date` → `{date, editable, items[], suggested_follow_ups[], suggested_companies[]}` (future 422). `POST /recruiter/daily-plan/items` (201) `{follow_up_id | company_id}` (duplicate 409, not a target 422, ≤ 50); `PATCH /recruiter/daily-plan/items/{id}` `{done}`; `DELETE` (204); an earlier day's item 422, another PO's 404.
- `GET /recruiter/activity-reports/{daily|weekly}/{start}` and `POST .../submit` (201) `{note?}` → `{period, period_start, period_end, status, counts, plan, note, submitted_at, kpis}`; a weekly start not a Monday 422, a future start 422, resubmission 409. Audit `recruiter_activity_report.submitted`.
- `GET /recruiter/activity-contacts?period&start&recruiter_id&limit&offset` → `{total, responses, response_rate, items[{contact, company, outreach, replied}]}`.
- `GET /recruiter/manager/activity-reports?period&start` (grid: `status` submitted|missing) and `GET /recruiter/manager/activity-reports/{user}/{period}/{start}`.
- Companies: create/PATCH/list/detail add `territory` (≤ 120); `GET /recruiter/companies?territory=` filters it exactly, case-insensitively.

## 12LR. Institution student pool and data permissions (pob-014) — addendum, 2026-11-07

`DEC-SCOPE-425`. Migration `0306_institution_student_pool`.

- `GET /recruiter/colleges/{org}/student-pool?permission_id&pass_out_year(2000-2100)&course&q&limit&offset` → `{college{id,name}, can_manage, permissions[{id, agreement_reference, mou_id, purposes, valid_from, expires_on, notes, status(active|scheduled|expired|revoked), users[{id, full_name}], student_count, created_at, revoked_at}], students{items[{id, candidate_code, name, passing_year, qualification, notice_status, permission_id, placement_status}], total, limit, offset}}`. pob-013 readers; out of scope 404. Students = those the caller may see now; permissions = all of the college's for a manager, else those naming the caller.
- `POST /recruiter/colleges/{org}/data-permissions` (201) `{agreement_reference, mou_id?, purposes[≥1], valid_from, expires_on ≥ valid_from, notes?, user_ids[1-50]}` → `{permission}`. `PATCH .../{permission}` (any subset; null refused) and `POST .../{permission}/revoke` → `{permission}`. Manager/super_admin only (403), archived college 409, revoked 409, foreign MoU 422, a person who cannot read candidates 422. Audit `institution_permission.create|update|revoke`.
- `POST /recruiter/candidate-imports[/preview]` add form fields `institution_permission_id`, `notice_status` (`notice_sent`|`consented`, required with a permission): 404 when the caller is not named on an active permission; reports and history carry `institution_permission {id, agreement_reference, organization_id, organization_name} | null`; a restricted match is reported without a candidate ("Already a candidate (restricted record)"). Same key with another permission 422.
- `GET /recruiter/candidates?institution_id=` filters by the college. Every candidate route treats an institution student outside the caller's active permission as 404; duplicate panels name them "Restricted record" (`id`, `candidate_code` null); profile shares 422 (not visible, or the permission lacks `placement`).

## 12LS. Vacancy sharing, campus calendar and outcomes (pob-015) — addendum, 2026-11-07

`DEC-SCOPE-426`. Migration `0307_institution_vacancy_shares`.

- `GET /recruiter/colleges/{org}/vacancies` → `{vacancies{shares[{id, requirement{id, code, title}, shared_on, channel, template{id, name}|null, notes, response, response_note, responded_at, responded_by_name, shared_by_name}] (latest 50, newest first), share_total, outcomes{applications, students, shortlisted, interviewed, offered, joined, external}, can_share}}`. pob-013 readers; out of scope 404.
- `GET /recruiter/colleges/{org}/vacancy-options?q` → `{items[{id, code, title, company_name}] (≤ 20), templates[{id, name}] (≤ 50)}`: the caller's shareable requirements and usable College/TPO templates. The college's writers only (403).
- `POST /recruiter/colleges/{org}/vacancy-shares` (201) `{requirement_id, channel, shared_on?, template_id?, notes? ≤ 500}` → the vacancies envelope. 403 not a writer, 404 requirement out of scope, 409 archived college or a same-day same-channel repeat, 422 not live / internal / future date / not a College/TPO template, 404 template not usable. Audit `bdm_organization.placement_vacancy_share`.
- `PATCH /recruiter/colleges/{org}/vacancy-shares/{share}` `{response, response_note?}` → the vacancies envelope; a share of another college 404; same values = no audit. Audit `bdm_organization.placement_vacancy_response`.

- `GET /recruiter/campus-summary` → `{colleges, shared_30d, awaiting, upcoming_30d, upcoming_truncated, outcomes{…}}` over the caller's colleges.

## 12LT. Vendor requirements and vendor submissions (pob-011) — addendum, 2026-11-08

`DEC-SCOPE-427`. Migration `0308_vendor_requirements`.

- `POST /recruiter/requirements` and `PATCH /recruiter/requirements/{id}` take `via_vendor_id` (uuid|null) and `end_client_disclosure` (`external`|`internal`; null 422). A vendor that is not a vendor-type company in the caller's company scope, is suspended or archived, or is the requirement's own company is 422. The detail gains `via_vendor{id, code, name}|null` and `end_client_disclosure`; list rows gain `end_client_disclosure`. Revisions and the audit diff show `via_vendor` (by name) and `end_client_disclosure`.
- `POST /recruiter/shares` takes `recipient` (`client` default | `vendor`), `detail` (`anonymised` default | `full`; vendor only), `consent_confirmed` and `consent_reference` (≤ 300; vendor only). Detail or consent on a client share is 422. A vendor share without a vendor on the requirement, or on the Portal channel, is 422. A contact of another company is 422. A vendor that is not active, has no current signed or active agreement, or has no NDA is 409. Full details without the consent stub are 409. The repeat 409 (`{message, duplicates}`) also lists candidates shared for another requirement of the same end client when either share went through a vendor.
- A share (list and create) gains `recipient`, `detail` (`named`|`anonymised`|`full`), `detail_label` and `consent_reference`; `company_id` is the recipient company.
- Public `GET /public/jobs`, the student `GET /workflows/it/jobs/open`, the candidate job board, and the student's applications and interviews show `company: "Confidential client"` for an `internal` requirement.
## 12LU. Source attribution, verification and outreach permission (pob-018) — addendum, 2026-11-08

`DEC-SCOPE-428`. Migration `0309_candidate_sourcing`.

- `POST`/`PATCH /recruiter/candidates[/{id}]` accept `source_date` (not in the future; omitted on create = today IST; null on PATCH 422), `referrer_name` (≤ 160), `referrer_candidate_id` (another pool candidate the caller can see, else 422), `vendor_id` (a live vendor company in the caller's company scope, else 422), `portal_import_reference` (≤ 120). Audited by the existing `candidate.create|update` diff.
- `GET /recruiter/candidates` rows add `source_date`. The candidate detail adds `sourcing {source_date, referrer_name, referrer{id, candidate_code, name}|null ("Restricted record" with null id/code when the viewer cannot see it), vendor{id, name}|null, institution{id, name}|null, portal_import_reference, last_verified_on, last_verified_by, outreach_status, outreach_status_changed_at, permission{method, given_on, note, recorded_by}|null, permitted}`.
- `POST /recruiter/candidates/{id}/verify` → the candidate detail; sets `last_verified_on` (today IST) and who. Writers only (403), outside the pool 404, archived 409. Audit `candidate.verify`.
- `PUT /recruiter/candidates/{id}/outreach` `{status: not_contacted|contacted|responded|consented|declined, method?, given_on?, note? ≤ 500}` → the candidate detail. `consented` requires `method` (call|email|whatsapp|linkedin|in_person|form|other) and `given_on` (not in the future); the permission fields with any other status 422; leaving `consented` withdraws the permission. Same values = no-op. Writers only (403), 404, archived 409. Audit `candidate.outreach` `{from, to, method?, given_on?}` (never the note).
- `POST /recruiter/shares`: 422 "Record each candidate's permission to be represented … : <names>" when a candidate has no outreach permission (consented, their own opt-in, or an institution student under pob-014).

## 12LV. Per-requirement sourcing plan and source funnel (pob-017) — addendum, 2026-11-08

`DEC-SCOPE-429`. Migration `0310_sourcing_plans`.

- `GET /recruiter/requirements/{id}/sourcing` → `{plan {target_profile, boolean_strings[{label, query}], target_found, target_screened, target_submitted, deadline, source_owner, updated_by, updated_at}, channels[{id, name, active}], searches[hrc-020 saved-search rows the caller can see], hidden_searches, funnel {rows[{source{id, name, active}, planned, found, screened, suitable, submitted, rejected, conversion}], total{…}}, progress {state: no_targets|met|on_track|breached, short[], deadline, days_left, found|screened|submitted {actual, target}}, can_edit, can_link_searches}`. Every requirement reader (officers included); out of scope 404.
- `PUT /recruiter/requirements/{id}/sourcing` `{target_profile?, boolean_strings[] ≤ 10, channel_ids[] ≤ 20, saved_search_ids[] ≤ 10, target_found|target_screened|target_submitted (0-10 000 | null), deadline?, source_owner_user_id?}` (unknown keys 422) → the same body. The whole plan is replaced; links the caller cannot see are kept. 422: owner not on the requirement, an inactive/unknown source, a search the caller cannot see; 403: not a requirement writer, or linking a search without candidate-reader rights; 409: cancelled. Audit `requirement_sourcing_plan.update {fields}`; the same plan again is a no-op.

## 12LW. Submission record (pob-021) — addendum, 2026-11-08

`DEC-SCOPE-430`. Migration `0313_submissions`. Additive on rec-019's routes.

- `POST /recruiter/shares` gains `detail` (client: `named`|`anonymised`; vendor: `anonymised`|`full`), `consent_confirmed` + `consent_reference` (≤ 300) and `interest_confirmed` (all required: 409 otherwise), and `include_compensation` (403 without `candidates.compensation`). 422 for a candidate without a location or failing a switched-on hard filter. The repeat 409 covers the same end client on any requirement or route. The response's share gains `compensation_included` and `follow_up {id, due_at, status}`; each item gains `submission_code, status, status_label, status_changed_at, rejection_reason, rejection_reason_label, next_action, next_action_on, acknowledged_at, consent_reference, match {score, required_matched, required_missing, preferred_matched, preferred_missing, explanation[]}`.
- `PATCH /recruiter/shares/{id}/items/{item_id}` gains `status`, `rejection_reason` (required with, and only with, `rejected`: 422), `next_action` (≤ 300), `next_action_on`, `acknowledged: true`. A closed submission is 409. A response maps onto the status unless `status` is sent. Audit `submission.status` {from, to, reason} per change.
- `GET /employer/shared-profiles` items gain `submission_code, match, status, status_label`; an anonymised submission's `candidate.name` and `current_company` are null and it has no resume; `candidate.expected_salary` only when sent. `PATCH /employer/shared-profiles/{id}` maps the response onto the status.
- `POST|PATCH /recruiter/companies` accept `submission_sla_hours` (1-720 | null); the company read returns it.

## 12LY. Placement closure record and invoice draft (pob-024) — addendum, 2026-11-08

`DEC-SCOPE-432`. Migration `0312_placements`.

- `GET /recruiter/placements?view=pending|joined|did_not_join|invoice_drafts&limit&offset` → `{items[{id, status, status_label, role, candidate, requirement, company, joined_on, invoice_status, invoice_status_label}], total, limit, offset, counts{pending, joined, did_not_join, invoice_drafts}}`; the invoice fields are null without the commercial gate. Scoped like `/recruiter/joinings`.
- `GET /recruiter/placements/{id}` → `{placement {id, status, status_label, role, offer_id, application, candidate, requirement, company, vendor, recruiter, po_owner, source, package|null, joining {expected_joining_date, actual_joining_date}, candidate_confirmation|null, client_confirmation|null ({by, on, via, via_label}), commercials|null {expected_fee, invoice {status: none|draft|issued|discarded, status_label, draft_amount, draft_due_date, draft_created_at, discard_reason, invoice (rec-031 invoice)|null}}, permissions {can_edit, can_change_owner, can_invoice}, created_at}}`. Out of scope 404; other roles 403.
- `PATCH /recruiter/placements/{id}` `{candidate_confirmation?, client_confirmation? ({by, on, via} | null), po_owner_user_id?}` → `{placement}`. Only the keys sent change. 403: a confirmation from a non-writer, the owner from a non-Team-Lead; 409: Did Not Join; 422: a future date, an unknown `via`, a missing `by`, an owner who is not an active placement_team user. Audit `placement.updated {fields}`.
- `POST /recruiter/placements/{id}/invoice-draft/issue` `{invoice_number, invoice_date, due_date, amount, note?}` → 201 `{placement}`: a rec-031 invoice on the requirement (its 422s and the `invoice_number_taken` 409). 409 `no_invoice_draft` when the placement has no open draft (a retry). 403 without the revenue gate.
- `POST /recruiter/placements/{id}/invoice-draft/cancel` `{reason (2-500)}` → `{placement}`; 409 `no_invoice_draft`.
- `GET /recruiter/applications/{id}/offer` and the offer routes: the offer gains `placement_id` (null until Accepted).
- Notices: `it.recruitment.invoice_due`, `it.recruitment.invoice_overdue` (entity `placement`, fixed text, `action_url` `/recruiter/placements/{id}`); beat `pob024-invoice-notices` daily 03:15 UTC.

## 12LZ. Vendor commercials, payables and performance (pob-012) — addendum, 2026-11-08

`DEC-SCOPE-433`. Migration `0314_vendor_bills`. Reads need `commercials.vendor.view`, writes `commercials.vendor.edit` (403 first); the vendor resolves through the company scope (404).

- `GET /recruiter/companies/{id}/vendor-bills` → `{items[bill], totals{payable|receivable: {billed, paid, outstanding, last_paid_on}}, can_edit}`; 422 when the company is not a vendor. `bill` = `{id, kind, kind_label, bill_number, bill_date, due_date, amount, milestone, note, paid, outstanding, payment_status, payment_status_label, overdue, cancelled_at, cancel_reason, requirement|null, created_by, payments[{id, paid_on, amount, reference, created_by, created_at}], permissions{can_pay, can_cancel}, created_at}`. Totals count open bills only.
- `POST /recruiter/companies/{id}/vendor-bills` `{kind: payable|receivable, bill_number (≤40), bill_date (not future), due_date (≥ bill date), amount (>0), milestone? (≤120), requirement_id? (one via this vendor), note? (≤500)}` → 201 `{bill}`. 409 `bill_number_taken`; 409 an archived/merged vendor.
- `POST /recruiter/vendor-bills/{id}/payments` `{paid_on (not future, not before the bill date), amount (≤ outstanding), reference? (≤100)}` → 201 `{bill}`. 409 `bill_cancelled`.
- `POST /recruiter/vendor-bills/{id}/cancel` `{reason}` → `{bill}`. 409 `bill_cancelled`, `bill_has_payments`.
- `GET /recruiter/vendors/performance?limit (1-100)&offset` → `{items[{vendor{id, code, name}, vendor_status_label, metrics}], total, limit, offset}`; `metrics` = `{requirements, responded_requirements, avg_response_hours|null, submitted, interviewed, placed, joined, interview_rate|null, joining_rate|null, payments{payable|receivable}}`.
- `GET /recruiter/vendors/{id}/performance` → the row plus `requirements[{requirement, created_at, first_submission_at|null, response_hours|null}]` and `submissions[{application_id, candidate, requirement, submitted_at, interviewed, placed, joined}]` — the rows every figure is counted from.
- Management ledger `GET /management/ledger/lines`: new automatic sources `vendor_payment` (expense) and `vendor_receipt` (income), vertical `recruitment`.
